# 10 — APIs and models (lib/apis/**, lib/models/**)

> Cross-spec decisions in [00-index.md](00-index.md) (section "Decisions") override any conflicting PC suggestion in this file.

Leaf of the ONSET desktop memoir. Covers every HTTP/GQL/WebSocket-payload service in `lib/apis/` and every data model in `lib/models/` (the generated `*.g.dart` files are described inside their owning model's section: they are the exact JSON mapping and must never be hand-edited in the Flutter tree). Every value below was read from the source; GQL documents are copied byte-exact (only `\$` Dart escapes removed). No secret exists in this area: the client ids quoted are Twitch's public ones (`kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko` web, `ue6666qo983tsx6so1t0vnawi233wa` Android TV) and the build-time `CLIENT_ID` / `TOKEN_PROXY_URL` / `UPDATE_TOKEN` / `UPDATE_REPO` defines (never in the repo).

## Purpose

The API layer is how ONSET talks to the outside world without the Twitch web player:
- **Helix** (`https://api.twitch.tv/helix`, `TwitchApi` + `TwitchModApi`) with the logged-in user's OAuth token (`Authorization: Bearer <token>`, `Client-Id: <CLIENT_ID>`) or, for logged-out viewers, an **app token** minted by ONSET's token proxy (`GET <TOKEN_PROXY_URL>/app-token`, a Cloudflare Worker holding a second app's secret; the token goes with that app's client id). OAuth validate/revoke on `https://id.twitch.tv/oauth2`.
- **Twitch private GQL** (`https://gql.twitch.tv/gql`) with the public web Client-ID, anonymously or with the viewer's **twitch.tv web token** (`Authorization: OAuth <auth-token cookie>`): playback tokens (live and VOD), VODs, clips, chat history, chat rules, directory sorts, channel profile, hype trains, community highlights, channel points, predictions, drops, goals, whispers history, notifications, recommendations, account editing, reports, follows… Mutations may be rerouted through a **Twitch TV device session** (`TwitchTvSessionInterceptor`) to pass Twitch's integrity check.
- **usher** (`https://usher.ttvnw.net`) master playlists with low-latency flags (`fast_bread=true`) — the core of the fork's mission (native ~2–4 s latency player; every throw means "fall back to the WebView engine").
- **Third parties**: BTTV (`api.betterttv.net/3/cached`), FFZ (`api.frankerfacez.com/v1`), 7TV (`7tv.io/v3` REST + `7tv.io/v4/gql`), recent-messages (`recent-messages.robotty.de/api/v2`), GIPHY (`api.giphy.com/v1/gifs`), GitHub releases (`api.github.com`) for in-app updates.
- **Models**: typed, tolerant parsers for all of the above plus realtime payloads (Hermes topics, 7TV EventAPI, BTTV socket, IRC lines and USERNOTICE tags, native IVS stats). Every parser is defensive: wrong types become defaults/null, never crashes; URLs are restricted to known hosts and https; ids to strict patterns before they reach a URL or query.
Design principle preserved for the PC port: degrade gracefully. A failing optional call returns null/empty and the feature hides; only primary content (stream list, profile, playback token) surfaces typed errors that screens word in the UI language.

## Screens and layouts

Which screens consume which APIs (store/widget files in parentheses):
- **Home › Following / Top / Browse** (`home.dart`, `stream_list_store.dart`, `browse_page.dart`, `categories_store.dart`, `category_streams.dart`): Helix `getFollowedStreams`, `getTopStreams`, `getStreamsUnderCategory`, `getTopCategories`, `getCategory`, `getStreamsByIds`; `TwitchDirectoryService.getCategoryStreams/getTagStreams` for non-Helix sorts and tag pages (`home/top/tags`); `TwitchHypeTrainService.getActiveStatuses` for card pills; `TwitchWatchStreakService.getStreaks` for streak flames; `TwitchStreamService.preload` on card press (via `PlaybackPreloader`); live feed (`live_feed_store.dart`, `live_feed.dart`) uses `TwitchStreamService.getStreamVariants` for its inline previews.
- **Home › Search** (`search.dart`, `search_store.dart`): Helix `searchChannels`, `searchCategories`; `TwitchDirectoryService.searchSuggestions`; `TwitchVodService.searchVideos` (Videos tab).
- **Followed videos / Continue watching** (`followed_videos_store.dart`, `continue_watching/stores`): `TwitchVodService.getFollowedVideos`, `getResumeWatching`, `getVideos`; Helix `getFollowedChannels`.
- **Followed categories row**: `TwitchFollowedCategoriesService`; follow/unfollow category through `TwitchViewerActionsService`.
- **Channel screen — video** (`video_store.dart`, `native_video_store.dart`, `ivs_video_store.dart`, `content_warning_gate.dart`, `create_clip_sheet.dart`, `stream_end.dart`): `TwitchStreamService` (usher URL/variants, refusals, quality ladder, restriction tier, free preview), Helix `getStream`, `getChannel` (content labels), `createClip`/`getCreatedClip`/`getTokenScopes`, `TwitchViewerActionsService.clipRefusal`, `TwitchVodService.streamHasPaidPromotion`, `TwitchChannelProfileService.isRestricted/isParticipatingDj`; `IvsStats` feeds the latency/stats overlay.
- **Channel screen — chat** (`chat_store.dart`, `chat_assets_store.dart`, `chat_message.dart`, `chat_user_modal.dart`, `user_card_info.dart`, `chat_details*`, `moderation.dart`, `mod_tools/*`, `emote_menu/*`, `community_highlights/*`, `hype_train/stores`): Helix emotes/badges/cheermotes/user emotes/chat color/send message/ban/unban/delete/warn/chat settings/followed-at/shared chat session/users by id; `TwitchModApi` (mod tools sheet); BTTV/FFZ/7TV emotes, badges, cosmetics; `getRecentMessages` (robotty) and `TwitchChatHistoryService` (GQL history); `TwitchChatRulesService`; `TwitchCommunityHighlightsService` (poll/prediction/pin snapshots) + Hermes models; `TwitchHypeTrainService`; `TwitchPointsService` (points chip/sheet, predictions); `TwitchCommunityService` (shoutout cards, leaderboards, resub share, drops, goals, weekly rewards, unlockable emotes, legendary chests, live-together strip, emote bits threshold, chat identity, unban request); `TwitchViewerActionsService` (votes, raids, shares, pins, lead mod, badges, gifts settings, GIFs, chat UI sync, unban request, goals, drops, chests, moments); `GiphyService` (GIF picker); `TwitchReportService` (report sheet, `widgets/report`).
- **Channel profile** (`channel_profile_screen.dart`, `channel_profile/stores`, `team_screen.dart`): `TwitchChannelProfileService` (profile, schedule, trailer), Helix `getScheduleExtras`, `getChannelTeams`, `getTeam`, `getUser`; `TwitchVodService.getChannelVideos/getChannelCollections/getCollectionVideos`; `TwitchClipService.getChannelClips/getChannelRecaps`; `TwitchSocialService.recommendations` (similar channels).
- **VOD screen** (`vod_screen.dart`, `vod/stores`): `TwitchVodService` (video, usher URL, token preload/invalidate, diagnose, comments replay, chapters, muted segments, content labels, paid promotion, viewing history, `updateViewedVideo`).
- **Clips** (`clips/*`, `my_clips_screen.dart`): `TwitchClipService` (feed, channel/category/curated clips, playback, download, extras, comments, recaps), `TwitchAccountGqlService` (comment eligibility/post/like, rename/delete own clips).
- **Whispers** (`whispers/*`): Helix `sendWhisper`, EventSub create/delete; `TwitchSocialService` threads/messages/privacy/allowance.
- **Notifications / Activity** (`notifications/*`): `TwitchSocialService` notifications, unseen count, mark read, settings.
- **Settings › Account** (`settings/account/*`, `auth_store.dart`, `user_store.dart`): `TwitchAccountGqlService`; Helix `getUserInfo`, `checkToken`, `validateToken`, `tokenUserId`, `revokeToken`, `getDefaultToken`, `getUserBlockedList`, block/unblock, chat colour; `TwitchTvAuthService` (TV session offer); `TwitchViewerActionsService.chatUiSettings/giftSettings`; `TwitchSocialService.whisperPrivacy`.
- **Settings › About / updates**: `OnsetUpdateService` + `OnsetRelease`.
- **Onboarding** (`onboarding_flow.dart`): Helix `getTopStreams(quiet: true)` mosaic.
- **Global**: `UnauthorizedInterceptor` dialog over any screen; `widgets/follow/follow_actions.dart` (follow buttons via viewer actions), `widgets/recommendations` (recommended shelf), `widgets/share_sheet.dart`, `widgets/profile_picture.dart` (Helix users).
This area itself draws no layout except the chat-line spans and the emote/badge detail sheet inside `irc.dart` (described in its file section) and the login dialog of `unauthorized_interceptor.dart`.

## Behaviour and state

- **Clients and interceptor chains**: (1) shared Helix Dio (8 s connect / 15 s receive / 10 s send, JSON default headers, `User-Agent: ONSET (Flutter Twitch Client)`) → debug logger → Firebase perf → `TwitchAuthInterceptor` (adds session Bearer + Client-Id to helix/oauth2 URLs unless the request names its own or sets `noSessionHeaders`) → `UnauthorizedInterceptor` (401 → login dialog unless `/validate` or `quiet`; logged-out viewers instead trigger an app-token retry at most once a minute). (2) anonymous GQL Dio (5 s / 8 s) with only `TwitchTvSessionInterceptor`. (3) `PlaybackPreloader.http` (5 s / 8 s, 60 s idle keep-alive) for live playback. (4) points Dio (5 s / 10 s) with the TV interceptor. (5) update Dio (5 s / 15 s, 30 s stream idle timeout). The Rust port keeps these separations (tokens must never cross hosts: Helix token only to Helix/oauth2; web token only to gql.twitch.tv; GitHub token only to api.github.com).
- **Error model**: `BaseApiClient` maps every transport/HTTP failure to `ApiException` + `ApiErrorKind` (table in base_api_client section); screens word them through `lib/l10n/api_errors.dart` (`apiErrorText`: English UI shows Twitch's own `message`/`error_description` when present, other languages show the localized kind; `SocketException` → `apiErrorUnableToConnect`; chat lines use `chatErrorText` = localized kind as a sentence + `chatTwitchReason(reason)` quote when Twitch's reason is ≤ 120 chars and single-line). Telemetry records only server errors, unknown and certificate failures, with reason `METHOD origin` (never paths/queries).
- **Viewer action results** are typed enums, never exceptions (`ViewerActionOutcome`, `FollowResult`, `AccountEditResult`, `GoalContributionResult`, `DropClaimResult`, `MomentClaimResult`, `GifSendResult`, `UnbanRequestOutcome`, points error codes → `PointsActionResult`). Integrity refusals (`failed integrity check`) are distinguished everywhere; follows map them to `needsTvSession` (offer the TV device login) or `integrityRefused` (already used).
- **Caching/state held in services**: `PlaybackTokenCache` (LRU 32, valid until `expires − 60 s`, in-flight sharing) for live (shared) and VOD (per service); live keys `login` / `login#hash(webToken)`, VOD keys `id` / `id#sha256(webToken)[0..16]`; refused tokens never cached. `TwitchStreamService` remembers per-channel refusal and free-preview end. `TwitchVodService` remembers the last refused web token id and counts watch-history failures per kind/token (3, or 1 for integrity, stops that request for the session). `FFZApi.badgeDefinitions` is filled by `getBadges`. Everything else is stateless; app-level caching (global emotes, per-channel assets) is in stores.
- **Pagination**: Helix cursors (`pagination.cursor`; user emotes ≤10 pages; mod lists ≤10 pages ×100; redemptions ≤2 pages; blocked users recursive with 150 ms spacing; users/channels in batches of 100). GQL: anonymous channel/category lists are single pages (≤100) because cursor paging is integrity-gated; search VODs (25/page), top clips (20/page), clip comments (20/page) and notifications (30/page, cursor only when a full page) do page.
- **Retries**: none automatic except: playback token query retried once without the refusal field when GQL rejects it; authenticated playback/VOD token falls back immediately to anonymous; channel clips retried without `isFeatured`; app token re-fetch on 401 (AuthStore, ≤1/min); web token renewal fired in the background on a 401 from the web token.
- **Timeouts**: as per client above; token proxy has its own overall 6 s deadline (`tokenProxyTimeout`); chat history 4 s; update downloads 30 s idle.
- **Validation before I/O**: Helix ids `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, opaque ids per service, logins `^[a-z0-9_]{1,25}$` / `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$`, clip slugs `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$`, VOD ids with optional `v`, text limits (chat 500, whisper 10 000, reasons 500, announcement 500, poll/prediction limits) — invalid input throws `ArgumentError` (programming error) or returns a failed/signed-out result without a request.

## Animations and motion

None in this area: lib/apis and lib/models perform network I/O and data parsing only, so they define no animation, transition or motion of their own. The only motion-related data are descriptive: `CelebrationEffect`/`CelebrationPreset`/`CelebrationIntensity.scale/opacity`/duration clamping (lib/models/celebration.dart) which parametrize the particle celebrations drawn by the channel screen, `FfzModifier` flags (rotate, slide, bounce, jam…) consumed by the FFZ modified-emote widget, the `gigantifiedEmoteFactor = 3.0` emote size, and `animateEmotes`/`animateGifs` switches selecting animated vs static URLs (`Emote.urlFor`). Their rendering and timing are specified in the chat and channel leaves.

## Settings

Settings (SettingsStore, specified in the settings leaf) that change behaviour in this area:
- `timestampType` (`TimestampType { disabled, twelve, twentyFour }`, default disabled) → chat-line timestamps in `IRCMessage.generateSpan` (`'h:mm'` vs `Hm`).
- `animateEmotes` (and GIF animation / show GIFs) → `Emote.urlFor(animate:)`, cheer image kind, `ChatGifImage`.
- `nameColorContrast` (readable chat colours / increased contrast) → `nameContrast` passed to `generateSpan` (4.5:1 or 7:1, or null when off) — name colour adjustment and paint fallback.
- `launchExternal` (open links in external browser) → link taps and asset sheet "Open in browser".
- Badge/emote scale → `badgeScale`, `emoteScale` multipliers of `defaultBadgeSize = 18` and `defaultEmoteSize = 28`.
- Chat filters (`ChatFilterSettings`, AutoMod flag filtering) → `_filteredWords`/`shownText`.
- `hideGiftCount` (default false) → `showGiftCount = !hideGiftCount` in `userNoticeText` / `IRCMessage.noticeText`.
- Per-event chat bubble filters → `chatBubbleEventOf`.
- Player engine (native low-latency vs WebView) decides whether `TwitchStreamService` is used at all; enhanced broadcasting (2K) uses the twitch.tv web token stored by AuthStore.
- Browse language filter → `language` params of `getTopStreams`, `getStreamsUnderCategory`, `getCategoryStreams`, `getTagStreams`, `getTopClips`.
- Build-time defines: `CLIENT_ID`, `TOKEN_PROXY_URL` (must match `TwitchApi.tokenProxyUrlPattern`, also enforced by CI), `UPDATE_REPO`, `UPDATE_TOKEN`, optional `FIREBASE_*` (perf/crash telemetry).
- Twitch-account synced chat settings (`ChatUiSetting`) and gift settings are read/written on Twitch, not stored locally.

## Strings

User-facing text in this area comes only from the ARB files (`lib/l10n/app_en.arb`, `app_fr.arb`) via `l10n`/`L10n.current`; English exception messages in `ApiException.message` are for logs only (except on an English chat line, see `chatErrorText`). Error kinds are worded by `lib/l10n/api_errors.dart` with these keys:

| Key | EN | FR |
|---|---|---|
| `apiErrorUnableToConnect` | Couldn’t connect to Twitch. | Impossible de joindre Twitch |
| `apiErrorNetwork` | No internet connection. Check your network. | Pas de connexion Internet. Vérifiez votre réseau. |
| `apiErrorConnectionTimeout` | The connection timed out. Try again. | Impossible de joindre Twitch à temps. Réessayez. |
| `apiErrorSendTimeout` | Sending took too long. Try again. | L’envoi a pris trop de temps. Réessayez. |
| `apiErrorReceiveTimeout` | Twitch took too long to respond. Try again. | Le serveur a mis trop de temps à répondre. Réessayez. |
| `apiErrorCancelled` | The request was canceled. | La requête a été annulée |
| `apiErrorCertificate` | Couldn’t verify Twitch’s security certificate. | Erreur de certificat de sécurité |
| `apiErrorUnexpected` | An unexpected error occurred. | Une erreur inattendue est survenue |
| `apiErrorBadRequest` | The request wasn’t valid. | Requête non valide |
| `apiErrorUnauthorized` | Log in to continue. | Connectez-vous pour continuer |
| `apiErrorForbidden` | Access denied: missing permissions. | Accès refusé. Autorisations insuffisantes |
| `apiErrorNotFound` | This content couldn’t be found. | Contenu introuvable |
| `apiErrorConflict` | This changed in the meantime. Refresh and try again. | Le contenu a changé entre-temps. Actualisez |
| `apiErrorInvalid` | The data sent wasn’t valid. | Données non valides |
| `apiErrorRateLimited` | Too many requests. Wait a moment, then try again. | Trop de requêtes. Patientez avant de réessayer |
| `apiErrorServer` | Twitch had a server error. Try again later. | Erreur du serveur. Réessayez plus tard |
| `apiErrorUnavailable` | Twitch is temporarily unavailable. | Service temporairement indisponible |
| `apiErrorMaintenance` | Twitch is under maintenance. Try again later. | Service en maintenance. Réessayez plus tard |
| `apiErrorServerTimeout` | Twitch’s server didn’t respond. Try again. | Le serveur ne répond pas. Réessayez |
| `apiErrorHttp` | HTTP error {status}. | Erreur HTTP {status} |
| `apiErrorUserNotFound` | This user doesn’t exist. | Cet utilisateur n’existe pas |
| `apiErrorChannelNotFound` | This channel doesn’t exist. | Cette chaîne n’existe pas |
| `apiErrorCategoryNotFound` | Category not found. | Catégorie introuvable |
| `apiErrorTokenProxyTimeout` | The ONSET token service didn’t respond in time. | Le serveur d’ONSET n’a pas répondu à temps |
| `chatTwitchReason` | Twitch’s reason: “{reason}” | Raison de Twitch : « {reason} » |

| `noticeWatchStreak` | {count, plural, one{{name} reached a {count}-stream Watch Streak!} other{{name} reached a {count}-stream Watch Streak!}} | {count, plural, one{Série de visionnage de {count} live pour {name} !} other{Série de visionnage de {count} lives pour {name} !}} |
| `noticeWatchStreakPoints` | {points, plural, one{They earned {points} channel point.} other{They earned {points} channel points.}} | {points, plural, one{{points} point de chaîne gagné.} other{{points} points de chaîne gagnés.}} |
| `pointsBusy` | Already in progress. | Déjà en cours. |
| `pointsNoReason` | no reason given | aucune raison donnée |
| `pointsResult` | {status, select, success{Done.} integrityRefused{Twitch’s anti-bot check refused this. Connect a Twitch session in Settings › Account, then try again.} notAuthenticated{Twitch rejected the login. Log in again to spend points.} network{Couldn’t reach Twitch. Try again.} other{Twitch refused: {reason}}} | {status, select, success{C’est fait.} integrityRefused{Le contrôle anti-robot de Twitch a refusé cette action. Connectez une session Twitch dans Paramètres › Compte, puis réessayez.} notAuthenticated{Twitch a refusé la connexion. Reconnectez-vous pour dépenser des points.} network{Impossible de joindre Twitch. Réessayez.} other{Twitch a refusé : {reason}}} |

Notes: `pointsResult` and `pointsAutomaticReward`, `noticeSubscribed` etc. are ICU `select`/`plural` messages; the select keys used by the code are listed in the owning model sections (e.g. `status` = `success|integrityRefused|notAuthenticated|network|unknown`; `type` = `highlight|randomEmote|chosenEmote|modifiedEmote|subOnlyMessage`; `plan` = `prime|tier|none`; `tiered` = `yes|no`; `end` = `more|last`; `how` = `global|owned|follow|bits|subscribe`). Names inside notice sentences are inserted as marker characters (`l10nMark(i)`) and rendered bold. Enum labels (`EmoteType.label`, `BadgeType.label`, clip periods, stream sorts) come from `lib/l10n/enum_labels.dart` (settings/l10n leaf); the English `toString()` of `EmoteType`/`BadgeType` is for logs.

## Network and data

Summary table (details and exact documents in the per-file sections):

| Service | Host / base | Auth | Client |
|---|---|---|---|
| TwitchApi / TwitchModApi | api.twitch.tv/helix, id.twitch.tv/oauth2, recent-messages.robotty.de/api/v2, TOKEN_PROXY_URL/app-token | Bearer session or app token + Client-Id (interceptor) | shared Helix Dio |
| TwitchStreamService | gql.twitch.tv/gql, usher.ttvnw.net/api/channel/hls | web Client-ID (+ OAuth web token) | PlaybackPreloader.http |
| TwitchVodService | gql, usher.ttvnw.net/vod | web Client-ID (+ OAuth web token for token/history) | anonymous GQL |
| TwitchClipService | gql, clip CDN (cloudfront d1ndex63qxojbr, *.twitch.tv, *.twitchcdn.net, *.ttvnw.net, *.jtvnw.net) | web Client-ID (+ token for comments) | anonymous GQL |
| Viewer actions / Account / Social / Report / Followed categories / Watch streaks / Chat history | gql | web Client-ID + OAuth web token | anonymous GQL (+TV reroute for mutations) |
| Community / Highlights / Hype train / Directory / Channel profile / Chat rules | gql | web Client-ID (optional token) | anonymous GQL |
| TwitchPointsService | gql | web Client-ID (+ token) | own Dio + TV interceptor |
| TwitchTvAuthService | id.twitch.tv/oauth2 device/token/validate/revoke | TV client id, form-encoded | anonymous GQL Dio |
| BTTVApi / FFZApi / SevenTVApi | api.betterttv.net/3/cached, api.frankerfacez.com/v1, 7tv.io/v3 + 7tv.io/v4/gql | none | shared Helix Dio (no auth headers added) |
| GiphyService | api.giphy.com/v1/gifs | api_key from Twitch's GIF config | anonymous GQL Dio |
| OnsetUpdateService | api.github.com + allowlisted download hosts | optional Bearer UPDATE_TOKEN (never on redirects) | own Dio |

GQL request shape everywhere: `POST https://gql.twitch.tv/gql` JSON `{"operationName": <name>, "query": <full document>, "variables": {...}}` (exceptions: points context query has no operationName; 7TV cosmetics body is only `{"query": …}`; `BatchGetWatchStreaksQuery` has no `variables`). Error handling of GQL bodies is per service (strict: any error throws; partial: data kept unless the error hits the requested field or is an integrity refusal; tolerant: null). Realtime payload models (Hermes, 7TV EventAPI, BTTV socket, IRC) are parsed here but the sockets live in lib/services and the chat layer. Persisted data from this area: `Emote.toJson` (recent emotes), `TwitchTvSession.encode()` (secure storage), downloaded clip files `onset-clip-<slug>.mp4`, update files `<cache>/updates/onset-<build>.apk`.

## Tested behaviour

The complete, correctly parsed list of every `group`/`test` call (with line numbers) is in **`desktop/SPEC/12-tests-index.md`** (generated by `desktop/tools/tests-index.mjs`); it is the oracle to port test by test to Rust (`wiremock`/`httpmock` for HTTP, JSON fixtures from `test/fixtures/`). HTTP is mocked in Dart with `http_mock_adapter` `DioAdapter` using full URLs. Per test file, what it asserts:

**test/apis**
- `test/apis/base_api_client_test.dart` — every status maps to its exception/kind (400, 401, 403, 404 with/without message, 409, 422, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504, unknown status → ServerException); `message` > `error` > `error_description` precedence, Twitch's explanation kept separately and blank/reason-phrase-only means none; patch/absolute URLs; `crashReasonFor` names only method + origin; a dropped connection after connect reads as `network` kind; connection fail/timeout/cancel kinds.
- `test/apis/bttv_api_test.dart` — global emotes, channel+shared emotes merged, 404 for unknown channel, badges map keyed by provider id, empty answers give empty results.
- `test/apis/channel_dj_role_test.dart` — `isParticipatingDj` reads the live DJ role; schema error, server error or bad login fail closed (false).
- `test/apis/clip_eligibility_test.dart` — `ClipEligibilityRefusal.fromGql` maps each error code, allowed/unknown/null mean no refusal; `clipRefusal` sends the exact document with the web token; errors/no token → null; a following-length refusal without minutes keeps its reason and the sheet words it.
- `test/apis/ffz_api_test.dart` — global emotes from all default sets, room info + channel emotes (+VIP badge), 404 → NotFound, user→badges map built in reversed badge order, `badgeDefinitions` by id, room `user_badge_ids` inverted.
- `test/apis/onset_update_service_test.dart` — `latest` with token and API headers; listed statuses / malformed / non-ONSET release / network error → null; bad repo never requested; anonymous: no Authorization header, 404 → not visible, 403/429 → rate limited (with a token those read as null); hash read through the redirect (also `sha256sum` output with filename), non-hash → null, unreachable hash → download error; download direct or via redirect followed by hand without the token, only to the four GitHub hosts and https, progress reported, mismatching hash deletes the file, no hash refuses, stalled body/hash time out, cancel stops and leaves nothing; `removeDownloadsUpTo` keeps only newer builds.
- `test/apis/seventv_api_test.dart` — global/channel emotes, empty URLs filtered, 404, channel's 7TV user id, `getEmoteSet` typed, cosmetics asked for all users in one aliased v4 query, no valid id → no request, answer without users → error.
- `test/apis/twitch_account_gql_clips_test.dart` — `deleteClip` sends DeleteClipsMutation as a user action, a clip absent from the answer is not deleted; `renameClip` sends the trimmed title, Twitch's error is a refusal; bad slug/title/no session send nothing.
- `test/apis/twitch_account_gql_comments_test.dart` — comment eligibility allowed/each refusal/unknown without token; `postComment` exact mutation returns the comment, refusal keeps code, integrity/signed out/empty/too long; like/unlike mutations; `ClipComment` like counts and dropped invalid nodes.
- `test/apis/twitch_account_gql_images_test.dart` — avatar upload: upload-URL mutation then PUT of the bytes; banner mutation; refusal or unsafe URL uploads nothing; error code is a refusal; `ProfileImageFile` accepts PNG/JPG/GIF ≤10 MB only.
- `test/apis/twitch_account_gql_profile_test.dart` — profile with web token, null without token/on error; rename status exact query; social links keep only http(s); username availability (integrity → unknown); display name/bio/username exact mutations, error code kept, invalid input not sent; social link add/edit/delete return the new list, errors are refusals, bad addresses never sent.
- `test/apis/twitch_api_test.dart` — emotes (type mapping), badges per set/version, users by login/id (odd creation date → null, unknown → NotFound), stream online/offline, `getStreamsByIds` query, revoke as form with the token's own client id, `getUserInfo(token:)` quiet with that token, `tokenUserId` sends the token alone, validate/checkToken (this app valid, other app foreign, missing client id refused, 401 invalid, server error rethrown, network not an answer), blocked list, quiet followed/top streams, `isFollowing`, pagination cursor and language params, `getChannel`.
- `test/apis/twitch_app_token_test.dart` — reads `{access_token, client_id}` from `<proxy>/app-token`; trailing slash ignored; port+path kept; redirect not followed; request quiet with no session header; no request names a client secret. **Refused without any request** (lines 147–160, 12 URLs): `''`, `http://onset-token.example.workers.dev`, `onset-token.example.workers.dev`, `https://`, `https://user:pass@onset-token.example.workers.dev`, `https://onset-token.example.workers.dev?next=1`, `ftp://onset-token.example.workers.dev`, `https://onset-token.example.workers.dev#x`, `https://onset-token.example.workers.dev/a b`, `https://onset-token.example.workers.dev/%2e%2e`, `https://onset_token.example.workers.dev`, `' https://onset-token.example.workers.dev'` (leading space). **Malformed bodies rejected** (lines 172–190, 10 cases): no `access_token` (`{"token": …}`), a number (`12345`), an empty token, a header injection (`abc\r\nX: 1`), spaces (`abc def ghi jkl`), an error object (`{"error":"unavailable"}`), no client id, a client id with spaces (`a b`), a numeric client id (`42`), an empty client id; plus an HTML page and a JSON list. **Statuses** 429, 502, 500, 404 (the `for` loop at lines 221–222) all throw ApiException. Timeout test (line ~232): a proxy answering after 5 s, called with `timeout: 100 ms`, must throw `TimeoutException` whose `kind == ApiErrorKind.tokenProxyTimeout`, in under 2 s elapsed. "The default deadline is short" (lines 255–260) only asserts `TwitchApi.appTokenTimeout <= 8 s`; the code value is 6 s (twitch_api.dart:204).
- `test/apis/twitch_auth_interceptor_test.dart` — headers added for helix and oauth2 URLs, never replacing a token the request names, none when `noSessionHeaders`, never for BTTV/FFZ/7TV/other hosts.
- `test/apis/twitch_block_api_test.dart` — block sends `target_user_id`, `source_context` and `reason`; without reason only the target.
- `test/apis/twitch_channel_profile_service_test.dart` — profile anonymously by lower-cased login, unknown → null, schedule (none → empty), bad login no request, errors without data throw typed, nested resolver error keeps the rest, user/schedule nulled by an error throws.
- `test/apis/twitch_channels_batch_api_test.dart` — `getChannelsByIds` asks many ids per request, >100 split into several requests.
- `test/apis/twitch_chat_history_service_test.dart` — one ChatHistory query with web token; nothing sent without token/bad channel; all drawn fields parsed (account colour fallback, plain text without fragments, sender-less message kept, unreadable entries skipped, newest 200); redirects not followed; only id-shaped ids kept; failures (unauthenticated, any GQL error even with data, 401, 500, timeout, malformed/hostile data) → empty; default timeout within 3–5 s.
- `test/apis/twitch_chat_rules_service_test.dart` — anonymous ChatRules by channel id; unknown → null; data-less answer throws; non-numeric id never sent.
- `test/apis/twitch_chat_ui_settings_test.dart` — reads the four synced settings with web token; a missing setting hides the sync; error/signed out → null; each setting saved with its own mutation; a change not echoed is not done; integrity refusal told apart.

## Wide and tablet layout

Not applicable to this area: lib/apis and lib/models contain no screen layout, so there is nothing that adapts to wide, tablet or desktop window sizes here. The only widgets inside (chat-line spans, emote/badge detail sheet, login dialog, bits threshold line) size themselves from the chat column and font scale, independently of window width; adaptive placement of chat and sheets is specified in the channel/chat leaves.

## PC equivalent

Rust crate mapping (one module per service, all on `reqwest` with `rustls`, `serde`/`serde_json`, `tokio`):
- `base_api_client` → `api::error` (`ApiError`, `ApiErrorKind` with the exact status table) + `api::http` thin wrappers `get/post/put/patch/delete` returning `Result<T, ApiError>`; crash reason = method + origin; logging via `tracing` (never bodies/tokens).
- `dio_client` + interceptors → several `reqwest::Client`s (timeouts as listed) and `reqwest-middleware` middlewares: `HelixAuth` (session Bearer/Client-Id, skip when already named or `NoSessionHeaders` extension), `Unauthorized` (emits an app event → "Session expired / Missing permissions" modal; app-token retry for anonymous), `TvSessionReroute` (same predicate and header replacement, 401 → mark rejected), optional `PerfTrace`.
- `twitch_api` / `twitch_mod_api` → `twitch::helix::{HelixClient, ModApi}`; models via `serde` structs with `#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]` and `#[serde(default)]` where the Dart code has defaults.
- `twitch_stream_service` → `twitch::playback::StreamService` (GQL token, usher URL with `url::form_urlencoded`, `futures::future::Shared` for in-flight token sharing, LRU via `lru` crate, `hls_m3u8` or a hand parser reproducing `HlsVariant`/`QualityLadder` exactly); feeds the PC low-latency player (libmpv/gstreamer) — every error → WebView-equivalent fallback (embedded webview via `wry`).
- `twitch_vod_service`, `twitch_clip_service` → `twitch::vod`, `twitch::clips` (`sha2` for token ids; clip download with `bytes_stream()` + size cap into `tokio::fs`).
- All GQL services (`viewer_actions`, `account_gql`, `social`, `community`, `community_highlights`, `hype_train`, `directory`, `channel_profile`, `chat_history`, `chat_rules`, `followed_categories`, `watch_streak`, `report`, `points`) → `twitch::gql::*` sharing a `GqlClient { http, web_token: Option<Secret> }` with documents as `const &str` copied from this spec and typed result enums identical to Dart's.
- `twitch_tv_auth_service` → `twitch::tv_auth` (device code flow, `keyring` for the session, open the activation URL with `open`/`webbrowser`).
- `bttv_api`, `ffz_api`, `seventv_api`, `giphy_service` → `thirdparty::{bttv, ffz, seventv, giphy}`.
- `onset_update_service` → `update::github` (same redirect allowlist and SHA-256 verification with `sha2`; desktop installer handoff per OS: Windows MSI/zip, macOS dmg/app replace, Linux AppImage replace).
- Models → `models::*` plain structs/enums; tolerant parsing via `serde_json::Value` helpers mirroring `chString`/`chInt`/`chDate`/`htField`; colours as `u32` ARGB; UTF-16-aware truncation helpers where Dart counts code units; realtime parsers (`hermes`, `hype_train_event`, `community_highlight_event`, `third_party_events`, `stream_push`) as `enum`s decoded from text frames received by `tokio-tungstenite`.
- **Known Android defects** (do not reproduce; PC fixes):
  1. `_splitWords` keeps an empty word before an emoji run (`a 😀 b` → `a`, `""`, `😀`, `b`) — PC: drop empty words everywhere (and keep filter/reply word indices consistent with that), so spacing is single.
  2. `IRCMessage.fromString` throws `RangeError` for a non-`tmi.twitch.tv` prefix without `!`, and mis-parses lines without `@tags` — PC parser must never panic: parse optional `@tags`, optional `:prefix` (nick = up to `!` or the whole prefix), command and params per IRCv3; an odd line yields a `Command::None` message or is skipped with a debug log, never an error that drops the connection.
  3. `getUserBlockedList` crashes on a body without `pagination` — PC: treat missing `pagination`/`cursor` as the last page (or map to `ApiError` kind `unknown`), never panic.
  4. `VodComment.fromGql` / `VodChapter.tryParse` throw on a non-finite `contentOffsetSeconds` / `positionMilliseconds` / `durationMilliseconds` — PC: treat non-finite (or out-of-range) numbers as missing: comment offset 0, chapter dropped; never fail the page.
  5. `isParticipatingDj` checks and sends the raw login (no trim/lower-case) — PC: normalise like the other profile calls (trim + lower-case, then validate).
  6. Update download: the second hop to the signed URL follows further redirects to any host — PC: disable automatic redirects and re-check every hop against the https/port-443/no-userinfo/host allowlist (SHA-256 still verified before use).
  7. UTF-16 length cuts (`shareResub` 255) can split an emoji — PC: keep the same limits measured in UTF-16 code units (to match Twitch: resub 255, report description 500, unban message 500 — those two are refused, not cut) but cut only on a char/grapheme boundary at or below the limit.
- `irc.dart` → split into `chat::irc::Message` (parser, exact rules) and the UI renderer (egui/iced/Slint rich-text spans with inline images via an image cache). (Toolkit superseded by D3: Slint.)

## Per-file sections


### lib/apis/base_api_client.dart

Foundation of every HTTP service. Imports `dart:io` `HttpException`/`SocketException`, `package:dio`, `Telemetry` (lib/services/telemetry.dart).

**Type aliases** (used everywhere in the API layer): `JsonMap = Map<String, dynamic>`, `JsonList = List<dynamic>`, `QueryParams = Map<String, dynamic>`, `ApiHeaders = Map<String, String>`.

**`crashReasonFor(RequestOptions request)`** returns `'${request.method} ${request.uri.origin}'` — the crash-report reason is ONLY method + scheme://host[:port]; never the path (it can name a channel or user id) nor the query (it can name whisper partners/chatters). The Rust port must apply the same redaction to any error log/crash report.

**`enum ApiErrorKind`** (order matters for nothing but keep names): `network` (no connection), `connectionTimeout`, `sendTimeout`, `receiveTimeout`, `cancelled`, `certificate` (TLS cert refused), `unknown` (anything else, and errors the app raises itself), `badRequest` (400), `unauthorized` (401), `forbidden` (403), `notFound` (404), `conflict` (409), `invalid` (422), `rateLimited` (429), `server` (500), `unavailable` (502), `maintenance` (503), `serverTimeout` (504), `http` (any other status, see `statusCode`), `userNotFound` (Helix returned an empty user list), `channelNotFound` (Helix returned no channel), `categoryNotFound` (no category with that id any more), `tokenProxyTimeout` (the logged-out token proxy did not answer in time). `lib/l10n/api_errors.dart` maps each kind to a localized sentence (see Strings section).

**`class ApiException implements Exception`**: fields `final String message` (English, logs/crash reports only, never shown), `final int? statusCode`, `final DioExceptionType? type`, `final ApiErrorKind kind` (default `unknown`), `final String? twitchMessage` (the server's own explanation from the body when non-blank; shown verbatim only on an English UI, otherwise logged). Positional constructor `ApiException(message, [statusCode, type, kind = unknown, twitchMessage])`. `toString()` = `'ApiException: $message'`.

Subclasses:
- `NetworkException(message)` → statusCode null, type `connectionError`, kind `network`.
- `TimeoutException(message, [kind = connectionTimeout])` → type always `DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout` (even for send/receive kinds), statusCode null.
- `ServerException(message, statusCode, [kind = server, twitchMessage])` → type `badResponse`.
- `NotFoundException(message, [twitchMessage, kind = notFound])` → statusCode 404, type `badResponse`.
- `UnauthorizedException(message)` → 401, `badResponse`, kind `unauthorized`.

**`abstract class BaseApiClient`**: `const BaseApiClient(Dio dio, String baseUrl)`; protected getter `dio`. `_buildUrl(endpoint)`: if the endpoint starts with `http://` or `https://` it is used verbatim, otherwise `'$baseUrl$endpoint'` (plain string concatenation, no slash normalisation). Five verbs, all `Future<T>` returning `response.data as T` (an unchecked cast: if the body type differs the call throws a Dart `TypeError`, NOT an ApiException):
- `get<T>(endpoint, {queryParameters, headers, cancelToken, extra, followRedirects})`
- `post<T>(endpoint, {data, queryParameters, headers, cancelToken, extra, followRedirects})`
- `put<T>(endpoint, {data, queryParameters, headers, cancelToken, extra})`
- `patch<T>(...)` same as put; `delete<T>(endpoint, {queryParameters, headers, cancelToken, extra})`.
`extra` is a per-request map read by interceptors (flags such as `UnauthorizedInterceptor.quiet`, `TwitchAuthInterceptor.noSessionHeaders`, `TwitchTvSessionInterceptor.userActionKey`). `followRedirects: null` keeps the client default (true, max 3); `false` makes a 3xx fail as `badResponse`. Every `DioException` is converted by `_handleError`.

**`_handleError(DioException)`** mapping:
| Dio type | Result |
|---|---|
| connectionTimeout | `TimeoutException('Connection timeout. Please try again.')` kind connectionTimeout |
| sendTimeout | `TimeoutException('Upload timeout. Please try again.', sendTimeout)` |
| receiveTimeout | `TimeoutException('Server response timeout. Please try again.', receiveTimeout)` |
| connectionError | `NetworkException('No internet connection. Please check your network.')` |
| badResponse | `_handleHttpError` (below) |
| cancel | `ApiException('Request was cancelled', null, null, cancelled)` |
| badCertificate | `ApiException('Security certificate error', null, null, certificate)` |
| unknown where inner error is `SocketException` or `HttpException` | `ApiException(error.message ?? 'An unexpected error occurred', null, null, network)` — deliberately a plain ApiException (NOT NetworkException) so callers that branch on NetworkException (token retry, anonymous-token skip) do not treat a dropped connection as offline, but the screen still words it as "network" |
| unknown (other) | `ApiException(error.message ?? 'An unexpected error occurred')` kind unknown |
(`_getTimeoutMessage` default branch `'Request timeout. Please try again.'` is unreachable.)

Telemetry: `Telemetry.recordError(exception, stackTrace, reason: crashReasonFor(request))` is called when the result is a `ServerException` OR the Dio type is `unknown` OR `badCertificate`. Timeouts, offline, 4xx (incl. 401) and cancellations are NOT recorded.

**`_handleHttpError`**: reads `statusCode` and body. If body is a JSON map: `text(key)` = the string value when non-blank after trim; `explanation = text('message') ?? text('error_description')` (screen-safe; never the bare `error` reason phrase); `serverMessage = text('message') ?? text('error') ?? text('error_description')` (logs). Mapping:
- 400 → `ApiException(serverMessage ?? 'Invalid request data', 400, badResponse, badRequest, explanation)`
- 401 → `UnauthorizedException('Please log in to continue')` (body ignored)
- 403 → `ApiException(serverMessage ?? 'Access denied. Insufficient permissions', 403, …, forbidden, explanation)`
- 404 → `NotFoundException(serverMessage ?? 'Resource not found', explanation)`
- 409 → `'Conflict with current state. Please refresh'`, kind conflict
- 422 → `'Invalid data provided'`, invalid
- 429 → `'Rate limit exceeded. Please wait before trying again'`, rateLimited
- 500 → `ServerException('Server error. Please try again later', 500)` (no twitchMessage)
- 502 → `ServerException('Service temporarily unavailable', 502, unavailable)`
- 503 → `ServerException('Service maintenance. Please try again later', 503, maintenance)`
- 504 → `ServerException('Server timeout. Please try again', 504, serverTimeout)`
- other → `ServerException(serverMessage ?? 'HTTP Error $statusCode occurred', statusCode ?? 500, http, explanation)` (so any other 4xx such as 410/418 is ALSO a ServerException and is recorded to telemetry).

Rust: `enum ApiErrorKind` + `struct ApiError { message, status: Option<u16>, transport: Option<TransportKind>, kind, twitch_message: Option<String> }` with variant helpers `is_network()`, `is_unauthorized()`, `is_not_found()`, `is_server()`; one `fn map_reqwest_error` and `fn map_status(status, body)` reproducing the table exactly.

### lib/apis/dio_client.dart

`DioClient.createClient({List<Interceptor>? additionalInterceptors})` builds THE shared Helix Dio (created once in `lib/main.dart`, line ~180, and passed to `TwitchApi`, `BTTVApi`, `FFZApi`, `SevenTVApi`). `BaseOptions`: `connectTimeout` 8 s, `receiveTimeout` 15 s, `sendTimeout` 10 s; default headers `User-Agent: ONSET (Flutter Twitch Client)`, `Accept: application/json`, `Content-Type: application/json`, `Connection: keep-alive`; `followRedirects: true`, `maxRedirects: 3`, `persistentConnection: true`. Interceptor order: (1) debug-only logger (only when `kDebugMode`): onRequest prints `→ METHOD uri`; onResponse prints `✅`/`❌` (2xx = ✅) + status + method + uri; onError prints `❌ <type.name> METHOD uri` and `   Status: <code>` when there is a response; (2) `FirebasePerformanceInterceptor`; (3) the optional additional interceptors in order. In `main.dart` two more are appended after the AuthStore exists: `TwitchAuthInterceptor(authStore)` then `UnauthorizedInterceptor(authStore)`. Final order on the shared client: logger(debug) → perf → auth headers → unauthorized handler.

The SECOND Dio ("anonymousGqlDio", main.dart) is a bare `Dio(BaseOptions(connectTimeout: 5 s, receiveTimeout: 8 s))` with NO default headers, no auth interceptor (it must never carry the user's Helix token), no perf interceptor; only `TwitchTvSessionInterceptor` is added to it. It is shared by: TwitchVodService, TwitchHypeTrainService, TwitchClipService, TwitchCommunityHighlightsService, TwitchChannelProfileService, TwitchDirectoryService, TwitchChatRulesService, TwitchChatHistoryService, TwitchFollowedCategoriesService, TwitchWatchStreakService, TwitchViewerActionsService, TwitchCommunityService, GiphyService, TwitchSocialService, TwitchAccountGqlService, TwitchReportService, TwitchTvAuthService. TwitchStreamService and TwitchPointsService build their own Dio (see their sections). The Rust port should keep at least three distinct `reqwest::Client`s: `helix` (8/15/10 s, default headers, auth middleware), `gql_anon` (5/8 s, no default headers), and the per-service ones. Debug logging must never print bodies or tokens (only method/url/status, as here).

### lib/apis/firebase_performance_interceptor.dart

`traceUrlFor(RequestOptions)` = `request.uri.origin` (service only; same privacy rule as crashReasonFor). `FirebasePerformanceInterceptor extends Interceptor`; extra key `'firebase_performance_metric'`. onRequest: `Telemetry.newHttpMetric(traceUrlFor(options), options.method)`; when non-null (build has Firebase) `metric.start()` (errors swallowed) and stored in `options.extra[_metricKey]`. onResponse/onError: `_stopMetric` removes the metric from extra, sets `httpResponseCode` when a status exists, `metric.stop()` (errors swallowed). Builds without the `FIREBASE_*` defines get null metrics → no-op. PC: optional; implement as a `tracing` span per request recording origin + method + status, or drop (telemetry is off by default in builds without Firebase).

### lib/apis/twitch_auth_interceptor.dart

`TwitchAuthInterceptor(AuthStore)`; extra flag constant `noSessionHeaders = 'twitch_auth_no_session_headers'`. onRequest: if `extra[noSessionHeaders] != true` AND the URL string starts with `https://api.twitch.tv/helix` or `https://id.twitch.tv/oauth2`, then for every entry of `authStore.headersTwitch` (= `{'Authorization': 'Bearer <token>'` only when a token exists, `'Client-Id': <tokenClientId ?? CLIENT_ID>}`) it adds the header ONLY IF the request does not already name that header (case-insensitive comparison). So a request that sets its own `Authorization` (e.g. validating a fresh login token) keeps it. Other hosts (BTTV, 7TV, FFZ, robotty, token proxy) never receive session headers. Logged-out viewers have the proxy app token, whose Client-Id is the proxy application's (`_tokenClientId`), not the build's CLIENT_ID.

### lib/apis/unauthorized_interceptor.dart

`UnauthorizedInterceptor(AuthStore)`; extra flag `quiet = 'onset.quietUnauthorized'`. `static bool promptsLogin(RequestOptions r)` = path does NOT end with `/validate` AND `extra[quiet] != true`. onError, when `response.statusCode == 401`:
1. if `!promptsLogin` → pass error on (`handler.next`).
2. if `authStore.isAnonymous` (logged-out viewer using the app token) → `unawaited(authStore.retryAppToken(refused: <the request's Authorization header value, found case-insensitively>))` (AuthStore rate-limits to once per minute and only refreshes if the refused header equals `Bearer <current>`), then pass error on; never a dialog.
3. otherwise → `_showLoginDialog()` then `handler.reject(err)`.
Non-401 errors pass through. Dialog: uses global `navigatorKey.currentContext` (no-op if null); static `_isDialogShowing` guard prevents stacking. Title/message: if `authStore.isLoggedIn` (logged in but token lacks a scope) → `authMissingPermissionsTitle` / `authMissingPermissions`; else → `authSessionExpiredTitle` / `authSessionExpired`. `barrierDismissible: false`; `OnsetAlertDialog` with actions `TextButton(commonCancel)` (closes) and `FilledButton(authLogIn)` (closes, then pushes `MaterialPageRoute` → `Provider<AuthStore>.value(child: LoginWebView())`). Flag reset on every close path and on `.then`. PC: a global "session expired / missing permissions" modal emitted through an app-event channel from the HTTP middleware; Login opens the PC OAuth flow.

### lib/apis/twitch_tv_session_interceptor.dart

Routes the viewer's GQL **mutations** through a separate "Twitch TV" (Android-TV device-code) session to pass Twitch's integrity check.

- `class TwitchTvTrace { bool usedTvSession = false; bool tvSessionRejected = false; }` — per-request observation object.
- `class TwitchTvCredentials { required TwitchTvSession session; required String clientSessionId; }`.
- `TwitchTvSessionInterceptor({required TwitchTvCredentials? Function() credentials, required void Function(String rejectedToken) onRejected})`.
- Constants: `userActionKey = 'onsetUserAction'`, `traceKey = 'onsetTvTrace'`, private `_viaTvKey = 'onsetViaTvSession'`; `userAction = {userActionKey: true}`; `tracedUserAction(trace) = {userActionKey: true, traceKey: trace}`; `backgroundOperations = {'UpdateUserViewedVideo'}` (never rerouted); `refusalText = 'failed integrity check'`; replaced headers (lower-case) `client-id`, `authorization`, `x-device-id`, `client-session-id`, `client-integrity`; mutation regex `^\s*mutation\b`.
- `isUserMutation(request)`: requires (`extra[userActionKey] == true` OR the request carries an `Authorization` header starting `OAuth `) AND method POST AND `uri.scheme == 'https' && host == 'gql.twitch.tv' && path == '/gql'` AND body is a Map whose `query` is a String matching the mutation regex AND `operationName` not in backgroundOperations. Persisted-query-only bodies (no `query`) are never rerouted; array bodies (batched) never.
- onRequest: if user mutation and `credentials()` non-null: remove the five replaced headers (case-insensitive) and add `headersFor(credentials)` = `{'Client-Id': 'ue6666qo983tsx6so1t0vnawi233wa' (kTwitchTvClientId), 'Authorization': 'OAuth <session.accessToken>', 'X-Device-Id': session.deviceId, 'Client-Session-Id': clientSessionId}`; set `extra[_viaTvKey]=true`; trace.usedTvSession = true.
- onResponse and onError: if sent via TV and status 401 → `_reject`: trace.tvSessionRejected = true; if `headers['Authorization']` (exact key) is a String starting `OAuth ` → `onRejected(<token after 'OAuth '>)` (store marks the session rejected). Response/error then passes on unchanged.
- `isIntegrityRefusal(body)`: true when body is a Map with `errors` List and any error Map's `'${message}'.toLowerCase()` contains `failed integrity check`. Used by callers (viewer actions, points, social…) to decide whether to fall back / show the TV-login offer.
Wiring (main.dart): credentials come from `TwitchTvSessionStore.credentials`, rejection → `markRejected`; added to anonymousGqlDio and to TwitchPointsService's own Dio. PC: a reqwest middleware with identical predicate; the TV session itself is obtained by `TwitchTvAuthService` (device code flow).

### lib/apis/twitch_api.dart

The Helix service (`class TwitchApi extends BaseApiClient`, base `https://api.twitch.tv/helix`), plus clip/token value types. Other bases: `_oauthBaseUrl = 'https://id.twitch.tv/oauth2'`, `_recentMessagesUrl = 'https://recent-messages.robotty.de/api/v2'`. Constructor `TwitchApi(Dio dio, {String? appClientId, String? tokenProxyUrl})` — defaults to the build constants `CLIENT_ID` and `TOKEN_PROXY_URL`. `late final TwitchModApi moderation = TwitchModApi(dio)` (same Dio). All Helix calls get `Authorization: Bearer …` + `Client-Id` from TwitchAuthInterceptor unless stated.

**Value types**
- `enum ClipIneligibility { notSubscribed, notFollowing, followingTooShort, categoryNotClippable, djCategory, participatingDj }`.
- `ClipEligibilityRefusal(reason, {int? requiredFollowMinutes})`; `static fromGql(Object? data)`: reads `data['canCreateClip']`; returns null unless it is a Map with `isAllowed == false`; `errorCode` mapping `CAN_CREATE_CLIP_NOT_SUBSCRIBED`→notSubscribed, `USER_NOT_FOLLOWING`→notFollowing, `FOLLOWING_LENGTH_REQUIREMENT_NOT_MET`→followingTooShort, `CATEGORY_NOT_CLIPPABLE`→categoryNotClippable, `DJ_CATEGORY_NOT_CLIPPABLE`→djCategory, `PARTICIPATING_DJ`→participatingDj, unknown code → null (allowed); `requiredFollowingLengthMinutes` kept only when an int > 0.
- `enum ClipCreateFailure { missingScope, restricted, offline, notClippable, rateLimited, unknown }`; `ClipCreateException(reason, [message = 'Clip failed']) extends ApiException`, `toString` `'ClipCreateException: $reason'`.
- `ClipCreation { String id; Uri? editUrl; get link => 'https://clips.twitch.tv/$id' }`; `CreatedClip { String id; String? thumbnailUrl }`.
- `AppToken { token, clientId }` (immutable; `toString` hides the token: `'AppToken($clientId)'`).
- `TokenCheck` with private kind valid/foreign/invalid: `TokenCheck.valid`, `TokenCheck.invalid`, `TokenCheck.foreign([clientId, hasUser=false])`; getters `isValid/isForeign/isInvalid`; `==` compares kind+clientId only (not hasUser); `toString` `'TokenCheck.<kind>'`.
- `enum BlockReason { harassment, spam, other }`, `enum BlockSource { chat, whisper }` (sent by `.name`).

**Validation helpers**: `_helixId = ^[0-9]{1,20}$`; `_checkId(id, name)` throws `ArgumentError.value(id, name, 'Not a Twitch id')` (before any request). `_messageId = ^[0-9a-fA-F-]{1,64}$`. `_reason(r)`: trim, truncate to `maxReasonLength = 500`. `maxTimeout = 14 days`. `_appTokenPattern = ^[A-Za-z0-9]{16,128}$`; `tokenProxyUrlPattern = r'^https://[A-Za-z0-9.-]+(:[0-9]+)?(/[A-Za-z0-9._~/-]*)?$'` (also used by CI); `_clientIdPattern = ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$`. `_modQuery(b, m)` = `{broadcaster_id: checked b, moderator_id: checked m}`.

**Methods** (HTTP, path, params → parse):
1. `getEmotesGlobal()` GET `/chat/emotes/global` → `data[]` → `EmoteTwitch.fromJson` → `Emote.fromTwitch(e, EmoteType.twitchGlobal)`.
2. `getEmotesChannel({id})` GET `/chat/emotes?broadcaster_id=id`; type by `emote_type`: `bitstier`→twitchBits, `follower`→twitchFollower, `subscriptions` and anything else → twitchChannel.
3. `getUserEmotes({userId, broadcasterId})` GET `/chat/emotes/user?user_id&broadcaster_id[&after=cursor]`, ids checked, extra quiet; loops up to `_maxEmotePages = 10` following `pagination.cursor` until null/empty; type: `globals`/`smilies`→twitchGlobal, `subscriptions`→twitchSub, `follower`→twitchFollower, `bitstier`→twitchBits, else twitchUnlocked. Needs `user:read:emotes`; old tokens get a quiet 401 and callers fall back to emote sets.
4. `getEmotesSets({setId})` GET `/chat/emotes/set?emote_set_id=` ; `globals`/`smilies`→twitchGlobal, `subscriptions`→twitchSub, else twitchUnlocked.
5. `getBadgesGlobal()` GET `/chat/badges/global`; for each `data[]` item `set_id` and each `versions[]` → `BadgeInfoTwitch.fromJson` → map key `'<set_id>/<version id>'` → `ChatBadge.fromTwitch`.
6. `getBadgesChannel({id})` GET `/chat/badges?broadcaster_id=` same mapping.
7. `getUserInfo({token, clientId})` GET `/users`; when `token` is given: headers `Authorization: Bearer <token>`, `Client-Id: clientId ?? appClientId`, extra `{quiet: true, noSessionHeaders: true}`; returns `UserTwitch.fromJson(data.first)` (empty list → StateError).
8. `getDefaultToken({timeout = appTokenTimeout (6 s)})`: endpoint = `<TOKEN_PROXY_URL with trailing '/' stripped from path>/app-token`; null (pattern mismatch or empty host) → throws `ApiException('No token proxy in this build')`. One overall deadline Timer cancels a CancelToken after `timeout` (covers connect). GET with `followRedirects: false`, extra `{quiet, noSessionHeaders}`. On ApiException after timeout → `TimeoutException('Token proxy timeout', tokenProxyTimeout)`; otherwise rethrow. Body must be a Map with `access_token` matching `_appTokenPattern` and `client_id` matching `_clientIdPattern`, else `ApiException('The token proxy sent no token')`. Returns `AppToken`.
9. `revokeToken({token, clientId})` POST `https://id.twitch.tv/oauth2/revoke`, form-urlencoded (`Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded`) body `client_id=<clientId ?? appClientId>&token=<token>`. (The auth interceptor will also add Bearer/Client-Id headers since the URL is under oauth2, unless already named.)
10. `validateToken({token})` → `checkToken(...).isValid`; logs `'Token validation: issued to another app.'` for foreign tokens.
11. `checkToken({token})` GET `https://id.twitch.tv/oauth2/validate`, header `Authorization: Bearer <token>`, extra noSessionHeaders. `client_id == appClientId` → valid; else `foreign(client_id if matches pattern else null, user_id non-empty string)`. `UnauthorizedException` → invalid (UnauthorizedInterceptor never prompts for /validate). Other ApiException → logged `'Token validation indeterminate: $e'` and rethrown.
12. `tokenUserId(token)` GET validate with `Authorization: OAuth <token>`, noSessionHeaders → `user_id` (non-empty String) or null; 401 → null; others throw. Used for the twitch.tv web cookie token.
13. `getTokenScopes()` GET validate (session headers), extra quiet → `Set<String>` of `scopes` strings.
14. `createClip({broadcasterId})` POST `/clips?broadcaster_id=` extra quiet. Error mapping by status: 401→missingScope, 403→restricted, 404→offline, 400→notClippable, 429→rateLimited, else unknown (`ClipCreateException(reason, e.message)`). Success: `data.first.id` must pass `isValidClipSlug` else unknown; `edit_url` kept only if https, no userinfo and host `twitch.tv` or ends with `.twitch.tv`.
15. `getClipsBetween({broadcasterId, broadcasterLogin = '', gameId, start, end})`: owner = broadcaster_id else game_id else none → `[]`; also `[]` if `end` not after `start`. GET `/clips?{owner}&started_at=<UTC ISO8601>&ended_at=<UTC ISO8601>&first=100` → `Clip.tryHelix(node, broadcasterLogin:)` skipping nulls.
16. `getCreatedClip(id)`: invalid slug → `ArgumentError`. GET `/clips?id=` → null when list empty (still processing), else `CreatedClip(id, thumbnailUrl only if starts with https://)`.
17. `getTopStreams({cursor, language = '', quiet = false})` GET `/streams[?after][&language]` (no params → null query), extra quiet when `quiet` → `StreamsTwitch.fromJson`.
18. `getFollowedStreams({id, cursor, first, quiet})` GET `/streams/followed?user_id=id[&after][&first=clamp(1,100)]`.
19. `getFollowedChannels({userId, cursor})` GET `/channels/followed?user_id&first=20[&after]` → `FollowedChannels.fromJson`.
20. `isFollowing({userId, broadcasterId})` GET `/channels/followed?user_id&broadcaster_id` quiet → true if any `data[]` item has `broadcaster_id == broadcasterId`.
21. `getStreamsUnderCategory({gameId, cursor, language})` GET `/streams?game_id[&after][&language]`.
22. `getStream({userLogin})` GET `/streams?user_login=` → first or throws `ApiException('$userLogin is offline', 404)` (kind unknown!).
23. `getStreamsByIds({userIds})` GET built manually: `/streams?user_id=a&user_id=b…&first=100` (ids NOT validated, not chunked — callers pass ≤100).
24. `getCheermotes({broadcasterId})` GET `/bits/cheermotes?broadcaster_id=` (checked) → `Cheermotes.fromHelix`.
25. `getUser({userLogin, id})` GET `/users` with `id=` preferred else `login=`; empty → `NotFoundException('User does not exist', null, userNotFound)`.
26. `getUsersByIds(ids)` batches of 100, GET `/users?id=..&id=..` (list query param repeated), ids checked; unknown ids omitted.
27. `getChannelsByIds(ids)` batches of 100, GET `/channels?broadcaster_id=..` repeated → `Map<broadcasterId, Channel>`.
28. `getChannel({userId})` GET `/channels?broadcaster_id=` → first or `NotFoundException('Channel does not exist', null, channelNotFound)`.
29. `searchChannels({query})` GET `/search/channels?first=8&query=` → `ChannelQuery.fromJson` list.
30. `getScheduleExtras({broadcasterId})` GET `/schedule?broadcaster_id&first=25` → `ScheduleExtras.fromHelix(data)`.
31. `getChannelTeams({broadcasterId})` GET `/teams/channel?broadcaster_id=` → first 20 `Team.fromJson` non-null.
32. `getTeam({name})` GET `/teams?name=` → first or null.
33. `getTopCategories({cursor})` GET `/games/top[?after]` → `CategoriesTwitch.fromJson`.
34. `getCategory({gameId})` GET `/games?id=`.
35. `searchCategories({query, cursor})` GET `/search/categories?first=8&query[&after]`.
36. `getUserBlockedList({id, cursor})` GET `/users/blocks?first=100&broadcaster_id=id[&after]`; `pagination` is REQUIRED: the code reads `data['pagination']['cursor']` unguarded before looking at `data`, so a body without `pagination` throws a Dart NoSuchMethodError (not an ApiException); recursion: while `pagination.cursor` non-null and page non-empty, waits **150 ms** then recurses (caps at 400 req/min vs Helix 800/min); empty first page → logs `'User does not have anyone blocked'` and `[]`.
37. `blockUser({userId, reason, source})` PUT `/users/blocks?target_user_id[&source_context=chat|whisper][&reason=harassment|spam|other]` → true / false on ApiException.
38. `unblockUser({userId})` DELETE `/users/blocks?target_user_id=` → bool.
39. `getSharedChatSession({broadcasterId})` GET `/shared_chat/session?broadcaster_id=` → null when empty else `SharedChatSession.fromJson`.
40. `getUserChatColor({userId})` GET `/chat/color?user_id=` → `color` or `''`; any ApiException → `''`.
41. `updateUserChatColor({userId, color})` PUT `/chat/color?user_id&color` (named color like `blue_violet` or `#RRGGBB` hex for Turbo/Prime) → bool (logs failure).
42. `banUser({broadcasterId, moderatorId, userId, duration, reason = ''})`: duration seconds must be 1…14 days else ArgumentError `'1s to 14 days'`; POST `/moderation/bans?broadcaster_id&moderator_id` body `{"data":{"user_id","duration"?(int seconds, omitted for permanent ban),"reason"}}`.
43. `unbanUser(...)` DELETE `/moderation/bans?broadcaster_id&moderator_id&user_id`.
44. `deleteChatMessages({broadcasterId, moderatorId, messageId})` DELETE `/moderation/chat?broadcaster_id&moderator_id[&message_id]` (null id = clear whole chat); invalid id → ArgumentError.
45. `warnUser({..., reason})` empty trimmed reason → ArgumentError `'A warning needs a reason'`; POST `/moderation/warnings?broadcaster_id&moderator_id` body `{"data":{"user_id","reason"}}`.
46. `getFollowedAt({broadcasterId, userId})` GET `/channels/followers?broadcaster_id&user_id` quiet → `followed_at` parsed or null (requires `moderator:read:followers`; 401 propagates as UnauthorizedException without dialog).
47. `updateChatSettings({broadcasterId, moderatorId, emoteMode, followerMode, followerModeDuration, slowMode, slowModeWaitTime, subscriberMode, uniqueChatMode})`: bounds `minSlowWait 3 s`, `maxSlowWait 120 s`, `maxFollowAge 90 days` (non-negative); body only present keys: `emote_mode`, `follower_mode`, `follower_mode_duration` (minutes), `slow_mode`, `slow_mode_wait_time` (seconds), `subscriber_mode`, `unique_chat_mode`; empty → `ArgumentError('Nothing to change')`; PATCH `/chat/settings?broadcaster_id&moderator_id`.
48. `createEventSubSubscription({type, version, condition, sessionId})` POST `/eventsub/subscriptions` quiet, body `{"type","version","condition":{…},"transport":{"method":"websocket","session_id":sessionId}}` → `data.first.id` (non-empty) else `ApiException('Malformed EventSub subscription')`.
49. `deleteEventSubSubscription(id)` DELETE `/eventsub/subscriptions?id=` quiet.
50. `sendWhisper({fromUserId, toUserId, message})`: trimmed 1…`maxWhisperLength = 10000` chars else `ArgumentError('A whisper is 1 to 10000 characters')` (text never in the error); POST `/whispers?from_user_id&to_user_id` body `{"message": text}`. Needs `user:manage:whispers` and a verified phone.
51. `sendChatMessage({broadcasterId, senderId, message})`: trimmed 1…`maxChatMessageLength = 500` else ArgumentError; POST `/chat/messages` quiet body `{"broadcaster_id","sender_id","message"}`; if `data.first.is_sent == false` → `ApiException('Chat message dropped', null, null, unknown, drop_reason.message)`.
52. `getRecentMessages({userLogin})` GET `https://recent-messages.robotty.de/api/v2/recent-messages/<login>` → `messages` list (raw IRC lines). Session headers are NOT added (different host).

### lib/apis/twitch_mod_api.dart

Helix moderation/broadcaster tools, exposed as `TwitchApi.moderation` (`TwitchModApi(Dio)`, base `https://api.twitch.tv/helix`, same shared Dio so the session `Authorization: Bearer` + `Client-Id` are added by TwitchAuthInterceptor). EVERY call passes `extra: {UnauthorizedInterceptor.quiet: true}` (`_quiet`): a 401 (missing scope, not a moderator) never shows the login dialog; the mod tool sheet words the failure itself.

**Enums** (wire values): `AnnouncementColor { primary, blue, green, orange, purple }` (sent by `.name`); `ChannelRole { moderator, vip }`; `SuspiciousStatus { monitored('ACTIVE_MONITORING'), restricted('RESTRICTED') }`; `AutoModDecision { allow('ALLOW'), deny('DENY') }`; `PredictionEnd { resolved('RESOLVED'), canceled('CANCELED'), locked('LOCKED') }`; `RedemptionDecision { fulfilled('FULFILLED'), canceled('CANCELED') }` — each with `helixValue`.

**`ModTextLimits`** (non-instantiable): `announcement 500`, `marker 140`, `blockedTermMin 2`, `blockedTerm 500`, `resolution 500`, `pollTitle 60`, `pollChoice 25`, `pollChoicesMin 2`, `pollChoicesMax 5`, `pollDurationMin 15 s`, `pollDurationMax 30 min`, `predictionTitle 45`, `predictionOutcome 25`, `predictionOutcomesMin 2`, `predictionOutcomesMax 10`, `predictionWindowMin 30 s`, `predictionWindowMax 30 min`, `streamTitle 140`, `tagsMax 10`, `tag 25`, `commercialMax 180` (seconds). The UI uses these for counters/validation too.

**Text helpers** (top-level, public): `_controlCharacters = [\u0000-\u001f\u007f  ]+` (the two line/paragraph separators are literal characters in the source). `singleLine(text, {maxLength})`: replace each run of those characters with ONE space, trim; if `maxLength` given, truncate to that many **Unicode code points (runes)** then `trimRight`. `isValidStreamTag(tag)` = `^[\p{L}\p{M}\p{N}]{1,25}$` (unicode letters, marks, digits only, 1–25). Private: `_helixId ^[0-9]{1,20}$` (`_id` → `ArgumentError.value(id, name, 'Not a Twitch id')`), `_opaqueId ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$` (`_opaque` → `'Not an id'`), `_required(text, name, max)` = singleLine+truncate, empty → `ArgumentError.value(text, name, 'Empty')`. `_list(data)` = `data['data']` filtered to `Map<String,dynamic>` items (non-list → `[]`). `_cursor(data)` = non-empty `pagination.cursor` or null.

**Pagination helper `_pages<T>(path, query, parse, {maxPages = 10})`**: GET `path` with `{...query, 'first': 100, 'after': cursor?}` (quiet) repeatedly, accumulating `_list(data).map(parse)` until no cursor or `maxPages` reached.

**Methods**
1. `sendAnnouncement({broadcasterId, moderatorId, message, color = primary})` POST `/chat/announcements?broadcaster_id&moderator_id`, body `{"message": required(≤500), "color": color.name}`. Scope `moderator:manage:announcements`.
2. `sendShoutout({broadcasterId, moderatorId, toBroadcasterId})` POST `/chat/shoutouts?from_broadcaster_id&to_broadcaster_id&moderator_id` (no body).
3. `getRoleUsers({broadcasterId, role})` paged GET `/moderation/moderators` (role moderator) or `/channels/vips` (vip), query `broadcaster_id` → `ChannelRoleUser.fromJson`.
4. `setRole({broadcasterId, userId, role, granted})` POST (grant) or DELETE (revoke) on the same role path with `?broadcaster_id&user_id`.
5. `getShieldMode({broadcasterId, moderatorId})` GET `/moderation/shield_mode?broadcaster_id&moderator_id` → `ShieldModeStatus.fromJson(first)`; empty → `ApiException('Malformed shield mode')`.
6. `setShieldMode({..., active})` PUT same path, body `{"is_active": active}` → parsed first, or `ShieldModeStatus(isActive: active)` when the answer has no item.
7. `createMarker({broadcasterId, description = ''})` POST `/streams/markers` body `{"user_id": broadcasterId, "description"?: singleLine(≤140) only when non-empty}`.
8. `startCommercial({broadcasterId, seconds})` 1…180 else `ArgumentError.value(seconds,'seconds','1 to 180')`; POST `/channels/commercial` body `{"broadcaster_id","length": seconds}` → `CommercialStart.fromJson(first)` or `CommercialStart(length: seconds)`.
9. `startRaid({broadcasterId, toBroadcasterId})` same ids → `ArgumentError(… 'Self')`; POST `/raids?from_broadcaster_id&to_broadcaster_id`.
10. `cancelRaid({broadcasterId})` DELETE `/raids?broadcaster_id`.
11. `getBlockedTerms({broadcasterId, moderatorId})` paged GET `/moderation/blocked_terms` → `BlockedTerm.fromJson`.
12. `addBlockedTerm({…, text})` singleLine(≤500); fewer than 2 runes → `ArgumentError('Too short')`; POST `/moderation/blocked_terms?broadcaster_id&moderator_id` body `{"text": term}` → first or `ApiException('Malformed blocked term')`.
13. `removeBlockedTerm({…, termId})` DELETE `/moderation/blocked_terms?broadcaster_id&moderator_id&id=<opaque>`.
14. `resolveHeldMessage({moderatorId, messageId, decision})` POST `/moderation/automod/message` body `{"user_id": moderatorId, "msg_id": opaque, "action": "ALLOW"|"DENY"}`.
15. `getPendingUnbanRequests({broadcasterId, moderatorId})` paged GET `/moderation/unban_requests?broadcaster_id&moderator_id&status=pending` → `UnbanRequest.fromJson` (model in lib/models/moderation.dart / unban_request.dart).
16. `resolveUnbanRequest({…, requestId, approve, resolution = ''})` PATCH `/moderation/unban_requests` with QUERY `broadcaster_id, moderator_id, unban_request_id=<opaque>, status=approved|denied, resolution_text?` (only when non-empty, singleLine ≤500). No body.
17. `setSuspiciousStatus({…, userId, status})`: `status == null` → DELETE `/moderation/suspicious_users?broadcaster_id&moderator_id&user_id`; else POST `/moderation/suspicious_users?broadcaster_id&moderator_id` body `{"user_id", "status": "ACTIVE_MONITORING"|"RESTRICTED"}`.
18. `getBan({broadcasterId, userId})` GET `/moderation/banned?broadcaster_id&user_id` → `BannedUser.fromJson(first)` returned only when its `userId == userId`, else null.
19. `getLatestPoll({broadcasterId})` GET `/polls?broadcaster_id&first=1` → `ChannelPoll?`.
20. `createPoll({broadcasterId, title, choices, duration})`: choices cleaned (singleLine ≤25, empties dropped) must be 2…5 else `ArgumentError('2 to 5')`; duration 15 s…30 min else `'15 s to 30 min'`; POST `/polls` body `{"broadcaster_id","title": required(≤60),"choices":[{"title":c}…],"duration": seconds}` → first or `ApiException('Malformed poll')`.
21. `endPoll({broadcasterId, pollId})` PATCH `/polls` body `{"broadcaster_id","id": opaque,"status":"TERMINATED"}` → ChannelPoll or `'Malformed poll'`.
22. `getLatestPrediction({broadcasterId})` GET `/predictions?broadcaster_id&first=1`.
23. `createPrediction({broadcasterId, title, outcomes, window})`: outcomes singleLine ≤25 non-empty, 2…10 else `'2 to 10'`; window 30 s…30 min else `'30 s to 30 min'`; POST `/predictions` body `{"broadcaster_id","title": required(≤45),"outcomes":[{"title":o}],"prediction_window": seconds}` → first or `ApiException('Malformed prediction')`.
24. `endPrediction({broadcasterId, predictionId, end, winningOutcomeId})`: resolved without winner → `ArgumentError('A resolved prediction needs a winner')`; PATCH `/predictions` body `{"broadcaster_id": checked numeric id, "id": _opaque(predictionId) (`^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$`, else `ArgumentError(… 'Not an id')`), "status": helixValue, "winning_outcome_id"? (only when resolved; also validated with `_opaque`, `ArgumentError(… 'Not an id')`)}`; returns `ChannelPrediction.fromJson(data[0])`, and throws `ApiException('Malformed prediction')` when the answer has no item.
25. `getPendingRedemptions({broadcasterId})`: GET `/channel_points/custom_rewards?broadcaster_id&only_manageable_rewards=true`; for each reward whose `id` matches `_opaqueId`, paged GET (`maxPages: 2`) `/channel_points/custom_rewards/redemptions?broadcaster_id&reward_id&status=UNFULFILLED&sort=OLDEST&first=100` → `RewardRedemption.fromJson`; the merged list is sorted ascending by `redeemedAt`. Sequential (one reward after another).
26. `resolveRedemption({broadcasterId, rewardId, redemptionId, decision})` PATCH `/channel_points/custom_rewards/redemptions?id&broadcaster_id&reward_id` body `{"status":"FULFILLED"|"CANCELED"}`.
27. `updateStreamInfo({broadcasterId, title, gameId, tags})`: body only with given keys — `title` required(≤140); `game_id` = `''` when empty string (clears category) else checked numeric id; `tags`: singleLine each, de-duplicated in insertion order (Set), empties removed, more than 10 or any failing `isValidStreamTag` → `ArgumentError('Up to 10 letters-only tags')`; empty body → `ArgumentError('Nothing to change')`; PATCH `/channels?broadcaster_id` with that JSON body.

All ArgumentErrors are thrown before any network I/O. Errors from the server follow the BaseApiClient table (403 forbidden etc., with Twitch's `message` as `twitchMessage`). Rust: `ModApi<'a>(&'a HelixClient)`; validate with the same regexes (`regex` crate with Unicode classes for tags), measure lengths in `chars()` (code points) to match runes.

### lib/apis/twitch_stream_service.dart

The live low-latency playback path: GQL `PlaybackAccessToken` → usher master playlist. Base URL `''` (every call is an absolute URL). Constructor `TwitchStreamService(Dio dio, {PlaybackTokenCache? tokenCache})` (`_http` = same Dio). Instances: `PlaybackPreloader.instance` uses `PlaybackTokenCache.shared` and `PlaybackPreloader.http` = `Dio(BaseOptions(connectTimeout 5 s, receiveTimeout 8 s))` with an `IOHttpClientAdapter` whose `HttpClient.idleTimeout = 60 s` (keeps the TLS connection to usher/gql warm); `NativeVideoStore` and `IvsVideoStore` and `live_feed.dart` construct their own over that same Dio (IVS store with the shared cache). No auth interceptor: headers are set explicitly. `typedef UsherRefusal = ({PlaybackRefusal? refusal, String? reason})`.

**GQL document** (sent as raw `query`, not a persisted hash, so hash rotations cannot break it). Exact concatenated text of `accessTokenQuery` (the `$` are literal GQL variables):
```
query PlaybackAccessToken_Template($login: String!, $isLive: Boolean!, $vodID: ID!, $isVod: Boolean!, $playerType: String!) {  streamPlaybackAccessToken(channelName: $login, params: {platform: "web", playerBackend: "mediaplayer", playerType: $playerType}) @include(if: $isLive) {    value signature authorization { forbiddenReasonCode } __typename  }  videoPlaybackAccessToken(id: $vodID, params: {platform: "web", playerBackend: "mediaplayer", playerType: $playerType}) @include(if: $isVod) {    value signature __typename  }}
```
`plainAccessTokenQuery` is identical except the live block selects `    value signature __typename` (no `authorization { forbiddenReasonCode }`). Whitespace is exactly as concatenated (two spaces before `streamPlaybackAccessToken`, four before `value`).

`accessTokenRequest(login, query)` body: `{"operationName":"PlaybackAccessToken_Template","query":query,"variables":{"isLive":true,"login":login,"isVod":false,"vodID":"","playerType":"embed"}}`. `_postAccessToken`: POST `https://gql.twitch.tv/gql` with headers `Client-ID: kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko` (+ `Authorization: OAuth <authToken>` when an auth token is passed). Plus the Dio defaults of PlaybackPreloader.http (none).

**`_fetchAccessToken(login, {authToken})`**: post with `accessTokenQuery`; token = `data.data.streamPlaybackAccessToken`. If that is not a map AND `data.errors` is a List → log `'Playback token query refused: …'` and retry once with `plainAccessTokenQuery`. If still not a map or `value == null` → `ApiException('No playback access token (channel offline?)')`. Builds `PlaybackAccessToken.fromValue(value, signature, refusal: PlaybackRefusal.fromCode(authorization.forbiddenReasonCode) when that is a String, else null)`. Only a token WITHOUT refusal is `put` into the cache (a refused one must be re-asked next time). Always `_remember(login, token)`.

**`_remember`**: `_previewEnds[login] = token.previewEndsAt` (or removed); `_refusals[login] = refusal` (or removed). Public readers: `refusalFor(userLogin)`, `freePreviewEndFor(userLogin)`.

**Cache logic `_cachedAccessToken(login, {authToken, forceFresh})`**: key `_cacheKey` = `login` when anonymous, else `'$login#${authToken.hashCode}'` (Dart String hashCode — PC can use any stable per-token hash; the point: authenticated and anonymous tokens, and different viewers, never share entries). With a cache: `forceFresh` → `invalidate(key)` then fetch; else a valid cached token is returned (and remembered); else an in-flight request for the same key (`pending`) is awaited and shared; else fetch and `track(key, future)`. PlaybackTokenCache (lib/services): keys lower-cased, LRU capacity 32, valid only if `expiresAt` known and `now + 60 s` is before it.

**`_getAccessToken(login, {authToken, forceFresh})`**: if a non-empty auth token (the viewer's twitch.tv web cookie token, needed for enhanced-broadcast 1440p/HEVC/AV1 renditions): try authenticated; on `UnauthorizedException` log `'Authenticated playback token refused: …'` and fire-and-forget `onWebTokenRejected()` (errors logged `'Web token renewal failed: …'`) so the NEXT request has a fresh cookie; on any other error log `'Authenticated playback token failed: …'`. Then (or with no token) fall back to the anonymous token immediately. The hook `Future<String?> Function()? onWebTokenRejected` is wired by the video stores to AuthStore.renewTwitchWebToken.

**`usherQueryParameters({signature, token, p})`** (exact order): `allow_source=true`, `allow_audio_only=true`, `fast_bread=true` (low-latency segments; no legacy `low_latency`/`player=twitchweb`), `supported_codecs=av1,h265,h264`, `reassignments_supported=true`, `playlist_include_framerate=true`, `p=<int>`, `sig=<signature>`, `token=<token value JSON>`, `type=any`. `_randomP()` = `DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch % 10000000`.

**Public methods**
- `getStreamVariants({userLogin, authToken, cancelToken})`: token, then GET `https://usher.ttvnw.net/api/channel/hls/<login>.m3u8` with those params (Dio-encoded), `cancelToken` cancels only the playlist (the token request is shared via the cache) → `HlsVariant.parseMasterPlaylist(body)`.
- `getUsherUrl({userLogin, authToken, forceFreshToken = false})`: returns the URL string `https://usher.ttvnw.net/api/channel/hls/<login>.m3u8?` + params joined `key=Uri.encodeQueryComponent(value)` with `&` (form encoding: spaces become `+`). Handed to the IVS/ExoPlayer player which fetches it itself.
- `streamRestrictionTier(userLogin)`: POST gql, header `Client-ID` web id, body `{"operationName":"StreamRestriction","query":"query StreamRestriction($login: String!) { user(login: $login) { stream { id restrictionType restrictionOptions } } }","variables":{"login":userLogin}}`; if `data.user.stream.restrictionOptions` is a List → `SubscriberTier.fromRestrictionOptions`; anything else/any error → null (logged `'Stream restriction unavailable: …'`).
- `qualityLadder(url)`: only for host `usher.ttvnw.net`, else null; re-requests the URL with `include_unavailable=true` added → `QualityLadder.parse` when the body is a String; DioException → null.
- `usherRefusal(url)` = `usherRefusalDetails(url)?.reason`.
- `usherRefusalDetails(url)`: host must be usher; GET with `responseType: plain`, `validateStatus: (_) => true`; status < 400 → null; body JSON-decoded must be a non-empty List whose first is a Map; `refusal = PlaybackRefusal.fromUsherCode(error_code)`; `reason = _usherReason(error)` (whitespace collapsed, trimmed, empty → null, longer than 200 chars → first 200 + `…`); both null → null; DioException/FormatException → null.
- `preload(userLogin, {authToken})`: `_getAccessToken` ignoring errors (logged `'Playback token preload failed: …'`) — triggered on stream-card press.

Callers must treat any throw as "fall back to the WebView engine" (CLAUDE.md mission). Rust: `StreamService { client, cache: Arc<Mutex<TokenCache>>, pending: HashMap<key, Shared<BoxFuture<Result<Token>>>> }` (futures::future::Shared for request coalescing), `on_web_token_rejected: Option<Arc<dyn Fn() -> BoxFuture<Option<String>>>>`; usher URL built with `url::form_urlencoded::Serializer`.

### lib/apis/twitch_vod_service.dart

Past broadcasts, highlights, uploads, collections, chat replay, chapters, muted parts, paid-promotion flags, watch positions and the VOD usher playlist — all through Twitch GQL with full query documents (no persisted hashes) and the public web Client-ID.
