Constructor `TwitchVodService(Dio dio, {PlaybackTokenCache? tokenCache})` (base `''`; default cache = a fresh `PlaybackTokenCache()` — NOT the shared live cache); in the app it uses anonymousGqlDio (connect 5 s / receive 8 s, TwitchTvSessionInterceptor attached). Comments and channel lists are fetched WITHOUT cursors because cursor paging is integrity-gated for anonymous clients (verified 2026-10-13); search paging does work anonymously (2026-10-20).

**Injected hooks** (wired in main.dart): `String? Function()? webToken` → `authStore.twitchWebToken` (the viewer's twitch.tv `auth-token` cookie; sent ONLY to gql.twitch.tv and only for the playback token and watch-position requests); `Future<String?> Function()? onWebTokenRejected` → `authStore.renewTwitchWebToken`; `String? Function()? viewerId` → logged-in user id or null.

**Exceptions**: `VodApiException(message, {bool isIntegrity = false}) extends ApiException` (`toString` `'VodApiException: $message'`); `VodForbiddenException(String? reason)` message `'Video playback is forbidden'`. `enum VodPlaybackProblem { subscriberOnly, geoBlocked, tokenRejected, notFound, proxyDetected, wifiRequired, unsupportedDevice, unknown }`.

**Shared field list `_videoFields`** (exact): `id title lengthSeconds createdAt viewCount status broadcastType previewThumbnailURL(width: 640, height: 360) game { id name } owner { id login displayName } broadcast { id } self { isRestricted }`.

**GQL documents** (operationName = the query name; `$` are GQL variables):
- ChannelVideos: `query ChannelVideos($login: String!, $type: BroadcastType, $first: Int) { user(login: $login) { id login displayName videos(first: $first, type: $type, sort: TIME) { edges { node { <videoFields> } } pageInfo { hasNextPage } } } }`
- CategoryVideos: `query CategoryVideos($id: ID!, $first: Int, $sort: VideoSort) { game(id: $id) { id videos(first: $first, sort: $sort) { edges { node { <videoFields> } } pageInfo { hasNextPage } } } }`
- FollowedVideos: `query FollowedVideos($ids: [ID!], $first: Int) { users(ids: $ids) { id login displayName videos(first: $first, sort: TIME) { edges { node { <videoFields> } } } } }`
- Video: `query Video($id: ID!) { video(id: $id) { <videoFields> } }`
- SearchVideos: `query SearchVideos($query: String!, $target: SearchForTarget) { searchFor(userQuery: $query, platform: "android", target: $target) { videos { cursor items { <videoFields> } pageInfo { hasNextPage } } } }`
- VideoComments: `query VideoComments($id: ID!, $offset: Int) { video(id: $id) { comments(contentOffsetSeconds: $offset) { edges { node { id contentOffsetSeconds commenter { id login displayName } message { fragments { text emote { emoteID } } userBadges { setID version } userColor } } } pageInfo { hasNextPage } } } }`
- VideoContentLabels: `query VideoContentLabels($id: ID!) { video(id: $id) { contentClassificationLabels { id } } }`
- BrandedContentStreamMetadataQuery: `query BrandedContentStreamMetadataQuery($channelId: ID!) { user(id: $channelId) { id stream { contentPolicyProperties { hasBrandedContent } } } }`
- BrandedContentVodMetadataQuery: `query BrandedContentVodMetadataQuery($vodId: ID!) { video(id: $vodId) { contentPolicyProperties { hasBrandedContent } } }`
- CollectionFirstVideo: `query CollectionFirstVideo($id: ID!) { collection(id: $id) { items(first: 1) { edges { node { id } } } } }`
- CollectionVideos: `query CollectionVideos($id: ID!) { collection(id: $id) { items(first: 100) { edges { node { ... on Video { <videoFields> } } } } } }`
- ChannelCollections: `query ChannelCollections($channelId: ID!) { user(id: $channelId) { collections(first: 50) { edges { node { id title thumbnailURL(width: 300, height: 300) updatedAt lengthSeconds items(first: 1) { totalCount edges { node { ... on Video { id } } } } } } } } }`
- VideoMutedSegments: `query VideoMutedSegments($id: ID!) { video(id: $id) { muteInfo { mutedSegmentConnection { nodes { offset duration } } } } }`
- VideoChapters: `query VideoChapters($id: ID!) { video(id: $id) { moments(momentRequestType: VIDEO_CHAPTER_MARKERS, types: [GAME_CHANGE]) { edges { node { positionMilliseconds durationMilliseconds description details { ... on GameChangeMomentDetails { game { id displayName boxArtURL(width: 40, height: 53) } } } } } } } }`
- VideoViewingHistory: `query VideoViewingHistory($id: ID!) { video(id: $id) { self { viewingHistory { position updatedAt } } } }`
- ResumeWatchingVideos: `query ResumeWatchingVideos($limit: Int!) { currentUser { id viewedVideos(first: $limit) { edges { history { position updatedAt } node { <videoFields> } } } } }`
- UpdateUserViewedVideo (mutation, accepted with web Client-ID + OAuth web token; anonymous → "unauthenticated"): `mutation UpdateUserViewedVideo($input: UpdateUserViewedVideoInput!) { updateUserViewedVideo(input: $input) { video { id } } }` (its operationName is in TwitchTvSessionInterceptor.backgroundOperations, so it is never rerouted to the TV session).
- VodToken: `query VodToken($id: ID!) { videoPlaybackAccessToken(id: $id, params: {platform: "web", playerBackend: "mediaplayer", playerType: "site"}) { value signature } }`
- VideosById (built dynamically): for each wanted id i: parameter `$i<i>: ID!` and field `v<i>: video(id: $i<i>) { <videoFields> } `; document `query VideosById(<params joined ", ">) { <fields>}`; variables `{"i0": id0, …}`.

**`_gql(operationName, query, variables, {headers = {}, partial = false, onErrors})`**: POST `https://gql.twitch.tv/gql`, headers `{'Client-ID': 'kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko', ...headers}`, body `{"operationName","query","variables"}`. If `errors` is a non-empty List: when `partial` and `data` is a Map → `onErrors(errors)` and continue; otherwise throw `VodApiException(<each error's message joined "; ">, isIntegrity: lower-cased messages contain "integrity")`. `data` not a Map → `VodApiException('Empty GQL response')`. Returns a copy of `data`. HTTP-level errors come through BaseApiClient mapping (401 → UnauthorizedException etc.).

**Id rules**: `normalizeVodId(raw)` accepts `123` or `v123`, requires `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, else `ArgumentError.value(raw,'vodId','Not a VOD id')` (programming error); `tryNormalizeVodId(raw)` returns null instead (for untrusted links). Channel ids / game ids validated `^[0-9]{1,20}$`; collection ids `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$`.

**Methods**
1. `getChannelVideos({login, VodType? type})` → ChannelVideos with `{login, type: type?.gqlName (null = all kinds), first: 100}`. `user` not a Map → `NotFoundException('Channel not found')`. Owner map `{id, login, displayName}` from the user passed to each `Vod.fromGql(node, owner:)`; nodes throwing FormatException skipped. Returns `VodPage(items, hasMore: pageInfo.hasNextPage == true)`.
2. `getFollowedVideos(ids, {perChannel = 5})`: every id must be numeric else `ArgumentError.value(id,'ids','Not a channel id')`; batches of `_followedBatch = 50` ids per request (FollowedVideos `{ids: batch, first: perChannel}`); `users` not a List → `VodApiException('Unreadable videos')`; merged and sorted by `createdAt` descending (newest first across channels). Used by the Following page's videos.
3. `getCategoryVideos(gameId)`: numeric else `ArgumentError('Not a category id')`; CategoryVideos `{id, first: 100, sort: 'VIEWS'}`; `game` not Map → `NotFoundException('Category not found')`; `videos` not Map → `VodApiException('Unreadable videos')`; at most 100 edges; if every edge failed to parse (items empty but edges not) → `VodApiException('Unreadable videos')`.
4. `searchVideos(query, {cursor})`: trimmed empty → `ArgumentError('Nothing to search')`; variables `{query: text, target: {index: 'VOD', limit: searchPageSize (25), cursor? (only non-empty)}}`; `searchFor.videos` not a Map → `VodApiException('Unreadable videos')`; items parsed (all unreadable while some were maps → same error); `hasMore` = `pageInfo.hasNextPage == true` AND `videos.cursor` a non-empty String; returns `VodSearchPage(items, cursor: hasMore ? cursor : null, hasMore)`.
5. `getVideo(vodId)` → Video `{id: normalized}`; `video` not a Map → null (deleted/wrong id); parse failure → `VodApiException('Unreadable video')`.
6. `getVideos(ids)`: normalizes with `tryNormalizeVodId` (invalid skipped), keeps the first `_videosBatch = 25`; empty → `VideoLookup(videos: [], missing: {})`. Sends VideosById with `partial: true`; `onErrors` collects each error's `path.first` alias into `refused`. For alias `v<i>`: Map → parsed (any exception skipped, logged `'Unreadable video skipped: <type>'`); null and not refused → id added to `missing` (deleted). Refused aliases are in neither list (can be retried). Throws only when the whole answer has no data.
7. `getResumeWatching({limit = 20})` → `List<ResumeWatchingEntry>?`, never throws. Requires `_usableWebToken` (else null) and not `_historyRefused('shelf', tokenId)`. ResumeWatchingVideos `{limit}` with `Authorization: OAuth <webToken>`, `partial: true`. On success clears `shelf#tokenId` failures. Requires `currentUser.id` (stringified) == `viewerId()` (a stale web token of another account is ignored; logs `'Continue watching of another account'`). Each edge: `history.position` must be an int ≥ 0, node parsed, `vod.id` must normalize; entry `ResumeWatchingEntry(vod, ViewingHistory(positionSec, updatedAt: DateTime.tryParse(history.updatedAt)))`. 401 → `_onWebTokenUnauthorized`; VodApiException → `_historyFailed('shelf', …)`; other → logged; all return null.
8. `getComments({vodId, offsetSeconds})` → VideoComments `{id, offset}`; missing `video.comments` → `VodApiException('No comments')`; returns `VodCommentPage(comments: VodComment.fromGql each node, hasNextPage)`. Twitch returns a page starting just before the offset, oldest first; the replay controller de-duplicates with `VodComment.key`.
9. `getContentLabels(vodId)` → ids of `contentClassificationLabels` (null → `[]`; not a List → `VodApiException('Unreadable labels')`; no video → `NotFoundException('Video not found')`).
10. `streamHasPaidPromotion(channelId)` (non-numeric → false) and `vodHasPaidPromotion(vodId)` via `_paidPromotion`: true only when `user.stream.contentPolicyProperties.hasBrandedContent == true` / `video.contentPolicyProperties.hasBrandedContent == true`; any error → false (logged `'Paid promotion unavailable: <type>'`).
11. `getCollectionVideos(collectionId)` invalid id → `[]`; CollectionVideos → Vods (FormatException skipped); any error → `[]`.
12. `getChannelCollections(channelId)` non-numeric → null; ChannelCollections → `VideoCollection.listFrom(data)`; error → null.
13. `firstVideoOfCollection(collectionId)` → numeric id of first item or null (errors → null). Used to open playlist links.
14. `getChapters(vodId)` → `sortedChapters(VodChapter.tryParse(edge.node)…)`; errors → `[]`.
15. `getMutedSegments(vodId)` → `MutedSegment(offset, duration)` for nodes with numeric `offset` & `duration` (toInt); errors → `[]`.
16. `getVodUsherUrl({vodId, forceFreshToken = false, anonymous = false})`: token via `_getToken`; `VodAccessInfo.fromTokenValue(token.value)`; forbidden → `VodForbiddenException(access.reason)`; URL = `https://usher.ttvnw.net/vod/<id>.m3u8?` + `vodUsherQueryParameters` encoded `key=Uri.encodeQueryComponent(value)` joined `&`. Params (exact order): `allow_source=true`, `allow_audio_only=true`, `supported_codecs=av1,h265,h264`, `playlist_include_framerate=true`, `p=<microsecondsSinceEpoch % 10000000>`, `sig`, `token` (no `fast_bread`, no `type`).
17. `diagnoseVodPlaylist(url)` → `VodPlaybackProblem?`, never throws, never logs the URL (it holds the token). Host not `usher.ttvnw.net` → unknown. GET with `validateStatus: (_) => true`, `responseType: json`. 2xx → null; 404 → notFound; body (first element if a List) `error_code`: `vod_manifest_restricted`→subscriberOnly, `content_geoblocked`→geoBlocked, `anonymizer_blocked`→proxyDetected, `cellular_geoblocked`→wifiRequired, `no_cdm_specified`→unsupportedDevice, prefix `nauth`→tokenRejected, else unknown (usher answers a bad token and a missing VOD alike with 403 empty body). Any exception → unknown.
18. `preloadToken(vodId)` → `_getToken(…, forceFresh: false)` ignoring errors (logged `'VOD token preload failed: …'`).
19. `invalidateToken(vodId)` → invalidates the anonymous key and the current web-token key.
20. `getViewingHistory(vodId)` → `ViewingHistory?`, never throws; needs valid id + usable web token + not refused('read'); VideoViewingHistory with OAuth web token; position int ≥ 0 else null.
21. `updateViewedVideo({videoId, WatchedVideoType type, positionSec})` → bool, never throws; requires normalizable id, numeric viewer id, `positionSec > 0`, usable token, not refused('write'). Variables `{"input":{"userID": viewerId, "videoID": id, "videoType": "LIVE"|"VOD", "position": positionSec}}` with `Authorization: OAuth <token>`; true when `updateUserViewedVideo` is a Map. (Live broadcasts are saved by stream id with type LIVE.)

**Token logic**
- `_tokenId(token)` = first 16 hex chars of SHA-256(utf8(token)) — names a web token without holding it.
- `_cacheKey(id, authToken)` = `id` or `'$id#${_tokenId(authToken)}'`.
- `_getToken(id, {forceFresh, anonymous})`: authToken = `webToken()` unless anonymous; if non-empty and its id ≠ `_refusedWebToken`: try `_cachedToken` authenticated; `UnauthorizedException` → log + `_onWebTokenUnauthorized`; `NetworkException`/`TimeoutException` → rethrow (anonymous would fail too, caller's retry policy applies); other ApiException → log and fall back. Then anonymous `_cachedToken`.
- `_cachedToken`: forceFresh → invalidate; cached → return; pending → return shared future; else `_fetchToken` + `track`.
- `_fetchToken`: VodToken `{id}` with optional `Authorization: OAuth`; missing map/value → `ApiException('No playback access token for this video')`; `signature` defaults `''`; cached only when not forbidden.
- `_onWebTokenUnauthorized(token)`: remember its id in `_refusedWebToken` (never sent again until it changes) and fire-and-forget renewal (`'Web token renewal failed: <type>'`).
- History failure counters `_historyFailures` keyed `'<kind>#<tokenId>'` with kinds `read`, `write`, `shelf`; `_historyMaxFailures = 3`; an integrity error jumps straight to 3; a success removes the key; at ≥3 the request is not sent again with that token this session.

Rust: `VodService` with `serde_json::Value` navigation (tolerant parsing), `sha2` for token ids, a shared `TokenCache` instance separate from live, `Arc<dyn Fn() -> Option<String>>` hooks.

### lib/apis/twitch_clip_service.dart

Anonymous GQL clips service (`TwitchClipService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio). Channel/category lists are a single page of up to 100 (cursor paging integrity-gated, verified 2026-10-13); the top-clips feed and clip comments page by cursor (verified 2026-10-20).

**Exceptions**: `ClipApiException(message, {isIntegrity = false})` (`toString` `'ClipApiException: $message'`); `ClipForbiddenException(String? reason)` message `'Clip playback is forbidden'`.

**`_clipFields`** (exact): `id slug title viewCount createdAt durationSeconds thumbnailURL videoOffsetSeconds curator { id login displayName } broadcaster { id login displayName } game { id name } video { id }`.

**Documents** (operationName = query name):
- ChannelClips: `query ChannelClips($login: String!, $first: Int, $period: ClipsPeriod, $sort: ClipsSort) { user(login: $login) { id login displayName clips(first: $first, criteria: {period: $period, sort: $sort}) { edges { node { <clipFields> } } pageInfo { hasNextPage } } } }`. The "featured" variant is the same text with the first occurrence of `<clipFields> }` replaced by `<clipFields> isFeatured }`.
- CuratedClips: `query CuratedClips($id: ID!, $first: Int, $period: ClipsPeriod, $sort: ClipsSort) { user(id: $id) { id clips(first: $first, criteria: {role: CURATOR, period: $period, sort: $sort}) { edges { node { <clipFields> } } pageInfo { hasNextPage } } } }`
- CategoryClips: `query CategoryClips($id: ID!, $first: Int, $period: ClipsPeriod, $sort: ClipsSort) { game(id: $id) { id clips(first: $first, criteria: {period: $period, sort: $sort}) { edges { node { <clipFields> } } pageInfo { hasNextPage } } } }`
- TopClipsFeed: `query TopClipsFeed($first: Int, $after: Cursor, $languages: [String!]) { topClips(first: $first, after: $after, languages: $languages) { edges { node { <clipFields> } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`
- Clip: `query Clip($slug: ID!) { clip(slug: $slug) { <clipFields> } }`
- ClipPlayback: `query ClipPlayback($slug: ID!) { clip(slug: $slug) { id playbackAccessToken(params: {platform: "web", playerBackend: "mediaplayer", playerType: "site"}) { signature value } videoQualities { quality frameRate sourceURL } assets { type videoQualities { quality frameRate sourceURL } } } }`
- ClipExtras: `query ClipExtras($slug: ID!) { clip(slug: $slug) { id isAutoCurated assets { type aspectRatio } } }`
- `_recapFields`: `id title state streamStartAt postedAt channel { id login displayName } clips { position clip { <clipFields> } }`; ChannelRecaps: `query ChannelRecaps($channelID: ID!, $first: Int) { recaps(filter: {channelIDs: [$channelID]}, first: $first) { edges { node { <recapFields> } } } }`; RecapById: `query RecapById($ids: [ID!]!) { recapsByIDs(ids: $ids) { <recapFields> } }`.
- ClipCommentsQuery (copy exactly, including the double spaces and the space before the comma): `query ClipCommentsQuery($slug: ID!, $first: Int = 20 , $after: Cursor) { clip(slug: $slug) { comments(first: $first, after: $after) { edges { cursor node { __typename ...ClipCommentFragment } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } } }  fragment UserBadgeFragment on Badge { setID imageUrlNormal: imageURL(size: NORMAL) imageUrlDouble: imageURL(size: DOUBLE) imageUrlQuadruple: imageURL(size: QUADRUPLE) version title }  fragment ClipCommentFragment on ClipComment { id body likeCount replyCount isHeartedByCreator isLikedByViewer isDownvotedByViewer isCollapsedByCommunity createdAt editedAt author { id login displayName profileImageURL(width: 70) } authorBadges { __typename ...UserBadgeFragment } emotes { emoteID from to } parentComment { id } }`

**`_gql(op, query, variables, {authToken})`**: POST `https://gql.twitch.tv/gql`, `Client-ID: kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko` (+ `Authorization: OAuth <authToken>` when non-empty). Any non-empty `errors` → `ClipApiException(messages joined "; ", isIntegrity: contains "integrity")` (no partial mode). `data` not a Map → `ClipApiException('Empty GQL response')`.

**Constants**: `topClipsPageSize = 20`, `recapsShelfSize = 10`, `maxClipDownloadBytes = 100 * 1024 * 1024`, `clipFilePrefix = 'onset-clip-'`; clip-CDN allowlist: exact host `d1ndex63qxojbr.cloudfront.net` or suffixes `.twitch.tv`, `.twitchcdn.net`, `.ttvnw.net`, `.jtvnw.net`; URL must be https, no userinfo (host compared lower-cased). Any other host gets no token (the clip then plays in the Twitch embed).

**Methods**
1. `getClipExtras(slug)` invalid slug → null; ClipExtras → `ClipExtras.tryParse(data.clip)`; any error → null (debug log only).
2. `getChannelRecaps(channelId)` non-numeric → `[]`; ChannelRecaps `{channelID, first: 10}` → `Recap.tryParse(edge.node)` non-null; error → `[]`.
3. `getRecap(id)`: invalid id → `RecapUnavailableException`; RecapById `{ids: [id]}`; `recapsByIDs` not a List → `ClipApiException('Unreadable recap')`; the node with `id == id` parsed (null → RecapUnavailableException); none → RecapUnavailableException.
4. `getClipComments(slug, {cursor, authToken})`: slug checked (ArgumentError); variables `{slug, first: 20, after?}`; with optional OAuth web token (so `isLikedByViewer` is the viewer's) → `ClipCommentPage.tryParse(data)` or `ClipApiException('Unreadable comments')`.
5. `getChannelClips({login, period, sort})`: variables `{login, first: 100, period: period.gqlName, sort: sort.gqlName}`. First tries the featured document; NetworkException/TimeoutException rethrow; integrity ClipApiException rethrow; other ClipApiException or ApiException → `featuredKnown = false` and retry the plain document. `user` not a Map → `NotFoundException('Channel not found')`. Each node parsed with `broadcaster: {id, login, displayName}` of the user; FormatException skipped. Returns `ClipPage(items, hasMore, featuredKnown)`.
6. `getCuratedClips({curatorId, period, sort})` (clips a user MADE): non-numeric → `NotFoundException('Not a user id')`; `user` not Map → `NotFoundException('User not found')`.
7. `getCategoryClips({gameId, period, sort})`: non-numeric → `ArgumentError('Not a category id')`; no game → `NotFoundException('Category not found')`; `clips` not Map → `ClipApiException('Unreadable clips')`; ≤100 edges; all unreadable → same error.
8. `getTopClips({cursor, language})` variables `{first: 20, after? (non-empty), languages?: [language] (non-empty)}`; `topClips` not Map → `ClipApiException('Unreadable clips')`; all unreadable → error; `cursor` = `endCursor` only when `hasNextPage == true` and non-empty → `ClipFeedPage`.
9. `getClip(slug)` → null when `clip` null (deleted); parse failure → `ClipApiException('Unreadable clip')`.
10. `getClipPlayback(slug, {portrait = false})`: no clip → `NotFoundException('Clip not found')`; token missing/value not String → `ApiException('No playback access token for this clip')`; token value JSON `authorization.forbidden == true` → `ClipForbiddenException(reason when non-empty String)`; renditions via `renditionsFrom` (empty → `ApiException('No playable rendition for this clip')`); returns `ClipPlayback(renditions, signature ('' if absent), token: value)`.
11. `renditionsFrom(clip, {portrait})`: picks the `assets[]` entry with `type == 'RECOMPOSED'` (portrait) or `'SOURCE'` (landscape) having a `videoQualities` list; its renditions; if non-empty, or portrait requested, return them (portrait never falls back); else top-level `videoQualities`. `_renditions`: `quality` parsed int > 0 (height), `sourceURL` must pass the CDN check, `frameRate` num or parseable string, finite > 0 else 30; sorted by height desc then frame rate desc; de-duplicated by `label` (first kept).
12. `signedClipUrl(sourceUrl, {signature, token})` = `signClipUrl` (lib/models/clip.dart). Never log a signed URL.
13. `downloadClip(slug, Directory folder)` (save to device): `getClipPlayback`; no renditions → `ApiException('No rendition for this clip')`; picks the first (best-first list) rendition with height ≤ 720, else the last; deletes any old `onset-clip-*.mp4` in the folder; streamed GET of the signed URL via the service Dio (`responseType: stream`); Content-Type must start with `video/` else `ApiException('Not a clip video')`; declared Content-Length > 100 MiB → `ApiException('Clip too large')`; writes `<folder>/onset-clip-<slug>.mp4`, aborting (and deleting the partial file) past 100 MiB; zero bytes → delete + `ApiException('Empty clip download')`. Returns the File.

Rust: same GQL strings as `const &str`; download via `reqwest` `bytes_stream()` into `tokio::fs::File` with the same byte cap and content-type check.

### lib/apis/twitch_viewer_actions_service.dart

All logged-in "viewer actions" that Helix does not offer, sent as GQL with the viewer's **twitch.tv web token** (`authToken`, the `auth-token` cookie captured at login — NOT the Helix OAuth token). `TwitchViewerActionsService(Dio)` base `''`, on anonymousGqlDio, so TwitchTvSessionInterceptor may reroute mutations through the TV session. Headers for every request (`_viewerHeaders`): `Client-ID: kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko`, `Authorization: OAuth <authToken>`. Mutations carry `extra: TwitchTvSessionInterceptor.userAction` (follow mutations carry `tracedUserAction(trace)`); queries carry no extra.

**Validation**: `_numericId ^[0-9]{1,20}$`, `_opaqueId ^[A-Za-z0-9_:\-]{1,128}$`, `_login ^[a-z0-9_]{1,25}$` (after lower-casing), `_badgeToken ^[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{1,64}$`. Constants: `maxResubMessageLength = 255`, `modPinSeconds = 1200` (20 min), `maxUnbanMessageLength = 500`. `_freshId()` = 16 bytes from `Random.secure()` as 32 lowercase hex chars (vote id, goal transaction id).

**Outcome model**: `enum ViewerActionOutcome { done, refused, integrityRefused, signedOut, failed }`; top-level `viewerActionOutcomeOf(body, field, succeeded?)`: body not a Map → failed; non-empty `errors` → `integrityRefused` if the joined lower-cased messages contain `integrity`, else failed; `data[field]` not a Map → failed; `payload.error` is a Map → refused; `succeeded` given and false → failed; else done. `_failedAtOnce(authToken)` (invalid ids) → signedOut when the token is empty, else failed — no request. `_send({operation, document, field, authToken, input, variables, succeeded})`: empty token → signedOut; POST gql body `{"operationName","query","variables": variables ?? {"input": input}}`; `UnauthorizedException` → signedOut; any other exception → failed (debug log `'<operation> failed: …'`).

**Documents and methods** (copy documents byte-exact; double spaces are part of them):
1. `voteInPoll({pollId, choiceId, userId, authToken})` — `PollsVoteMutation`, field `voteInPoll`, input `{"pollID","choiceID","userID","voteID": freshId}`. Document: `mutation PollsVoteMutation($input: VoteInPollInput!) { voteInPoll(input: $input) { voter { __typename ...PollVoterFragment } error { code } } }  fragment ViewablePollFragment on Poll { id title status startedAt durationSeconds choices { id title votes { total } } settings { communityPointsVotes { cost isEnabled } } topCommunityPointsContributor { communityPointsAmount user { displayName } } self { voter { choices { pollChoice { id } } votes { total } } } votes { total } }  fragment PollVoterFragment on PollVoter { id choices { pollChoice { id } } poll { __typename ...ViewablePollFragment } votes { total } }`.
2. `joinRaid({raidId, authToken})` / `leaveRaid` — `JoinRaidMutation`: `mutation JoinRaidMutation($input: ID!) { joinRaid(input: { raidID: $input } ) { raidID } }`; `LeaveRaidMutation`: `mutation LeaveRaidMutation($input: ID!) { leaveRaid(input: { raidID: $input } ) { raidID } }`; variables `{"input": raidId}`; success requires `payload.raidID == raidId`.
3. `shareWatchStreak({channelId, milestoneId, authToken})` — `ShareViewerMilestoneMutation`, field `shareViewerMilestone`, input `{"channelID","milestoneID"}`. Document: `mutation ShareViewerMilestoneMutation($input: ShareViewerMilestoneInput!) { shareViewerMilestone(input: $input) { milestone { __typename ...ViewerMilestoneFragment } error { code } } }  fragment ViewerMilestoneFragment on ViewerMilestone { id category value achievementTimestamp shareStatus }`.
4. `shareResub({channelLogin, tokenId, authToken, message})` — `UseChatNotificationToken`, field `useChatNotificationToken`, variables `{"channelLogin": lower-cased, "tokenID", "message"?: trimmed, truncated with `substring(0, 255)` — 255 UTF-16 code units, which can split a surrogate pair (emoji) — omitted when empty}`; success `isSuccess == true`. Document: `mutation UseChatNotificationToken($channelLogin: String!, $tokenID: ID!, $message: String, $selection: AlertViewerCustomizationSelectionInput) { useChatNotificationToken(input: { channelLogin: $channelLogin tokenID: $tokenID message: $message selection: $selection includeStreak: false } ) { isSuccess } }`.
5. `setLeadModerator({channelId, userId, granted, authToken})` — grant: `AssignLeadModMutation` `mutation AssignLeadModMutation($input: AssignChannelRoleInput!) { assignChannelRole(input: $input) { isAssigned error { code } } }` (field `assignChannelRole`, success `isAssigned == true`); revoke: `UnassignLeadModMutation` `mutation UnassignLeadModMutation($input: UnassignChannelRoleInput!) { unassignChannelRole(input: $input) { isUnassigned error { code } } }` (success `isUnassigned == true`); input `{"channelID","roleID":"lead_mod","targetUserID"}`.
6. `pinMessage({channelId, messageId, authToken})` — `PinChatMessage` `mutation PinChatMessage($input: PinChatMessageInput!) { pinChatMessage(input: $input) { pinnedChatMessage { id pinnedMessage { id } } error { code } } }`, input `{"channelID","durationSeconds":1200,"messageID","type":"MOD"}`, success `pinnedChatMessage` is a Map.
7. `unpinMessage({pinId, authToken})` — operation `unpinChatMessage` (lower-case u) `mutation unpinChatMessage($input: UnpinChatMessageInput!) { unpinChatMessage(input: $input) { unpinnedChatMessage { id pinnedMessage { id } } error { code } } }`, input `{"id": pinId, "reason": "UNPIN"}`, success `unpinnedChatMessage` Map.
8. `clipRefusal({broadcasterId, authToken})` → `ClipEligibilityRefusal?` — query `CanCreateClipForChannelQuery`: `query CanCreateClipForChannelQuery($broadcasterId: ID!) { canCreateClip(broadcasterID: $broadcasterId) { isAllowed errorCode requiredFollowingLengthMinutes } }`, variables `{"broadcasterId"}`; empty token/invalid id → null without request; parses `ClipEligibilityRefusal.fromGql(body.data)`; any error → null. Used by the clip button to explain why clipping is not allowed before trying Helix.
9. `acknowledgeWarning({channelId, authToken})` — `AcknowledgeChatWarning`: `mutation AcknowledgeChatWarning($input: AcknowledgeChatWarningInput!) { acknowledgeChatWarning(input: $input) { error { code } } }`, input `{"channelID"}`.
10. `selectBadge({scope, channelId, authToken, ChatBadgeChoice? badge})` (chat identity): invalid channel or badge tokens → failed-at-once. `badge == null` → deselect: channel scope `DeselectChannelBadgeMutation` `mutation DeselectChannelBadgeMutation($input: DeselectChannelBadgeInput!) { deselectChannelBadge(input: $input) { user { id } } }` input `{"channelID"}`; global scope `DeselectGlobalBadgeMutation` `mutation DeselectGlobalBadgeMutation { deselectGlobalBadge { user { id } } }` with `variables: {}`. Select: channel `SelectChannelBadgeMutation` `mutation SelectChannelBadgeMutation($input: SelectChannelBadgeInput!) { selectChannelBadge(input: $input) { isSuccessful user { id } } }`, global `SelectGlobalBadgeMutation` `mutation SelectGlobalBadgeMutation($input: SelectGlobalBadgeInput!) { selectGlobalBadge(input: $input) { isSuccessful user { id } } }`; input `{"badgeSetID","badgeSetVersion","channelID"? (channel scope only)}`; success `isSuccessful != false`.
11. `setSubFlairHidden({hidden, authToken})` — `UpdateSubFlairHiddenMutation`: `mutation UpdateSubFlairHiddenMutation($input: UpdateUserSubscriptionSettingsInput!) { updateUserSubscriptionSettings(input: $input) { subscriptionSettings { isBadgeModifierHidden } } }`, field `updateUserSubscriptionSettings`, input `{"isBadgeModifierHidden": hidden}`.
12. `createUnbanRequest({channelId, message, authToken})` → `UnbanRequestOutcome`: empty token → signedOut; invalid id, empty or trimmed message longer than 500 UTF-16 code units (Dart `.length`) → failed; `CreateUnbanRequest`: `mutation CreateUnbanRequest($input: CreateUnbanRequestInput!) { createUnbanRequest(input: $input) { unbanRequest { id } error { code } } }`, variables `{"input":{"channelID","requesterMessage": text}}`; 401 → signedOut; other → failed; else `UnbanRequestOutcome.fromBody`.
13. `contributeToGoal({channelId, goalId, amount, authToken})` → `GoalContributionResult`: empty token → signedOut; invalid ids or amount ≤ 0 → failed; `CommunityGoalMutation`: `mutation CommunityGoalMutation($input: ContributeCommunityPointsCommunityGoalInput!) { contributeCommunityPointsCommunityGoal(input: $input) { error { code } } }`, input `{"amount","channelID","goalID","transactionID": freshId}`; 401 → signedOut.
14. `claimDrop({dropInstanceId, authToken})` → `DropClaimResult`: `ClaimDropRewardsMutation`: `mutation ClaimDropRewardsMutation($dropInstanceId: ID!) { claimDropRewards(input: { dropInstanceID: $dropInstanceId } ) { dropInstanceID status dropType { id campaign { id } } isUserAccountConnected } }`, variables `{"dropInstanceId"}`.
15. `openChests({channelId, freeCount, paidCount, authToken})` (hype-train/"chests" treasure): invalid id, negative counts or both zero → signedOut/failed at once; `OpenChests`: `mutation OpenChests($input: OpenChestsInput!) { openChests(input: $input) { txnID error { code message } } }`, input `{"channelID","freeCount","paidCount","shouldOpenFtue": false}`, success `txnID` is a String.
16. `claimMoment({momentId, authToken})` → `MomentClaimResult`: empty token → signedOut; invalid id → invalid; `ClaimMomentMutation`: `mutation ClaimMomentMutation($momentID: ID!) { claimCommunityMoment(input: { momentID: $momentID } ) { moment { id } error } }`, variables `{"momentID"}`.
17. Follows → `FollowResult` via `_follow` (empty token → `noWebSession`; non-numeric id → failed; a fresh `TwitchTvTrace` passed in `extra`; result `FollowResult.fromBody(body, field, trace:)`; 401 → `tvSessionExpired` if `trace.tvSessionRejected` else `signedOut`; other error → failed):
    - `followChannel({channelId, authToken})` `FollowUserMutation` variables `{"targetId", "disableNotifications": false}`, field `followUser`: `mutation FollowUserMutation($targetId: ID!, $disableNotifications: Boolean!) { followUser(input: { targetID: $targetId disableNotifications: $disableNotifications } ) { follow { disableNotifications } error { code } } }`.
    - `unfollowChannel` `UnfollowUserMutation` `{"targetId"}`, field `unfollowUser`: `mutation UnfollowUserMutation($targetId: ID!) { unfollowUser(input: { targetID: $targetId } ) { follow { disableNotifications } } }`.
    - `followCategory({categoryId, authToken})` `FollowGameMutation` `{"gameID"}`, field `followGame`: `mutation FollowGameMutation($gameID: ID!) { followGame(input: { gameID: $gameID } ) { game { __typename ...GameModelFragment } } }  fragment TagModelFragment on Tag { id localizedName tagName isAutomated isLanguageTag localizedDescription scope }  fragment GameModelFragment on Game { id name originalReleaseDate viewersCount followersCount displayName broadcastersCount boxArtURL(width: 285, height: 380) gameTags: tags(limit: 5, tagType: CONTENT) { __typename ...TagModelFragment } coverURL(width: 1600, height: 240) }`.
    - `unfollowCategory` `UnfollowGameMutation` — identical with `unfollowGame` in place of `followGame` (operation `UnfollowGameMutation`).
18. `giftSettings({authToken})` → `GiftSettings?` — `UserGiftedSubSettingsQuery`: `query UserGiftedSubSettingsQuery { currentUser { id subscriptionSettings { giftsToFollowedChannelsOnly isGiftCountHidden } } }`, `variables: {}` → `GiftSettings.fromSettings(currentUser.subscriptionSettings)`; errors → null.
19. `updateGiftSettings({settings, authToken})` — `UserGiftedSubSettingsMutation`: `mutation UserGiftedSubSettingsMutation($input: UpdateUserSubscriptionSettingsInput!) { updateUserSubscriptionSettings(input: $input) { subscriptionSettings { giftsToFollowedChannelsOnly isGiftCountHidden } } }`, input `{"giftsToFollowedChannelsOnly","isGiftCountHidden"}`; success requires the returned settings to equal the requested ones.
20. `chatUiSettings({authToken})` → `ChatUiSettings?` — `ChatUISettingsQuery`: `query ChatUISettingsQuery { currentUser { id chatUISettings { isReadableChatColorsEnabled isEmoteAnimationsEnabled isGifsEnabled isGifAnimationsEnabled } } }`.
21. `updateChatUiSetting({setting, enabled, authToken})` — operation/document/field from the `ChatUiSetting` enum (lib/models/chat_ui_settings.dart), input `{<setting.field>: enabled}`, success `payload[field] == enabled`.
22. `gifPickerConfig({channelId, authToken})` → `GifPickerConfig?` — `GetGifPickerConfig`: `query GetGifPickerConfig($channelID: ID!) { gifPickerConfig(channelID: $channelID) { isEnabled isAllowlisted apiKey contentRating } }`.
23. `sendGif({channelId, gif, searchTerm, authToken})` → `GifSendResult`: empty token → signedOut; invalid id → failed; `sendGifMessage` (lower-case s): `mutation sendGifMessage($input: SendGifMessageInput!) { sendGifMessage(input: $input) { error secondsUntilCanSend message { id } } }`, input `{"channelID","gifID": gif.id,"gifURL": gif.url,"searchTerm"? (trimmed, non-empty)}`; 401 → signedOut; else `GifSendResult.fromBody`.

Every method returns a typed result and never throws to the UI. Rust: `ViewerActions { gql: GqlClient }` where each method takes `&WebToken`; documents as `const` strings; result enums identical.

### lib/apis/twitch_account_gql_service.dart

Account editing through GQL with the viewer's twitch.tv web token (Settings › Account / Edit profile, clip comments, clip management). `TwitchAccountGqlService(Dio)` base `''` on anonymousGqlDio. Headers: `Client-ID: kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko`, `Authorization: OAuth <authToken>`.

**Validation constants**: `_numericId ^[0-9]{1,20}$`, `_opaqueId ^[A-Za-z0-9_:\-]{1,128}$`, public `loginPattern ^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_]{2,24}$`, `isValidLogin(login)` = pattern && length ≤ 25 (so 3–25 chars, first char alphanumeric); `maxCommentLength = 500`; `maxClipTitleLength = 100`. From profile_edit: `EditableProfile.maxDescriptionLength = 300`, `SocialLink.maxTitleLength = 25`, `ProfileImageFile.maxBytes = 10 MiB`.

**Helpers**
- `_query(operation, document, authToken, {variables = {}})`: empty token → null without a request; POST gql body `{"operationName","query","variables"}` with no `extra`; body not Map or non-empty `errors` → null; else `body['data']`; any exception → null (debug log).
- `_mutate({operation, document, field, authToken, variables})` → `AccountEditResult<Map>`: empty token → signedOut; POST with `extra: TwitchTvSessionInterceptor.userAction`; 401 → signedOut; other exception → failed; else `_resultOf(body, field)`.
- `_resultOf`: body not Map → failed; non-empty errors → `integrityRefused` if text contains "integrity" else failed; `data[field]` not Map → failed; `payload.error` Map → refused with `code` (String or null); `payload.error` non-empty String → refused with that string as code; else done with `value: payload`.
- `_failedAtOnce<T>(token)` → signedOut if token empty else failed (no request).

**Shared fragment text** `_userModelFragments` (leading two spaces): `  fragment UserModelFragment on User { id login displayName primaryColorHex chatColor description profileImageURL(width: 300) bannerImageURL roles { isStaff } createdAt turboStatus { hasActiveTurbo } }  fragment CurrentUserModelFragment on User { __typename ...UserModelFragment accountHealth { isVerifiedEmail } turboStatus { hasActiveTurbo } }`.
`_socialMediasFragment`: `  fragment SocialMediasFragment on Channel { socialMedias { id name title url } }`.
`_clipCommentFragments`: `  fragment UserBadgeFragment on Badge { setID imageUrlNormal: imageURL(size: NORMAL) imageUrlDouble: imageURL(size: DOUBLE) imageUrlQuadruple: imageURL(size: QUADRUPLE) version title }  fragment ClipCommentFragment on ClipComment { id body likeCount replyCount isHeartedByCreator isLikedByViewer isDownvotedByViewer isCollapsedByCommunity createdAt editedAt author { id login displayName profileImageURL(width: 70) } authorBadges { __typename ...UserBadgeFragment } emotes { emoteID from to } parentComment { id } }`.

**Documents and methods**
1. `profile({authToken})` → `EditableProfile?`: `EditableProfile` query `query EditableProfile { currentUser { id login displayName description profileImageURL(width: 300) bannerImageURL } }` → `EditableProfile.tryParse(data.currentUser)`.
2. `renameStatus({authToken})` → `LoginRenameStatus?`: `query UserLoginRenameStatusQuery { currentUser { loginRenameStatus { isEligible eligibleAt } } }`.
3. `socialLinks({authToken})` → `List<SocialLink>?`: `query SocialLinks { currentUser { id channel { socialMedias { id name title url } } } }` → `SocialLink.listFrom(currentUser.channel)`.
4. `usernameAvailability({login, authToken})`: invalid login → `taken` without request; `query IsUsernameAvailableQuery($login: String!) { isUsernameAvailable(username: $login) }` with `{"login": lower-cased}`; bool → available/taken; anything else (incl. failures) → unknown.
5. `updateDisplayName({userId, displayName, authToken})`: trimmed name 1–25 chars and numeric id else failed-at-once; `UpdateUserDisplayNameMutation`: `mutation UpdateUserDisplayNameMutation($userID: ID!, $newDisplayName: String!) { updateUser(input: { userID: $userID displayName: $newDisplayName } ) { error { code } user { __typename ...CurrentUserModelFragment settings { preferredLanguageTag } } } }` + userModelFragments; variables `{"userID","newDisplayName"}`; field `updateUser`; result value = `EditableProfile.tryParse(payload.user)` (null → failed). (Display name may only change capitalization of the login; Twitch answers an error code otherwise.)
6. `updateDescription({userId, description, authToken})`: trimmed ≤ 300; `UpdateUserDescriptionMutation`: `mutation UpdateUserDescriptionMutation($userID: ID!, $newDescription: String!) { updateUser(input: { userID: $userID description: $newDescription } ) { error { code } user { __typename ...CurrentUserModelFragment } } }` + fragments; variables `{"userID","newDescription"}` (empty allowed = clear bio).
7. `changeUsername({userId, login, authToken})`: `ChangeUsernameMutation`: `mutation ChangeUsernameMutation($userID: ID!, $newLogin: String!) { changeUsername(input: { userID: $userID login: $newLogin } ) { error { code } user { __typename ...CurrentUserModelFragment } } }` + fragments; variables `{"userID","newLogin": lower-cased}`; field `changeUsername`.
8. `addSocialLink({channelId, title, url, authToken})` → `AccountEditResult<List<SocialLink>>`: `_validLink` = trimmed title 1–25 and `SocialLink.isValidUrl(url)`; `CreateSocialLink`: `mutation CreateSocialLink($input: CreateSocialMediaInput!) { createSocialMedia(input: $input) { channel { __typename ...SocialMediasFragment } error } }` + socialMediasFragment; input `{"channelID","title": trimmed,"url": trimmed}`; field `createSocialMedia`; value = `SocialLink.listFrom(payload.channel)` (the full new list).
9. `updateSocialLink({channelId, linkId, title, url, authToken})`: `UpdateSocialLink`: `mutation UpdateSocialLink($input: UpdateSocialMediaInput!) { updateSocialMedia(input: $input) { channel { __typename ...SocialMediasFragment } error } }` + fragment; input `{"channelID","id","title","url"}`.
10. `deleteSocialLink({channelId, linkId, authToken})`: `DeleteSocialLink`: `mutation DeleteSocialLink($input: DeleteSocialMediaInput!) { deleteSocialMedia(input: $input) { channel { __typename ...SocialMediasFragment } } }` + fragment; input `{"channelID","id"}`.
11. `commentEligibility({slug, authToken})` → `ClipCommentEligibility?`: invalid slug → null; `query ClipCommentEligibilityQuery($slug: ID!) { clip(slug: $slug) { self { commentCreationEligibility { isAllowed errorCodes requiredFollowingLengthMinutes } } broadcaster { self { follower { followedAt } } } } }`.
12. `postComment({slug, body, authToken})` → `AccountEditResult<ClipComment>`: trimmed 1–500; `CreateClipComment`: `mutation CreateClipComment($input: CreateClipCommentInput!) { createClipComment(input: $input) { comment { __typename ...ClipCommentFragment } error } }` + clipCommentFragments; variables `{"input":{"clipSlug","body"}}`; value = `ClipComment.tryParse(payload.comment)` (null → failed).
13. `setCommentLiked({slug, commentId, liked, authToken})` → `AccountEditOutcome`: `LikeClipComment` `mutation LikeClipComment($input: LikeClipCommentInput!) { likeClipComment(input: $input) { error } }` / `UnlikeClipComment` `mutation UnlikeClipComment($input: UnlikeClipCommentInput!) { unlikeClipComment(input: $input) { error } }`; input `{"clipSlug","commentID"}`.
14. `deleteClip({slug, authToken})` → outcome: `DeleteClipsMutation` `mutation DeleteClipsMutation($input: DeleteClipsInput!) { deleteClips(input: $input) { clips { slug } } }`, input `{"slugs":[slug]}`; done only if returned `clips` contains that slug, else refused.
15. `renameClip({slug, title, authToken})` → `AccountEditResult<String>`: trimmed 1–100; `UpdateClipMutation` `mutation UpdateClipMutation($input: UpdateClipInput!) { updateClip(input: $input) { clip { slug title } error { message } } }`, input `{"slug","title"}`; value = saved `clip.title` (non-String → failed). Note: `error { message }` (a Map) makes `_resultOf` return refused with code null.
16. `uploadProfileImage({userId, kind, file, authToken})` → outcome: numeric id and `file.isAcceptable` (1 byte–10 MiB) required; avatar: `CreateProfileImageUploadURL` `mutation CreateProfileImageUploadURL($input: CreateProfileImageUploadURLInput!) { createProfileImageUploadURL(input: $input) { uploadURL uploadID error { code } } }`; banner: `CreateProfileBannerImageUploadURL` `mutation CreateProfileBannerImageUploadURL($input: CreateProfileBannerImageUploadURLInput!) { createProfileBannerImageUploadURL(input: $input) { uploadURL uploadID error { code } } }`; input `{"userID","format": "PNG"|"JPG"|"GIF"}`. Then `uploadURL` must parse as https with a host (else failed); PUT the raw bytes to it with headers `Content-Type: <image/png|image/jpeg|image/gif>` and `Content-Length: <bytes>` (through BaseApiClient on the same Dio, no auth headers since not Twitch-Helix); success → done, any error → failed.

Screens: Settings › Account (edit display name, description, username with availability check and rename-eligibility date, social links ≤5, avatar/banner upload), clip comments sheet, "My clips" manage (rename/delete). Rust: `AccountGql` with identical documents; image upload with `reqwest::Client::put(url).header(CONTENT_TYPE, mime).body(bytes)`.

### lib/apis/twitch_social_service.dart

Whisper history, whisper privacy, on-site (bell) notifications, notification settings and channel recommendations through GQL with the viewer's web token. `TwitchSocialService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio. Also declares `class OnsiteNotificationPage { List<OnsiteNotification> notifications; String? cursor }`.

**Validation/constants**: `_numericId ^[0-9]{1,20}$`, `_threadId ^[0-9]{1,20}_[0-9]{1,20}$` (Twitch thread ids are `<lowerId>_<higherId>`), `_opaqueId ^[A-Za-z0-9_:.\-]{1,256}$` (notification ids and cursors), `_login ^[a-z0-9_]{1,25}$`; `threadsPage = 30`, `messagesPage = 50`, `notificationsPage = 30`, `maxRecommendations = 12`.

**Headers** `_headers(authToken)`: `Client-ID: kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko` plus `Authorization: OAuth <token>` only when a non-empty token is given (recommendations work anonymously).
**`_query(op, doc, variables, {authToken})`**: if a token is passed but empty → null (no request); null token → anonymous request. Body not Map / non-empty errors / data not Map → null; exceptions → null.
**`_send(op, doc, field, variables, {authToken, succeeded})`**: empty token → signedOut; POST with `extra: TwitchTvSessionInterceptor.userAction`; result `viewerActionOutcomeOf(body, field, succeeded)`; 401 → signedOut; other → failed.

**Fragments** (exact):
- `fragment WhisperMessageFragment on WhisperMessage { id from { login displayName id chatColor displayBadges { setID version } } sentAt content { content emotes { emoteID from id setID to } } }`
- `fragment WhisperThreadFragment on WhisperThread { id participants { login displayName id chatColor } messages(first: 1) { edges { node { __typename ...WhisperMessageFragment } } } unreadMessagesCount isArchived isMuted }`

**Documents and methods**
1. `whisperThreads({me, authToken})` → `List<WhisperHistoryThread>?`: `me` numeric else null; `UserWhisperThreadsQuery` `{limit: 30}`: `query UserWhisperThreadsQuery($limit: Int!, $cursor: Cursor) { currentUser { whisperThreads(first: $limit, after: $cursor) { edges { cursor node { __typename ...WhisperThreadFragment } } pageInfo { hasNextPage hasPreviousPage } totalCount totalUnreadCount } } }  <WhisperMessageFragment>  <WhisperThreadFragment>` (two spaces between parts). `edges` not a List → null; each `WhisperHistoryThread.fromJson(edge.node, me: me)` non-null. First page only (no cursor paging).
2. `whisperMessages({threadId, authToken})` → `List<WhisperHistoryMessage>?`: thread id pattern else null; `WhisperMessageQuery` `{thread: threadId, numMessages: 50}`: `query WhisperMessageQuery($thread: ID, $cursor: Cursor, $numMessages: Int) { whisperThread(id: $thread) { messages(first: $numMessages, after: $cursor) { edges { node { __typename ...WhisperMessageFragment } cursor } pageInfo { hasNextPage } } lastMessage { __typename ...WhisperMessageFragment } } }  <WhisperMessageFragment>`; parsed messages sorted ascending by `sentAt` (oldest first).
3. `whisperPrivacy({authToken})` → `WhisperPrivacy?`: `query WhisperSettingsGetterQuery { currentUser { login whisperSettings { isWhisperBanned isBlockingWhispersFromStrangers isWhispersDisabledByParent } } }`, variables `{}`.
4. `setBlockStrangers({block, authToken})`: `UpdateWhisperSettings` `mutation UpdateWhisperSettings($input: UpdateWhisperSettingsInput!) { updateWhisperSettings(input: $input) { user { login whisperSettings { isWhisperBanned isBlockingWhispersFromStrangers } } } }`, variables `{"input":{"isBlockingWhispersFromStrangers": block}}`; success when the returned `user.whisperSettings` parse to `blockStrangers == block`.
5. `removeWhisperAllowance({threadId, authToken})`: invalid id → failed (no request, even if token empty); `RemoveWhisperThreadWhitelistMutation` `mutation RemoveWhisperThreadWhitelistMutation($threadID: ID!) { updateWhisperThread(input: { removeWhitelist: true threadID: $threadID } ) { thread { id } } }`, variables `{"threadID"}`, field `updateWhisperThread`, success `thread.id == threadId`. (Revokes a stranger's permission obtained by replying.)
6. `notifications({userId, authToken, language, cursor})` → `OnsiteNotificationPage?`: numeric user, cursor (if any) matching opaque pattern; variables `{"user","limit":30,"language"? (_languageOf),"cursor"?}`; `OnsiteNotificationsForUserQuery`: `query OnsiteNotificationsForUserQuery($user: ID, $limit: Int, $cursor: Cursor, $language: String, $capabilities: [OnsiteNotificationsCapability!]) { user(id: $user) { notifications(first: $limit, after: $cursor, language: $language, capabilities: $capabilities) { edges { cursor node { __typename ...OnsiteNotificationFragment } } } } }  fragment OnsiteNotificationFragment on OnsiteNotification { actions { body type url } body category createdAt creators { profileImageURL(width: 70) id displayName } destinationType displayType extra { __typename ... on Clip { slug } ... on Video { id offsetSeconds title } ... on VideoComment { id contentOffsetSeconds video { id } } ... on ClipComment { id clip { slug } parentComment { id } } ... on User { login id } ... on Game { id } ... on OnsiteNotificationExternalLink { url } ... on OnsiteNotificationGuestStar { channelID channelName } ... on Story { id creator { id } } ... on TwitchProvidedBackground { assetkey } ... on StoryClipBlock { clip { id } clipAsset { id } } ... on DropCampaign { id } ... on TimeBasedDrop { id } ... on EventBasedDrop { id } ... on ManualTriggerBasedDrop { id } ... on Subfeed { id feedLocation } ... on OnsiteNotificationRecap { id clip { slug } } } id isRead thumbnailURL type updatedAt }`. Walks `edges`, remembering the last valid edge cursor; next-page cursor = that cursor only when the page had ≥ 30 edges, else null.
   `_languageOf(code)`: first part of `code` split on `-`/`_`, lower-cased, must match `^[a-z]{2,3}$` else omitted (e.g. `fr_FR` → `fr`).
7. `unseenNotifications({userId, authToken})` → `int?` (non-numeric userId → null, no request): `OnsiteNotificationsSummaryQuery` `query OnsiteNotificationsSummaryQuery($user: ID, $capabilities: [OnsiteNotificationsCapability!]) { user(id: $user) { notifications(capabilities: $capabilities) { summary { lastSeenAt unseenCount safetyUnreadSummary { unreadCount } } } } }` → `unseenCount` int ≥ 0 else null. Drives the bell badge.
8. `markNotificationsRead({ids, authToken})`: empty list or any id not opaque → failed; `ReadOnsiteNotificationMutation` `mutation ReadOnsiteNotificationMutation($input: ReadNotificationsInput!) { readNotifications(input: $input) { count } }`, variables `{"input":{"ids": ids}}`, success `count` is an int.
9. `notificationSettings({userId, authToken})` → `List<NotificationCategorySetting>?` (non-numeric userId → null, no request): `UserNotificationSettingsQuery` `query UserNotificationSettingsQuery($user: ID) { user(id: $user) { notificationSettings { category platforms { platformName settingState isEnabled } } } }`; parsed list, empty → null. (Read-only display.)
10. `recommendations({section, channelLogin, authToken})` → `List<RecommendedChannel>?`: login lower-cased must match `_login` (else null); `PersonalSections` with variables `{"input":{"sectionInputs":[section.apiValue],"contextChannelName"?: login}}`: `query PersonalSections($input: PersonalSectionInput!) { personalSections(input: $input) { type title { localizedFallback } items { __typename ... on PersonalSectionChannel { user { id login displayName profileImageURL(width: 70) } content { __typename ... on Stream { id broadcaster { id broadcastSettings { id title } } codec isDualFormat viewersCount game { displayName } } } } } } }`. Flattens all sections' items, skipping the context channel itself and duplicate user ids, stopping at 12. Token optional (anonymous recommendations work).
11. `notInterested({channelId, reason, authToken})`: non-numeric `channelId` (`^[0-9]{1,20}$`) → `failed` with no request (checked before the token); `AddRecommendationFeedbackMutation` `mutation AddRecommendationFeedbackMutation($input: AddRecommendationFeedbackInput!) { addRecommendationFeedback(input: $input) { recommendationFeedback { id } } }`, input `{"category": reason.apiValue ("NOT_INTERESTED"|"OFFENSIVE"|"OTHER"),"itemID": channelId,"itemType":"CHANNEL","sourceItemPage":"","sourceItemRequestID":"","sourceItemTrackingID":""}`, success `recommendationFeedback` is a Map.

Screens: Whispers inbox (thread list with unread counts, conversation history merged with live EventSub whispers), whisper privacy toggle in Settings, Activity/notifications screen (Twitch bell list, unread badge, mark read), Home "Recommended" and channel "Similar channels" shelves with "Not interested" menu.

### lib/apis/twitch_community_service.dart

Read-only community data for the channel screen (shoutout cards, leaderboards, resub sharing, drops, community goals, weekly visit rewards, unlockable emotes, legendary emote season, costreams / Guest Star, emote card bits threshold, chat identity, last unban request). `TwitchCommunityService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio. Never throws: every call goes through `_ask(operation, query, variables, {authToken})` which POSTs `https://gql.twitch.tv/gql` with `Client-ID: kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko` (+ `Authorization: OAuth <token>` when non-empty) and body `{"operationName","query","variables"}`, returning the raw decoded body, or null on any exception (debug log `'<operation> unavailable: …'`). Parsing (and error detection) is done by each model's `tryParse/fromBody/listFrom`. Validation: `_numericId ^[0-9]{1,20}$`, `_login ^[a-z0-9_]{1,25}$` (applied after lower-casing).

**Documents and methods**
1. `getShoutoutProfile({channelLogin, targetLogin, authToken})` → `ShoutoutProfile?`: logins lower-cased and validated. `ShoutoutQuery` variables `{channelLogin, targetLogin}`: `query ShoutoutQuery($channelLogin: String!, $targetLogin: String!) { channel(name: $channelLogin) { id name } user(login: $targetLogin) { id displayName profileImageURL(width: 300) primaryColorHex channel { id name home { shelves { categoryShelf(first: 3) { edges { node { id boxArtURL(width: 285, height: 380) displayName } } } } } schedule { id nextSegment { id categories { id displayName } hasReminder startAt endAt isCancelled title } } } } }` → `ShoutoutProfile.tryParse`. If parsed and a token exists, second call `ShoutoutHighlightStatusQuery` `{targetLogin}` with the token: `query ShoutoutHighlightStatusQuery($targetLogin: String!) { user(login: $targetLogin) { id self { follower { followedAt } canFollow banStatus { createdAt } } } }` → `profile.withFollowing(ShoutoutProfile.followingIn(body))`.
2. `getLeaderboards({channelId, authToken})` → `ChannelLeaderboards?`: `ChannelLeaderboardsQuery` `{channelId, fetchChannelSettings: true}` (token optional; with it `myPosition` is the viewer's): `query ChannelLeaderboardsQuery($channelId: ID!, $fetchChannelSettings: Boolean!) { user(id: $channelId) { settings @include(if: $fetchChannelSettings) { leaderboard { defaultLeaderboard isCheerEnabled isSubGiftEnabled isClipEnabled timePeriod } } channel { leaderboardSet { bits { __typename ...UserLeaderboardFragment } subGift { __typename ...UserLeaderboardFragment } clip { __typename ...ClipLeaderboardFragment } } } } }  fragment UserLeaderboardItemFragment on UserLeaderboardItem { rank score user { id displayName } }  fragment UserLeaderboardFragment on UserLeaderboard { id items { edges { node { __typename ...UserLeaderboardItemFragment } } } myPosition { __typename ...UserLeaderboardItemFragment } }  fragment LeaderboardClipAssetMetadataFragment on ClipAsset { thumbnailURL(height: 45) curator { id displayName } }  fragment LeaderboardClipMetadataFragment on Clip { slug title assets { __typename ...LeaderboardClipAssetMetadataFragment } }  fragment ClipLeaderboardItemFragment on ClipLeaderboardItem { rank score clip { __typename ...LeaderboardClipMetadataFragment } }  fragment ClipLeaderboardFragment on ClipLeaderboard { id items { edges { node { __typename ...ClipLeaderboardItemFragment } } } myPosition { __typename ...ClipLeaderboardItemFragment } }`.
3. `getResubNotification({channelId, authToken})` → `ResubNotification?` (token required): `query ChannelResubNotificationQuery($channelId: ID!) { user(id: $channelId) { login self { resubNotification { id months token isGiftSubscription gifter { login } } } } }`. The `token` is later spent by `TwitchViewerActionsService.shareResub`.
4. `getDropCampaigns({channelId, authToken})` → `List<DropCampaign>?`: same operationName `ViewerDropsCampaignsQuery` for both variants, variables `{channelID}`. Signed-in document: `query ViewerDropsCampaignsQuery($channelID: ID!) { user(id: $channelID) { id channel { viewerDropCampaigns { __typename ...DropCampaignModelFragment timeBasedDrops { __typename ...TimeBasedDropModelFragment ...TimeBasedDropSelfEdgeFragment } } } } }` + `  fragment DropCampaignModelFragment on DropCampaign { id name game { id name boxArtURL(width: 285, height: 380) } owner { name } imageURL accountLinkURL detailsURL startAt endAt status self { isAccountConnected } summary { isPermanentlyDismissible } }  fragment DropBenefitEdgeFragment on DropBenefitEdge { benefit { id game { name } imageAssetURL name entitlementLimit distributionType } entitlementLimit }  fragment TimeBasedDropModelFragment on TimeBasedDrop { id name requiredMinutesWatched requiredSubs requiredTurboSubs startAt endAt preconditionDrops { id } benefitEdges { __typename ...DropBenefitEdgeFragment } }  fragment TimeBasedDropSelfEdgeFragment on TimeBasedDrop { self { currentMinutesWatched currentSubs currentTurboSubs hasPreconditionsMet isClaimed dropInstanceID } }`. Anonymous document: `query ViewerDropsCampaignsQuery($channelID: ID!) { user(id: $channelID) { id channel { viewerDropCampaigns { __typename ...DropCampaignModelFragment timeBasedDrops { __typename ...TimeBasedDropModelFragment } } } } }  fragment DropCampaignModelFragment on DropCampaign { id name game { id name boxArtURL(width: 285, height: 380) } owner { name } imageURL accountLinkURL detailsURL startAt endAt status summary { isPermanentlyDismissible } }  fragment DropBenefitEdgeFragment on DropBenefitEdge { benefit { id game { name } imageAssetURL name entitlementLimit distributionType } entitlementLimit }  fragment TimeBasedDropModelFragment on TimeBasedDrop { id name requiredMinutesWatched requiredSubs requiredTurboSubs startAt endAt preconditionDrops { id } benefitEdges { __typename ...DropBenefitEdgeFragment } }` (no `self` selections). Parsed by `DropCampaign.listFrom`.
5. `getCommunityGoals({channelId, authToken})` → `List<CommunityGoal>?`: anonymous `CommunityGoalQuery` `{id}`: `query CommunityGoalQuery($id: ID!) { user(id: $id) { channel { communityPointsSettings { goals { __typename ...CommunityGoalItemFragment } } } } }  fragment CommunityPointsImageFragment on CommunityPointsImage { url url2x url4x }  fragment CommunityGoalItemFragment on CommunityPointsCommunityGoal { id status backgroundColor durationDays title description image { __typename ...CommunityPointsImageFragment } defaultImage { __typename ...CommunityPointsImageFragment } amountNeeded pointsContributed smallContribution perStreamUserMaximumContribution isInStock startedAt endedAt type }`. If goals non-empty and a token exists: `CommunityGoalUserContributionsQuery` `{id}` with token: `query CommunityGoalUserContributionsQuery($id: ID!) { user(id: $id) { channel { self { communityPoints { goalContributions { goal { id } userPointsContributedThisStream } } } } } }` → each goal `.withContributedThisStream(mine[goal.id] ?? 0)`.
6. `getWeeklyRewards({channelId, authToken})` (token required) → `WeeklyRewards?`: `query WeeklyVisitRewardsQuery($channelID: ID!) { channel(id: $channelID) { id self { id weeklyVisitRewards { daysVisitedThisWeek accumulatedWeeks hasEarnedWeeklyRewardThisWeek hasVisitedToday currentReward { tier channelPoints badge { id image4x: imageURL(size: QUADRUPLE) } } eventConfig { id daysRequiredPerWeek endDate weekResetDates rewardTiers { tier channelPoints badge { id image4x: imageURL(size: QUADRUPLE) } } } } } } }`.
7. `getUnlockableEmotes({channelId})` → `List<UnlockableEmote>?` (anonymous): `query OnsetUnlockableEmotes($channelID: ID!) { user(id: $channelID) { id subscriptionProducts { id tier emotes { id token } } } }`.
8. `getLegendaryCollection({channelId, authToken})` → `LegendaryCollection?`: `query LegendaryEmoteCollection($channelID: ID) { legendaryEmoteSeason(channelID: $channelID) { id campaign { id name imageURL } emotePool { tier isOwned emote { id token } } userProgress { chestInventory { chestType unopenedCount } } } }`.
9. `getCostreamers({channelLogin})` → `LiveTogether?`: `query CostreamersRoster($login: String!) { user(login: $login) { id stream { id costreamDetails { role costreamersCount totalViewersCount organizer { id login displayName profileImageURL(width: 70) stream { id viewersCount } } topCostreamers { id login displayName profileImageURL(width: 70) stream { id viewersCount } } } } } }` → `LiveTogether.fromCostream`.
10. `getGuestStarSession({channelId})` → `LiveTogether?`: `query GuestStarSessionViewerQuery($channelID: ID!) { channel(id: $channelID) { owner { __typename ...GuestStarUserWithFollowDetailsFragment } guestStarSessionCall { id guests { user { __typename ...GuestStarUserWithFollowDetailsFragment } } } } }  fragment GuestStarUserWithFollowDetailsFragment on User { id login displayName profileImageURL(width: 70) primaryColorHex self { canFollow follower { followedAt } } stream { id viewersCount } }` → `LiveTogether.fromGuestStar`.
11. `getEmoteBitsThreshold({emoteId})` → `int?`: emote id `^[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,64}$`; `EmoteCard` `{emoteId}`: `query EmoteCard($emoteId: ID!, $includeIsParticipatingDJ: Boolean = false ) { emote(id: $emoteId) { id type setID token assetType subscriptionSummaries { id tier emotes { id token assetType } } owner { __typename ...ChannelModelWithoutStreamModelFragment stream { type } } bitsBadgeTierSummary { threshold self { isUnlocked numberOfBitsUntilUnlock } } } user { hasPrime } }  fragment ChannelModelWithoutStreamModelFragment on User { channelId: id followers { totalCount } description login displayName profileImageURL(width: 300) bannerImageURL roles { isPartner isAffiliate isMonetized isParticipatingDJ @include(if: $includeIsParticipatingDJ) } lastBroadcast { startedAt } broadcastSettings { isMature title } }` → `bitsThresholdFrom(body)` (emote-detail sheet: "Unlock with N bits").
12. `getChatIdentity({channelId, authToken})` (token required) → `ChatIdentity?`: `query ChatIdentityQuery($channelID: ID!) { user { id login displayName chatColor availableBadges { __typename ...UserBadgeFragment } selectedBadge { __typename ...UserBadgeFragment } subscriptionSettings { isBadgeModifierHidden } } channel: user(id: $channelID) { id displayName self { displayBadges { __typename ...UserBadgeFragment } availableBadges { __typename ...UserBadgeFragment } availableChannelAuthorityBadges { __typename ...UserBadgeFragment } selectedBadge { __typename ...UserBadgeFragment } selectedChannelAuthorityBadge { __typename ...UserBadgeFragment } isFounder isModerator isVIP subscriptionBenefit { tier } subscriptionTenure(tenureMethod: CUMULATIVE) { months } } } channelFlair: channel(id: $channelID) { creatorBadgeFlair { setting } } }  fragment UserBadgeFragment on Badge { setID imageUrlNormal: imageURL(size: NORMAL) imageUrlDouble: imageURL(size: DOUBLE) imageUrlQuadruple: imageURL(size: QUADRUPLE) version title }` (note `user` without arguments = current user).
13. `getUnbanRequest({channelId, authToken})` (token required) → `UnbanRequestState?`: `query LastUnbanRequest($channelID: ID!) { channel(id: $channelID) { id self { id canRequestUnban lastUnbanRequest { id status createdAt resolverMessage } } unbanRequestsSettings { cooldownMinutes isEnabled } } }`.

Methods that need a token return null without a request when it is empty. Rust: `CommunityService::ask(&self, op, query, vars, token: Option<&str>) -> Option<serde_json::Value>`.

### lib/apis/twitch_points_service.dart

Channel points (balance, rewards, bonus chest), predictions (viewer side) and emote-unlock rewards through GQL. Helix offers none of this to viewers. `TwitchPointsService(Dio)` base `''`; in the app it is created with `TwitchPointsService.withDefaults(interceptors: [twitchTvInterceptor])` = its OWN `Dio(defaultOptions)` where `defaultOptions` = `BaseOptions(connectTimeout: 5 s, receiveTimeout: 10 s)` (no default headers) plus the TV-session interceptor — a hung mutation would keep the sheet's controls disabled, so it must end.

**Context query** (sent with NO `operationName`, a Dart `'''` multi-line string — keep newlines and two-space indentation exactly; it starts directly with `query` (no leading newline) and ENDS with a newline after the final `}`):
```
query OnsetCommunityPoints($login: String!) {
  channel(name: $login) {
    id
    self {
      communityPoints {
        balance
        availableClaim { id }
      }
    }
    communityPointsSettings {
      name
      image { url }
      automaticRewards {
        id
        type
        cost
        isEnabled
        defaultImage { url }
      }
      customRewards {
        id
        title
        cost
        isEnabled
        isUserInputRequired
        backgroundColor
        image { url }
      }
    }
    activePredictionEvents {
      id
      title
      status
      createdBy {
        ... on User {
          id
          displayName
        }
      }
      outcomes {
        id
        title
        color
        totalPoints
        totalUsers
      }
    }
  }
}
```
(Field `image { url }` was confirmed live; `url_1x` is rejected by GQL.)

**Methods**
1. `fetchContext({userLogin, authToken})` → `CommunityPointsContext`, never throws: POST `https://gql.twitch.tv/gql` headers `Client-ID: kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko` (+ `Authorization: OAuth <web token>` when non-empty), body `{"query": contextQuery, "variables": {"login": userLogin}}`; any error → `CommunityPointsContext.empty` (debug log `'Community points unavailable for <login>: …'`). Signed out → balance null, rewards/predictions still shown.
2. `parseContext(data)` (visible for tests) / `_parseContext`: `data.data.channel` Map else empty. `self.communityPoints` → `balance` (num→int), `availableClaim.id`; `communityPointsSettings.name` → pointsName, `.image.url` → pointsImageUrl. Rewards = `automaticRewards` parsed by `PointsReward.tryParseAutomatic` + `customRewards` parsed by `PointsReward.tryParse`, then filtered to `isEnabled`, then sorted ascending by cost (official order). Predictions = `activePredictionEvents` parsed by `Prediction.tryParse`.
3. `redeemReward({channelId, reward, authToken, userInput})` → `String?` error code (null = success): `_mutate(operation: 'redeemCommunityPointsCustomReward', inputType: 'RedeemCommunityPointsCustomRewardInput!', input: {"channelID","cost": reward.cost,"rewardID": reward.id,"title": reward.title,"transactionID": fresh,"prompt"?: userInput})`.
4. `claimBonus({channelId, claimId, authToken})`: `claimCommunityPoints`, `ClaimCommunityPointsInput!`, input `{"channelID","claimID"}`.
5. `makePrediction({eventId, outcomeId, points, authToken})`: `makePrediction`, `MakePredictionInput!`, input `{"eventID","outcomeID","points","transactionID"}`.
6. `fetchPredictionAccess({eventId, authToken})` → `PredictionAccess?`: empty token or id → null; POST with operationName `OnsetPredictionAccess`, query `query OnsetPredictionAccess($eventID: ID!) { currentUser { id predictionsSettings { hasAcceptedTOS isTemporaryChatBadgeEnabled } } predictionEvent(id: $eventID) { id self { restriction } } }`, variables `{"eventID"}` → `PredictionAccess.fromBody`; errors → null.
7. `acceptPredictionTerms({temporaryBadgeEnabled, authToken})`: operationName `AcceptPredictionTerms`, document `mutation AcceptPredictionTerms($input: UpdateUserPredictionSettingsInput!) { updateUserPredictionSettings(input: $input) { error { code } settings { hasAcceptedTOS isTemporaryChatBadgeEnabled } } }`, payload field `updateUserPredictionSettings`, input `{"hasAcceptedTOS": true, "isTemporaryChatBadgeEnabled": temporaryBadgeEnabled}`.
8. `unlockChosenEmote({channelId, cost, emoteId, modified, authToken})`: modified → operationName `UnlockModifiedEmote`, document `mutation UnlockModifiedEmote($input: UnlockChosenModifiedSubscriberEmoteInput!) { unlockChosenModifiedSubscriberEmote(input: $input) { balance error { code } } }`, field `unlockChosenModifiedSubscriberEmote`; else operationName `UnlockChosenSubscriberEmote`, document `mutation UnlockChosenSubscriberEmote($input: UnlockChosenSubscriberEmoteInput!) { unlockChosenSubscriberEmote(input: $input) { balance error { code } } }`, field `unlockChosenSubscriberEmote`; input `{"channelID","cost","emoteID","transactionID"}`.

**`_mutate({operation, inputType, input, authToken, document, operationName})`**: POST gql with `extra: TwitchTvSessionInterceptor.userAction`, headers web Client-ID + `Authorization: OAuth <token>`, body `{"operationName"?: operationName (omitted when null), "query": document ?? 'mutation Onset($input: <inputType>) { <operation>(input: $input) { error { code } } }', "variables": {"input": input}}`. Note the default document is `mutation Onset(...)` with the input type INCLUDING its `!` (from inputType) and no explicit operationName in the body. Result: non-empty `errors` → first error's `message` string (or `'unknown_error'` when that Map has no message); if the first error is NOT a Map the cast `(errors.first as JsonMap?)` throws and the catch-all returns `'request_failed'`; `data[operation]` not a Map → `'unknown_error'` (a missing payload says nothing about whether points were spent); `payload.error` Map → its `code` string or `'unknown_error'`; else null. `UnauthorizedException` → `'unauthorized'`; any other exception → `'request_failed'`. The caller converts via `PointsActionResult.fromError(code)`.

**`_transactionId()`** = `DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch` in hex + `identityHashCode(Object())` in hex (caller-generated idempotency id so a retry cannot charge twice). Rust: hex of micros + a random u32.

Screens: the channel-points chip/sheet under the chat (balance with the streamer's points name and icon, bonus-chest claim button, rewards list with costs and input prompt, emote-unlock rewards with emote picker), and the prediction card/sheet (odds, outcomes, bet amount, terms acceptance, region restriction spectator mode).

### lib/apis/twitch_directory_service.dart

Live streams of a category or a tag in any sort order, plus search-box suggestions, from anonymous GQL (`Client-ID: kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko`, no token). `TwitchDirectoryService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio. Only the first answer is available (cursor paging integrity-gated, verified 2026-10-13), so results carry no cursor (`StreamsTwitch(streams, const {})` — empty pagination map → the list UI shows no "load more").

- `DirectoryApiException(message)` (`toString` `'DirectoryApiException: $message'`).
- `enum StreamSort { viewersHigh('VIEWER_COUNT'), recommended('RELEVANCE'), viewersLow('VIEWER_COUNT_ASC'), recent('RECENT') }` (`gql`; labels in lib/l10n/enum_labels.dart). `viewersHigh` is Helix's own order — the only one that pages (the category screen uses Helix `getStreamsUnderCategory` for it and this service for the others).
- Constants: `categoryPageSize = 100`, `tagPageSize = 30` (larger `first` values are refused), `_numericId ^[0-9]{1,20}$`, `_login ^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$`, public `tagPattern ^[\p{L}\p{N}]{1,25}$` (unicode), `maxSuggestions = 6`.
- `_streamFields` (verified 2026-10-13): `id title viewersCount createdAt type language freeformTags { name } broadcaster { id login displayName } game { id name displayName }`.
- `DirectoryStreams`: `query DirectoryStreams($id: ID!, $first: Int, $options: GameStreamOptions) { game(id: $id) { id streams(first: $first, options: $options) { edges { node { <streamFields> } } } } }`.
- `TagStreams`: `query TagStreams($first: Int, $options: StreamOptions) { streams(first: $first, options: $options) { edges { node { <streamFields> } } } }`.
- `SearchSuggestions`: `query SearchSuggestions($queryFragment: String!, $requestID: ID!) { searchSuggestions(queryFragment: $queryFragment, requestID: $requestID, withOfflineChannelContent: true) { edges { node { id text content { __typename ... on SearchSuggestionChannel { id user { id login displayName } } ... on SearchSuggestionCategory { id game { id name displayName } } } } } } }`.

**`_gql(op, query, variables)`** → record `(data, errors)`: POST gql; `errors` = up to the first 3 error messages, each cut to 200 UTF-16 units by `chString`, joined `"; "` ('' when none — these are shown to the viewer); `data` not a Map → throws `DirectoryApiException(errors or 'Malformed GQL response')`.
**`_options(sort, language)`** = `{"sort": sort.gql, "broadcasterLanguages": [LANG upper-case]}` — the languages key only when `language` matches `^[a-z]{2}$` (`''` = all; `'other'` is not sent but filtered client side).

**Methods**
1. `getCategoryStreams({gameId, sort, language = ''})`: non-numeric → `ArgumentError('Not a category id')`; variables `{id, first: 100, options}`; `game` not a Map → `DirectoryApiException(errors)` when errors exist, else `NotFoundException('Category not found', null, ApiErrorKind.categoryNotFound)`; else `_streams(game.streams, errors, language)`.
2. `getTagStreams({tag, sort, language = ''})`: tag not matching `tagPattern` → `ArgumentError('Not a Twitch tag')`; variables `{first: 30, options: {..._options, "freeformTags": [tag]}}`; `_streams(data.streams, …)`.
3. `_streams(connection, errors, language)`: connection not a Map → `DirectoryApiException(errors or 'Unreadable streams')`; edges = `chList(edges, max: 100)`; nodes → `streamFromGql`; none readable while edges or errors exist → same exception; partial loss logged `'Directory: N unreadable streams skipped <errors>'`; when `language == 'other'` keep only streams whose language is `other`.
4. `streamFromGql(node)` (public static, reused by others): node Map with `type == 'live'` (reruns/premieres dropped); broadcaster `id` numeric, `login` valid, `createdAt` via `chDate`; builds a Helix-shaped `StreamTwitch(userId, login, displayName (≤64) ?? login, gameId (numeric else ''), game.displayName ?? game.name (≤100) ?? '', title (≤300) ?? '', viewersCount clamped 0…2^31, startedAt ISO-8601 UTC, thumbnail 'https://static-cdn.jtvnw.net/previews-ttv/live_user_<login lower-case>-{width}x{height}.jpg' (built from the login, not trusted), tags: up to 10 freeformTags names ≤25, language: lower-cased ≤5 kept only if `^([a-z]{2}|other)$` else '')`. `id` stays `''`.
5. `searchSuggestions(fragment)`: trimmed empty → `[]` without request; variables `{queryFragment: first 100 chars, requestID: 'onset-' + (query.hashCode as unsigned 32-bit)}` (PC: any stable per-query id string with that prefix); `searchSuggestions` not a Map → `DirectoryApiException(errors or 'No search suggestions')`; up to 6 edges → `SearchSuggestion.fromGql(node)`.

**`enum SearchSuggestionKind { channel, category, text }`; `SearchSuggestion { String text; SearchSuggestionKind kind = text; String? login }`** (immutable). `fromGql(node)`: `text` trimmed non-empty (≤100) required; content `__typename == 'SearchSuggestionChannel'` with valid `user.login` → channel suggestion, text = displayName (≤64) ?? login, login lower-cased; `SearchSuggestionCategory` → category suggestion, text = game displayName ?? name ?? text; else plain text. Equality/hash: case-insensitive text + kind.

Screens: Category screen (sort chips: viewers high/low, recommended, recent; language filter), Tag screen (streams with a tag, from tapping a tag chip), Search screen suggestion dropdown.

### lib/apis/twitch_channel_profile_service.dart

Channel profile page data from anonymous GQL (web Client-ID, no token). `TwitchChannelProfileService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio. `ChannelProfileApiException(message)` (`toString` `'ChannelProfileApiException: $message'`). Login regex `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$`, channel id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, restricted statuses `{'RESTRICTED','SUSPENDED','TEMPORARILY_RESTRICTED'}`.

Documents (verified anonymously 2026-10-13; stream `createdAt`/`game` on 2026-10-29):
- `ChannelProfile`: `query ChannelProfile($login: String!) { user(login: $login) { id login displayName description createdAt profileImageURL(width: 300) bannerImageURL offlineImageURL primaryColorHex roles { isPartner isAffiliate } followers { totalCount } channel { id socialMedias { name title url } } panels { id type ... on DefaultPanel { title imageURL linkURL description altText } } lastBroadcast { id title startedAt game { id displayName } } stream { id type viewersCount createdAt game { displayName } } } }`
- `ChannelSchedule` (`segments` takes no `first`; answers this week and next): `query ChannelSchedule($login: String!) { user(login: $login) { id channel { id schedule { id segments { id startAt endAt title isCancelled categories { id name } } nextSegment { id startAt } } } } }`
- `ChannelTrailer`: `query ChannelTrailer($login: String!) { user(login: $login) { id channel { id trailer { video { id title lengthSeconds previewThumbnailURL(width: 640, height: 360) } } } } }`
- `ChannelProfileRestriction`: `query ChannelProfileRestriction($channelID: ID!) { user(id: $channelID) { id channelSafetyStatus } }`
- `ChannelDjRole`: `query ChannelDjRole($login: String!) { user(login: $login) { id roles { isParticipatingDJ } } }`

**`_gql(op, query, rawLogin)`**: login = trimmed lower-cased, must match else `ArgumentError('Not a Twitch login')`; POST with variables `{"login": login}`; errors = every entry of `errors` joined with `"; "`, each written as `'${e['message']}'` when it is a Map (a Map without `message` yields the literal text `null`) or `'$e'` (its string form) when it is not a Map — this text becomes the user-visible exception message; `data` not a Map → `ChannelProfileApiException(errors or 'Malformed GQL response')`; nested resolver errors leave the rest usable (every block optional).

**Methods**
1. `isParticipatingDj(login)` → bool (invalid login or any error → false). It does NOT use `_gql`: the RAW login is checked against `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$` without trim or lower-casing (a login with surrounding spaces → false with no request) and sent unchanged in `{"login": login}` (upper-case stays upper-case); POST `ChannelDjRole` with the web Client-ID; result `data.user.roles.isParticipatingDJ == true` (DJ program channels: clips disabled, VOD audio rules).
