2. `isRestricted(channelId)` → bool: a non-numeric id (`^[0-9]{1,20}$`) → false with no request; otherwise any `channelSafetyStatus[]` string whose trimmed upper-case is in the restricted set; errors → false (shows a "this channel is restricted" notice).
3. `getTrailer(login)` → `ChannelTrailer?`: channel missing and errors → throw; else `ChannelTrailer.fromGql(channel.trailer.video)`.
4. `getProfile(login)` → `ChannelProfile?`: `user == null` → throw `ChannelProfileApiException(errors)` if errors, else null (no such channel); `ChannelProfile.fromGql(user)` or throw `ChannelProfileApiException('Unreadable profile')`.
5. `getSchedule(login)` → `ChannelSchedule`: schedule null with errors → throw; else `ChannelSchedule.fromGql(schedule)` (null → `ChannelSchedule.empty`).

**`ChannelTrailer { String videoId; String title; Duration? length; String? thumbnailUrl }`** (declared in this file): `fromGql(video)`: id `^[0-9]{1,20}$` required; title ≤300 or ''; `lengthSeconds` (chInt) > 0 → Duration; thumbnail https only.

Screens: Channel profile (About tab header with banner/avatar/accent colour/partner badge/followers, social links, panels with Markdown, last broadcast, live status; Schedule tab; trailer card on offline channels). Helix `getScheduleExtras` adds vacation and recurring flags; Helix `getChannelTeams` adds teams.

### lib/apis/twitch_community_highlights_service.dart

Snapshots of a channel's current poll, predictions and pinned chat message from anonymous GQL (web Client-ID, no token). Realtime changes come from Hermes (lib/services, see models/hermes.dart); these are the join/resubscribe snapshots. `TwitchCommunityHighlightsService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio.
- `CommunityHighlightsApiException(message, {isIntegrity = false})` (`toString` `'CommunityHighlightsApiException: $message'`).
- `typedef ChannelPredictions = ({Prediction? active, Prediction? locked, Prediction? lastResolved})` (community_highlights `Prediction`).
- Channel id check `_checked(raw)`: trimmed, `^[0-9]{1,20}$` else `ArgumentError('Not a Twitch user id')`.

Documents:
- `GetViewablePoll`: `query GetViewablePoll($channelID: ID!) { user(id: $channelID) { id viewablePoll { id title status durationSeconds remainingDurationMilliseconds startedAt endedAt totalVoters votes { total } choices { id title totalVoters votes { total } } settings { multichoice { isEnabled } } } } }`
- `GetActivePrediction` (`resolvedPredictionEvents` must stay or the document is rejected, verified 2026-10-13): `query GetActivePrediction($channelID: ID!, $resolvedCount: Int = 1) { user(id: $channelID) { id channel { id activePredictionEvents { id ...PredictionSnapshot } lockedPredictionEvents { id ...PredictionSnapshot } resolvedPredictionEvents(first: $resolvedCount) { edges { node { id ...PredictionSnapshot } } } } } } fragment PredictionSnapshot on PredictionEvent { id title status createdAt endedAt lockedAt predictionWindowSeconds outcomes { id title color totalPoints totalUsers topPredictors { id points user { id displayName } } } winningOutcome { id } }`
- `GetPinnedChat`: `query GetPinnedChat($channelID: ID!) { channel(id: $channelID) { id pinnedChatMessages(first: 1, messageType: MOD) { edges { node { id type startsAt updatedAt endsAt pinnedMessage { id content { text fragments { text content { __typename ... on Emote { emoteID: id } } } } sentAt sender { id login displayName chatColor displayBadges(channelID: $channelID) { id setID version } } } pinnedBy { id login displayName } } } } } }`

**`_gql(op, query, variables, {required Set<String> fields})`**: POST gql; body not a Map → `CommunityHighlightsApiException('Malformed GQL response')`. With non-empty errors: messages joined `"; "`; `isIntegrity` if they contain "integrity"; `partial` = data is a Map with at least one non-null value; `onField` = some error's `path.last` is one of `fields`. Throws when `isIntegrity || !partial || onField` (an error ON the requested field would otherwise read as "nothing there" and hide a highlight that exists); otherwise partial data is used (e.g. a prediction's nested `createdBy` resolver error). `data` not a Map → `CommunityHighlightsApiException('Empty GQL response')`.

Methods:
1. `getPoll(channelId)` (fields `{viewablePoll}`) → `Poll.fromGql(data.user.viewablePoll)`.
2. `getPredictions(channelId)` (fields `{activePredictionEvents, lockedPredictionEvents}`) → `(active: first parseable of channel.activePredictionEvents, locked: first of lockedPredictionEvents, lastResolved: first of resolvedPredictionEvents.edges[].node)` under `data.user.channel`.
3. `getPinned(channelId)` (fields `{pinnedChatMessages}`) → first `PinnedChatMessage.fromGql(edge.node)` under `data.channel.pinnedChatMessages.edges`, or null.
Screen: the highlights stack pinned above chat on the channel screen (poll card with bars and countdown, prediction card, pinned message with "pinned by" and expiry).

### lib/apis/twitch_hype_train_service.dart

Hype trains (and the "piñata" variant) from anonymous GQL. Hermes provides realtime updates; this is the snapshot on join, on resubscribe and while realtime is down. `TwitchHypeTrainService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio.
- `HypeTrainApiException(message, {isIntegrity = false})` (`toString` `'HypeTrainApiException: $message'`).
- `sealed class HypeTrainSnapshot` with `HtNoTrain()`, `HtApproachingSnapshot(HypeTrainApproaching approaching)`, `HtOngoingSnapshot(HypeTrainExecution train, {required DateTime fetchedAt})` (`fetchedAt` = local time of the answer, used to age `remainingSeconds`).
- Channel id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`; `normalizeChannelId(raw)` trims, else `ArgumentError('Not a Twitch user id')`. `_statusesChunk = 100`.

Fragments embedded by string interpolation:
- `_reward` = `id type ... on HypeTrainEmoteReward { emote { id token } } ... on HypeTrainBadgeReward { badge { id setID imageURL(size: DOUBLE) } }`
- `_conductor` = `source participation { source action quantity } user { id displayName login profileImageURL(width: 150) }`

Documents (verified against live trains 2026-10-13; `difficultySettings.levels` deliberately omitted — hundreds of levels):
- `GetHypeTrainExecution` (variables `{channelId, includeSharedHypeTrain: true}`): `query GetHypeTrainExecution($channelId: ID!, $includeSharedHypeTrain: Boolean!) { user(id: $channelId) { id channel { id hypeTrain { execution { id isActive isFastMode startedAt expiresAt updatedAt endedAt endReason progress { ...P } conductors { <conductor> } config { id difficulty isEnabled difficultySettings { difficulty maxLevel } kickoff { minPoints } conductorRewards { source type rewards { <reward> } } participationConversionRates { action source value } calloutEmote { id token } } allTimeHigh { ...C } isGoldenKappaTrain isTreasureTrain treasureTrainDetails { discountPercentage discountLevelThreshold status } variantTrainDetails { __typename ... on CommunityTrainDetails { currentRewardAmount nextRewardAmount previousRewardAmount rewardLevelThreshold maxRewardLevel status } ... on MythicTrainDetails { selectedEmote { id token } emotePool { id token } } } sharedHypeTrainDetails @include(if: $includeSharedHypeTrain) { id sharedConductors { user { ...U } conductors { <conductor> } } sharedProgress { user { ...U } channelProgress { ...P } } sharedAllTimeHighRecords { user { ...U } channelAllTimeHigh { ...C } } } } approaching { id goal participants eventsRemaining { events secondsRemaining } levelOneRewards { <reward> } isGoldenKappaTrain isTreasureTrain hypeTrainType } } } } } fragment L on HypeTrainLevel { id value goal rewards { <reward> } } fragment P on HypeTrainProgress { level { ...L } goal progression total remainingSeconds allTimeHighState } fragment C on HypeTrainCompleted { level { id value } goal progression total } fragment U on User { id displayName login profileImageURL(width: 150) }`
- `GetHypeTrainRewards` (`{channelId}`): `query GetHypeTrainRewards($channelId: ID!) { user(id: $channelId) { id channel { id hypeTrain { execution { id config { potentialRewards { id level value { <reward> } } } } } } } }`
- `HypeTrainStatuses` (`{channelIds: [...]}`): `query HypeTrainStatuses($channelIds: [ID!]!) { allActiveHypeTrainStatuses(channelIDs: $channelIds) { channel { id } level isAllTimeHighTrain isSharedTrain hypeTrainType } }`
- `GetOngoingPinata` (`{channelID}`): `query GetOngoingPinata($channelID: ID!) { user(id: $channelID) { id channel { id hypeTrain { execution { id isActive endedAt expiresAt variantTrainDetails { __typename ... on PinataTrainDetails { id deteriorationStage } } self { isEligibleForRewards } } } } } }`

**`_gql(op, query, variables)`**: like the highlights one without the `fields` rule — throws `HypeTrainApiException(messages, isIntegrity)` when errors are integrity-related or there is no partial data; `'Malformed GQL response'` when the body is not a Map; `'Empty GQL response'` when data is not a Map.

Methods:
1. `getActiveStatuses(channelIds)` → `Map<channelId, HtCardStatus>`, never throws: de-duplicates, keeps valid ids, chunks of 100; per item `channel.id` and `level` ≥ 1 → `HtCardStatus(level, variant: htVariantOf(item), isShared: isSharedTrain, isAllTimeHigh: isAllTimeHighTrain)`; a failing chunk is skipped (stream-card "Hype train Lv N" pills).
2. `getHypeTrain(channelId)` → `HypeTrainSnapshot`: `_snapshot(data)`: no `user.channel.hypeTrain` → `HtNoTrain`; `execution` with `isActive == true` and parseable (`HypeTrainExecution.parse`) → `HtOngoingSnapshot(train, fetchedAt: now)`; else `HypeTrainApproaching.parse(approaching)` → approaching; else no train. Any parse exception → `HypeTrainApiException('Malformed hype train: $e')`; transport → ApiException.
3. `getUpcomingRewards(channelId)` → `List<(int level, List<HtReward>)>` sorted by level, never throws (`[]`): from `execution.config.potentialRewards[]` with `level` ≥ 1 and `HtReward.parse(value)` non-null.
4. `getOngoingPinata(channelId)` → `PinataState?` = `PinataState.fromBody({'data': data})`; errors → null.
Screens: hype-train banner above chat (level, progress bar, countdown, conductors, rewards, shared/treasure/golden-kappa/community/mythic variants), stream-card pills, piñata banner.

### lib/apis/twitch_tv_auth_service.dart

OAuth **device-code** login for the "Twitch TV" session used to pass Twitch's integrity check on GQL mutations (TwitchTvSessionInterceptor). `TwitchTvAuthService(Dio dio, {DateTime Function()? now})`, base `https://id.twitch.tv/oauth2`, on anonymousGqlDio (so no Helix auth headers). Client id is the public Android-TV id `kTwitchTvClientId = 'ue6666qo983tsx6so1t0vnawi233wa'`; no secret (public client).
- URLs: `deviceUrl = https://id.twitch.tv/oauth2/device`, `tokenUrl = …/token`, `validateUrl = …/validate`, `revokeUrl = …/revoke`.
- `scopes = ['channel_read', 'chat:read', 'user_blocks_edit', 'user_blocks_read', 'user_follows_edit', 'user_read']` (joined with spaces).
- `deviceGrantType = 'urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:device_code'`.
- `_form(url, fields)`: `dio.post` with `contentType: application/x-www-form-urlencoded`, `validateStatus: (_) => true` (every status returned, nothing thrown for HTTP errors; bypasses BaseApiClient error mapping).

Methods:
1. `requestDeviceCode()` → `TwitchTvDeviceCode?`: POST device `client_id=<tv id>&scopes=<space-joined>`; status ≠ 200 → null; parse with `TwitchTvDeviceCode.fromJson(body, now: now())`; exception → null (logs runtime type only, never tokens).
2. `pollToken(code)` → `TwitchTvPoll`: if `code.isExpiredAt(now)` → `TwitchTvPollExpired` without request; POST token `client_id, device_code, grant_type=<deviceGrantType>`; 200 → `TwitchTvGrant.fromJson` → `TwitchTvPollGranted(grant)` or `TwitchTvPollFailed` if unreadable; otherwise `pollOf(body)`; exception → Failed.
3. `pollOf(body)` (static): lower-cased `body.message`: contains `authorization_pending` → Pending; `slow_down` → SlowDown (caller increases the interval); `denied` → Denied; `expired` or `invalid device` → Expired; else Failed.
4. `refresh(refreshToken)` → `TwitchTvRefresh`: POST token `client_id, grant_type=refresh_token, refresh_token`; 200 → granted (unreadable grant → unreachable); 4xx → rejected (session must be redone); other/exception → unreachable (keep session, retry later).
5. `revoke(accessToken)`: POST revoke `client_id, token`; errors swallowed.
6. `validate(accessToken)` → `TwitchTvValidation`: GET validate with `Authorization: OAuth <token>`, `validateStatus` all; 401 → invalid; non-200 → unknown; body → `TwitchTvIdentity.fromJson`; null or `client_id != tv id` → invalid; else `valid(userId)`. Exceptions → unknown.
Types in this file: `enum TwitchTvValidity { valid, invalid, unknown }`; `TwitchTvValidation` (`.valid(userId)`, `.invalid()`, `.unknown()`, fields `validity`, `userId?`); `enum TwitchTvRefreshOutcome { granted, rejected, unreachable }`; `TwitchTvRefresh` (`.granted(grant)`, `.rejected()`, `.unreachable()`, fields `outcome`, `grant?`).
Screen: the "Connect Twitch TV session" offer (`TwitchTvLoginOffer`) shown when a follow/vote/etc. hits `failed integrity check`: shows the user code and `https://www.twitch.tv/activate?device-code=<code>` (QR / open link), polls every `interval`. The session store (lib/screens/…/stores) persists `TwitchTvSession.encode()` in secure storage. PC: same flow, open the activation URL in the system browser.

### lib/apis/twitch_chat_history_service.dart

The chat said before the viewer joined, read once per chat opened via GQL `ChatHistory` (`Channel.recentChatMessages`). Twitch answers only for a logged-in twitch.tv session, so it uses the viewer's **web token** + web Client-ID (never ONSET's Helix token). `TwitchChatHistoryService(Dio dio, {timeout = 4 s})` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio. Constants: `maxMessages = 200` (newest kept), channel id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, login `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$`.
Document (operationName `ChatHistory`): `query ChatHistory($channelLogin: String!, $channelID: ID) { channel(name: $channelLogin) { recentChatMessages { id sentAt deletedAt content { text fragments { text content { __typename ... on Emote { id } ... on CheermoteToken { bitsAmount } ... on GifContent { id url } } } } sender { id login displayName chatColor } senderBadges(channelID: $channelID) { setID version } senderChatColor sourceChannel { id } parentMessage { id content { text } sender { id login displayName } } threadParentMessage { id sender { id login displayName } } } } }` (no AutoMod topics, no Shared Chat source badges).
`getRecentMessages({channelLogin, channelId, authToken})` → `List<ChatHistoryMessage>`, never throws: empty token or invalid login/id → `[]`; POST with headers `Client-ID` web + `Authorization: OAuth <token>`, variables `{"channelLogin": lower-cased, "channelID": channelId}`, `followRedirects: false` (token must not follow a redirect to another host), a CancelToken, and `.timeout(4 s)` which cancels the request and throws (caught). Any failure → `[]` (debug log only prints the error TYPE — the error could carry the token). `_parse(body)`: body not Map → `[]`; `body['errors'] != null` (any non-null errors value, even beside data) → `[]` — a body carrying `"errors": null` is accepted (partial list not trusted); `data.channel.recentChatMessages` List; keep only the last 200 BEFORE parsing; each `ChatHistoryMessage.tryParse`, nulls dropped; order oldest first as sent. These lines are rendered greyed at the top of chat as "history" (the chat-delay and deleted-message features apply to them).

### lib/apis/twitch_chat_rules_service.dart

A channel's chat rules from anonymous GQL (verified 2026-10-20). `TwitchChatRulesService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio. `ChatRulesApiException(message)` (`toString` `'ChatRulesApiException: $message'`).
Document `ChatRules`: `query ChatRules($userID: ID!) { user(id: $userID) { id displayName profileImageURL(width: 150) chatSettings { rules } } }`.
`getRules(channelId)` → `ChatRules?`: id `^[0-9]{1,20}$` else `ArgumentError('Not a channel id')`; POST header `Client-ID` web, variables `{"userID": channelId}`; `data` not a Map → `ChatRulesApiException('Malformed GQL response')`; returns `ChatRules.fromGql(data.user)` (null when no such channel). Shown as the "Chat rules" sheet before first message in a channel (Twitch's `willShowRules` = has non-blank rules).

### lib/apis/twitch_followed_categories_service.dart

The categories the viewer follows (Helix has no such list) via GQL with the viewer's **web token**. `TwitchFollowedCategoriesService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio. Document (validated 2026-10-20) operationName `FollowedGamesQuery`: `query FollowedGamesQuery($first: Int) { currentUser { followedGames(first: $first) { nodes { id name displayName slug viewersCount boxArtURL(width: 285, height: 380) } } } }`, variables `{"first": 100}`.
`getFollowedCategories({authToken})` → `List<FollowedCategory>?`: empty token → null without request; POST headers `Client-ID: kimne78kx3ncx6brgo4mv6wki5h1ko`, `Authorization: OAuth <token>`; ANY `errors` List → null; `data.currentUser` not Map → null; `followedGames.nodes` not List → null; else nodes parsed with `FollowedCategory.tryParse` (nulls skipped) in Twitch's order. Every failure (integrity refusal, schema change, rejected token, network) → null once (debug log); the caller hides the section; no retries. Screen: Following › "Followed categories" row (box art, viewers), long-press opens twitch.tv page; follow/unfollow category goes through TwitchViewerActionsService.

### lib/apis/twitch_watch_streak_service.dart

The viewer's watch streaks (read only; this app sends no watch events so it never advances a streak; sharing and "Save streak" are not done here except the share mutation in viewer actions). `TwitchWatchStreakService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio, web token. Numeric id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`.
Documents (validated 2026-10-20):
- `WatchStreakMilestoneQuery`: `query WatchStreakMilestoneQuery($channelId: ID!) { channel(id: $channelId) { self { watchStreakMilestone { watchStreakMilestone { id value shareStatus } watchStreakThreshold watchStreakCopoBonus state expiresAt } } } channelViewerMilestoneSettings(channelID: $channelId) { id isWatchStreakOptOut } }`
- `BatchGetWatchStreaksQuery`: `query BatchGetWatchStreaksQuery { batchGetWatchStreaks { channel { id } value isSuspended } }` (sent with NO `variables` key at all: `'variables': ?null` omits it).
`_ask(op, query, token, variables)`: POST gql with web Client-ID + `Authorization: OAuth <token>`; body `{"operationName","query","variables"?}`; returns the decoded map or null on any exception.
Methods: `getWatchStreak({channelId, authToken})` → `WatchStreak.tryParse(body)` (empty token/invalid id → null); `getStreaks({authToken})` → `WatchStreak.parseBatch(body)` = `Map<channelId, streakValue>` of running streaks (shown as a flame count on followed stream cards). Screen: streak row in the channel-points sheet, streak change toasts ("Streak recovered" / "Streak expired").

### lib/apis/twitch_report_service.dart

Report users, channels, emotes, chat messages and clips through GQL `ReportContent` with the web token. `TwitchReportService(Dio)` base `''`, anonymousGqlDio. `maxDescriptionLength = 500`; numeric id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`; opaque id `^[A-Za-z0-9_:\-]{1,128}$`.
Document: `mutation ReportContent($input: ReportContentInput!) { reportContent(input: $input) { content contentID } }`.
- `isReportable(target)` = numeric `targetUserId` AND opaque `contentId` AND (`extra` empty OR numeric) — the UI hides "Report" otherwise.
- `report({target, reason, description, authToken})` → `ViewerActionOutcome`: empty token → signedOut; not reportable, reason not in `target.reasons`, or trimmed description longer than 500 UTF-16 code units (Dart `.length`) → failed; POST with `extra: TwitchTvSessionInterceptor.userAction`, headers web Client-ID + OAuth, variables `{"input":{"content": target.contentType,"contentID": target.contentId,"targetID": target.targetUserId,"reason": reason.id,"description": text,"extra": target.extra}}`. `_outcomeOf`: body not Map → failed; non-empty errors → integrityRefused if `isIntegrityRefusal(body)` else **refused** (unlike viewerActionOutcomeOf which says failed); `data.reportContent` not Map → failed; `contentID == target.contentId` → done else failed. 401 → signedOut; other exceptions → failed.
Screen: the Report sheet (reason list per kind, optional description with 500-char counter) from user card, channel menu, emote sheet, message long-press, clip menu.

### lib/apis/bttv_api.dart

BetterTTV REST (`BTTVApi(Dio)`, base `https://api.betterttv.net/3/cached`), on the shared Helix Dio (default headers `User-Agent: ONSET (Flutter Twitch Client)`, `Accept/Content-Type: application/json`; no Twitch auth added — different host; 8/15/10 s timeouts; errors mapped by BaseApiClient). No caching here (GlobalAssetsStore caches globals; per-channel results are cached by the chat assets store).
1. `getEmotesGlobal()` GET `/emotes/global` → JSON array of `EmoteBTTV` → `Emote.fromBTTV(e, EmoteType.bttvGlobal)`.
2. `getEmotesChannel({id})` GET `/users/twitch/<twitchUserId>` → `EmoteBTTVChannel.fromJson` → `channelEmotes` as `bttvChannel` followed by `sharedEmotes` as `bttvShared` (a channel without BTTV answers 404 → NotFoundException, callers treat as none).
3. `getBadges()` GET `/badges` → array of `BadgeInfoBTTV` → `Map<providerId (Twitch user id), ChatBadge.fromBTTV>`.
Rust: `BttvClient` with `serde` structs `EmoteBttv { id, code, #[serde(default)] animated }`, `BttvChannel { #[serde(rename="channelEmotes")] …, #[serde(rename="sharedEmotes")] … }`.

### lib/apis/ffz_api.dart

FrankerFaceZ REST (`FFZApi(Dio)`, base `https://api.frankerfacez.com/v1`), shared Helix Dio.
1. `getEmotesGlobal()` GET `/set/global` → for each id in `default_sets` (ints), `sets[<id as string>].emoticons` → `EmoteFFZ.fromJson` → `Emote.fromFFZ(e, EmoteType.ffzGlobal)` (sets not listed in `default_sets` are ignored).
2. `getRoomInfo({id})` GET `/room/id/<twitchUserId>` → record `(RoomFFZ room, List<Emote>)`: `RoomFFZ.fromJson(data.room)`; emotes from `sets[<room.set>].emoticons` as `ffzChannel`. Room carries custom mod/VIP badge images and `user_badge_ids`.
3. `getBadges()` GET `/badges/ids` → `badges[]` → `BadgeInfoFFZ`; sets the public mutable field `Map<String, ChatBadge>? badgeDefinitions` = `{badge.id.toString(): ChatBadge.fromFFZ(badge)}` (used to resolve room `user_badge_ids`); returns `Map<userId, List<ChatBadge>>` built by iterating badges in REVERSE order and appending for each user id in `users[<badge id>]` (so later-defined badges come first per user).
Rust: `FfzClient`; the reverse-order rule must be preserved.

### lib/apis/seventv_api.dart

7TV (`SevenTVApi(Dio)`, base `https://7tv.io/v3`; plus v4 GQL), shared Helix Dio. Also declares `SevenTvChannel { String setId; String? userId; List<Emote> emotes }`.
1. `getEmotesGlobal()` GET `/emote-sets/global` → `emotes[]` → `Emote7TV.fromJson` → `Emote.from7TV(e, sevenTVGlobal)` (NOT filtered by empty URL here).
2. `getEmotesChannel({id})` → `(channel.setId, channel.emotes)` from `getChannel`.
3. `getChannel({id})` GET `/users/twitch/<twitchUserId>` → `emote_set.id` (String, required), `user.id` (7TV user id, used to subscribe to 7TV EventAPI cosmetics/emote-set updates), emotes = `_activeEmotes(emote_set.emotes, sevenTVChannel)` (parsed, emotes with empty url dropped).
4. `getEmoteSet({id, type})` GET `/emote-sets/<Uri.encodeComponent(id)>` → `_activeEmotes(emotes ?? [], type)` (used for personal sets and live set switches).
5. `getUsersCosmetics(twitchIds)` → `Map<twitchId, SevenTvUserCosmetics>`: de-duplicates, keeps `^[0-9]{1,20}$`; empty → `{}` without request. POST `https://7tv.io/v4/gql` body `{"query": "query { users { <lookups> } } <fragment>"}` where lookups = for each index i: `u<i>: userByConnection(platform: TWITCH, platformId: "<id>") { ...Cosmetics }` joined by single spaces. Fragment (`_imageFields` = `url mime scale width height frameCount`): `fragment Cosmetics on User { style { activePaint { id name data { layers { opacity ty { __typename ... on PaintLayerTypeSingleColor { color { hex } } ... on PaintLayerTypeLinearGradient { angle repeating stops { at color { hex } } } ... on PaintLayerTypeRadialGradient { repeating stops { at color { hex } } } ... on PaintLayerTypeImage { images { <imageFields> } } } } shadows { color { hex } offsetX offsetY blur } } } activeBadge { id name description images { <imageFields> } } } personalEmoteSet { id emotes { items { alias flags { zeroWidth } emote { id defaultName flags { defaultZeroWidth } images { <imageFields> } } } } } }`. `data.users` not a Map → `FormatException('7TV cosmetics answer without users')`. Each id → `SevenTvUserCosmetics.fromGql(users['u<i>'] as Map or null)` (an unknown user gives empty cosmetics). Callers batch chatters (name paints, 7TV badges, personal emotes).
Rust: `SevenTvClient`; cosmetics batching via the same aliasing scheme.

### lib/apis/giphy_service.dart

GIPHY search for the chat GIF picker using the API key Twitch hands the viewer (`GifPickerConfig`). `GiphyService(Dio)` base `https://api.giphy.com/v1/gifs`, on anonymousGqlDio (no default headers). Constants `pageSize = 24`, `maxQueryLength = 50`.
- `search({config, query})`: trimmed empty → `trending(config)`; else `_gifs('/search', config, {q: first 50 chars})`.
- `trending({config})` → `_gifs('/trending', config, {})`.
- `_gifs(path, config, extra)`: `!config.canSend` → null without request; GET with query `api_key=<config.apiKey>&limit=24&offset=0&rating=<pg|pg-13>&…extra`; `data` List → unmodifiable list of `GiphyGif.fromJson` non-null; else null; any exception → null (debug log `'GIPHY <path> failed: <describeFailure>'`).
- `describeFailure(error)` (visible for tests): DioException with response → `'HTTP <status>'`; ApiException with status → `'HTTP <status>'`; else the runtime type (never the URL, which carries the api key).
Only the first page is fetched (no infinite scroll).

### lib/apis/onset_update_service.dart

In-app updates from the GitHub releases of the update repository (`UPDATE_REPO` define, `owner/name`; optional read-only `UPDATE_TOKEN` define only while the repo is private). `OnsetUpdateService({required String token, required String repo, Dio? dio, Future<Directory> Function()? cacheDirectory, Duration idleTimeout = 30 s})` → base `https://api.github.com`, its OWN Dio (default `BaseOptions(connectTimeout: 5 s, receiveTimeout: 15 s)`), never the app's: Twitch interceptors must not see these requests and the GitHub token must not reach Twitch. Default cache dir = `getTemporaryDirectory()`.

**Exceptions** (plain `Exception`s, not ApiException): `UpdateDownloadException` (network/server/unexpected redirect), `UpdateIntegrityException` (APK hash mismatch or no valid hash), `UpdateCancelledException`, `UpdateNotVisibleException` (anonymous build + 404: private repo or no release — not a failure), `UpdateRateLimitedException` (anonymous + 403/429).

**Rules**: repo valid when non-empty, no `..`, matches `^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$`; hash pattern `^[0-9a-f]{64}$`; anonymous = empty token. API headers: `Authorization: Bearer <token>` (only when a token exists), `Accept: application/vnd.github+json`, `X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2022-11-28`. Allowed redirect hosts (exact): `api.github.com`, `github.com`, `objects.githubusercontent.com`, `release-assets.githubusercontent.com`. Asset URL `https://api.github.com/repos/<repo>/releases/assets/<id>`.

**Methods**
1. `latest()` → `OnsetRelease?`: invalid repo → null; GET `/repos/<repo>/releases/latest` with API headers → `OnsetRelease.tryParse`. ApiException: anonymous & 404 → `UpdateNotVisibleException`; anonymous & 403/429 → `UpdateRateLimitedException`; otherwise log only status/message (never the request, which holds the token) and null. Other errors → null.
2. `expectedSha256(release, {cancelToken})` → lowercase hex or null: an invalid repo → null without a request (so `download` then throws `UpdateIntegrityException`, not a download error); opens the `.sha256` asset, reads at most 4096 bytes (more → treated as integrity issue → null); text decoded byte-by-byte with `String.fromCharCodes` (Latin-1, not UTF-8), trimmed, first whitespace-separated token lower-cased must match the hash pattern; fetch failure → rethrown mapped by `_failure` (download/cancel), integrity → null.
3. `download(release, {onProgress, cancelToken})` → `File`: expected hash first (null → `UpdateIntegrityException`); cancelled → `UpdateCancelledException`; directory `<cache>/updates` cleared entirely; writes `<cache>/updates/onset-<build>.apk.part` from the APK asset stream, progress `(received, total)` with total = response content-length when > 0 else `release.apkSize`; SHA-256 of the part file must equal expected (else integrity error); renamed to `onset-<build>.apk`. Any failure deletes the part file and throws the mapped exception (log shows status or runtime type only).
4. `removeDownloadsUpTo(installedBuild)`: in `<cache>/updates`, keeps only files named `^onset-(\d{1,9})\.apk$` with build > installed; deletes everything else (old APKs, `.part` files, anything).
- `_openAsset(id)`: GET asset URL with headers API headers + `Accept: application/octet-stream`, `followRedirects: false`, stream response, accepting 200 or 3xx. 200 → body. 3xx → cancel that body, resolve `Location` relative to the request; must be https, port 443, no userinfo, host in the allowlist, else `UpdateDownloadException`; second GET to the signed URL WITHOUT the token (only `Accept: application/octet-stream`), stream — made with Dio defaults (`followRedirects: true`, default `validateStatus`), so a FURTHER redirect from the signed host is followed automatically to ANY host with no allowlist check (the allowlist only guards the first hop; the token is never sent there and the SHA-256 check still applies).
- `_read(stream, cancel, onChunk)`: `stream.timeout(idleTimeout)` (Dio's receive timeout does not cover streamed bodies) — no bytes for 30 s → download exception; cancellation (even before start) → `UpdateCancelledException`; errors from onChunk stop the read.
- `_failure(e)`: passes through the three update exceptions; Dio cancel → cancelled; anything else → `UpdateDownloadException`.
Screen: Settings › About "Check for updates" / update banner with release notes, progress bar, cancel; the APK is then handed to the Android installer. PC equivalent: same GitHub release flow but desktop assets (see PC section); installer handoff is OS-specific.

### lib/models/playback_access_token.dart

`class PlaybackAccessToken` (immutable): `String value` (the token, itself a JSON document), `String signature`, `DateTime? expiresAt` (required named but nullable), `PlaybackRefusal? refusal` (why Twitch would refuse; a token is still given e.g. for a sub-only free preview), `DateTime? previewEndsAt`. Factory `PlaybackAccessToken.fromValue({value, signature, refusal})` computes `expiresAt = parseExpiry(value)` and `previewEndsAt = parsePreviewEnd(value)`.
- `parseExpiry(value)`: `jsonDecode(value)` must be a Map whose `expires` is a num (unix seconds, may be fractional) → `DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch((expires*1000).round(), isUtc: true)`; otherwise / FormatException → null.
- `parsePreviewEnd(value)`: decoded Map; `chansub` must be `{'view_until': num}` and `expires` a num (both converted the same way); returns `view_until` only when it is strictly before `expires`, else null (a far-future `view_until` means no preview limit).
`enum PlaybackRefusal { subscribersOnly, geoblocked, bannedFromChannel, unauthorized, proxyDetected, wifiRequired, unsupportedDevice }`:
- `fromCode(code)` (GQL `authorization.forbiddenReasonCode`): `UNAUTHORIZED_ENTITLEMENTS`→subscribersOnly, `GEOBLOCKED`→geoblocked, `CHANNEL_RESTRICTED_BANNED_USER`→bannedFromChannel, `UNAUTHORIZED`→unauthorized; `NONE`/unknown/null → null.
- `fromUsherCode(code)`: `anonymizer_blocked`→proxyDetected, `cellular_geoblocked`→wifiRequired, `no_cdm_specified`→unsupportedDevice, else null.
`enum SubscriberTier { any, tier2, tier3 }`; `fromRestrictionOptions(List options)`: contains `ALLOW_TIER_3_ONLY` → tier3; else contains `ALLOW_TIER_2_AND_3_ONLY` → tier2; else any. Rust: `struct PlaybackAccessToken { value: String, signature: String, expires_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>, refusal: Option<PlaybackRefusal>, preview_ends_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>> }`.

### lib/models/hls_variant.dart

`HlsVariant { String name; String url }` (name e.g. `1080p60 (source)`, `720p60`, `audio_only`). `static parseMasterPlaylist(String playlist)`: split lines (`LineSplitter`, handles \r\n), trim each, drop empty; if no lines or the first is not exactly `#EXTM3U` → `[]`. Walk: a line starting `#EXT-X-MEDIA:` sets `pendingName` to the `NAME="([^"]*)"` capture (or null if absent); a non-`#` line with a pending name adds `HlsVariant(name: pendingName, url: line)` and clears it. `#EXT-X-STREAM-INF` lines are ignored (the URL after it is paired with the preceding MEDIA name). Order preserved as in the playlist (Twitch lists source first). URLs are kept as-is (absolute in Twitch playlists).

### lib/models/quality_ladder.dart

`UnavailableQuality { String name; int? bitrate }`. `QualityLadder { Map<String,int> bitrates = {}; List<UnavailableQuality> unavailable = [] }`, `static const empty`. Constant `_unavailableMediaId = 'com.amazon.ivs.unavailable-media'`.
- `bitrateOf(quality)` = `bitrates[normalizeQualityName(quality)]`; `normalizeQualityName` (lib/screens/channel/video/quality_selection.dart) = remove `\s*\(.*?\)` groups (e.g. ` (source)`), trim, lower-case, collapse whitespace runs to `_`.
- `unavailableBesides(available)` = unavailable entries whose normalized name is not among the normalized available names.
- `parse(playlist)`: same line cleaning and `#EXTM3U` check (else `empty`). `#EXT-X-MEDIA:` → pendingName = attribute NAME; `#EXT-X-STREAM-INF:` with pending name → `BANDWIDTH` parsed as int, stored under the normalized name when > 0, pending cleared; `#EXT-X-SESSION-DATA:` with `DATA-ID` == the unavailable media id → `_unavailableMedia(VALUE)`.
- `_attribute(line, key)`: first try quoted `(?:[:,])KEY="([^"]*)"`, else unquoted `(?:[:,])KEY=([^,]*)`.
- `_unavailableMedia(value)`: base64-decode → UTF-8 → JSON List; entries that are maps with non-empty String `NAME` and `RESOLUTION` → `UnavailableQuality(name, bitrate: BANDWIDTH when int > 0)`; FormatException → `[]`.
Used with `TwitchStreamService.qualityLadder(url)` (request with `include_unavailable=true`) to show which qualities exist but are locked (e.g. sub-only 1440p) and per-quality bitrates in the quality menu.

### lib/models/vod.dart

- `enum VodType { archive('ARCHIVE'), highlight('HIGHLIGHT'), upload('UPLOAD') }` with `gqlName`, `fromGql(Object?)` (exact match else null).
- `class Vod`: `String id`, `String title`, `int lengthSeconds`, `DateTime createdAt`, `int viewCount`, `String ownerId`, `String ownerLogin`, `String ownerDisplayName`, `String? previewThumbnailUrl`, `String? gameId`, `String? gameName`, `VodType? type`, `bool isRecording = false` (status `RECORDING`: still live, archive growing), `bool isRestricted = false` (`self.isRestricted == true`: sub-only for this viewer), `String? broadcastId` (the live stream id this archive recorded, only if `^[0-9]{1,20}$`). Getter `duration = Duration(seconds: lengthSeconds)`.
- `Vod.fromGql(JsonMap node, {JsonMap? owner})`: required `id` String, `lengthSeconds` finite num, `createdAt` parseable by `DateTime.tryParse('${node['createdAt']}')`, else `FormatException('Invalid video node')`. `title` String or `''`; `viewCount` finite num → int else 0; owner = `node.owner` when a Map else the passed `owner` (channel lists) — `id`/`login`/`displayName` Strings or `''`; `previewThumbnailURL`; `game.id`/`game.name`; `broadcastType` → VodType; `broadcast.id`. Non-matching types never throw (helper `_as<T>`).
- `VodPage { List<Vod> items; bool hasMore }` (single page up to 100, no cursor paging anonymously).
- `VodSearchPage { List<Vod> items; String? cursor; bool hasMore = false }`.
- `enum WatchedVideoType { live('LIVE'), vod('VOD') }`.
- `ViewingHistory { int positionSec; DateTime? updatedAt }`.
- `ResumeWatchingEntry { Vod vod; ViewingHistory history }`.
- `VideoLookup { List<Vod> videos; Set<String> missing }` (missing = answered null without error → deleted).
- `MutedSegment { int offset; int duration }` (seconds).

### lib/models/vod_access.dart

`VodAccessInfo { bool forbidden; String? reason; List<String> restrictedBitrates; DateTime? expiresAt; bool authenticated = false }`.
- `fromTokenValue(value)` (tolerant; unreadable → allowed, `restrictedBitrates: []`): decode JSON Map; `authorization.forbidden == true` → forbidden; `authorization.reason` non-empty String → reason; `chansub.restricted_bitrates` List of Strings (qualities only subscribers can watch, e.g. `["1080p60","chunked"]`); `expiresAt = PlaybackAccessToken.parseExpiry(value)`; `authenticated` = `user_id` is a num, or a non-empty String (token asked with the viewer's credentials, so a refusal is the viewer's own).
- `fromUsherUrl(url)` = reads the `token` query parameter of an usher URL; missing → anonymous allowed.

### lib/models/vod_chapter.dart

`VodChapter { Duration start; Duration length; String title; String? gameId; String? boxArtUrl }`, getter `end = start + length`. `tryParse(node)`: node Map; `positionMilliseconds` num ≥ 0 and `durationMilliseconds` num required (negative duration → 0); no finiteness check: a non-finite value (JSON `1e400` decodes to `Infinity`; NaN passes the `< 0` test) makes `toInt()` throw `UnsupportedError` (confirmed with the Dart SDK: `Unsupported operation: Infinity or NaN toInt`) — `TwitchVodService.getChapters` catches it and returns `[]`; title = trimmed `details.game.displayName` when non-blank, else trimmed `description` when non-blank, else null (chapter dropped); `gameId` non-empty String; `boxArtURL` only if starts with `https://`. Top-level `sortedChapters(iterable)` sorts ascending by start (copy). `chapterAt(chapters, at)`: last chapter whose start ≤ `at` in a sorted list, null if before the first.

### lib/models/vod_comment.dart

- `VodFragment { String text; String? emoteId }` (Twitch emote id when it is an emote; 7TV/BTTV/FFZ are matched client-side in plain text).
- `VodComment { String? id; int offsetSeconds; List<VodFragment> fragments; List<({String setId, String version})> badges; String? commenterId, commenterLogin, commenterDisplayName; String? color }`. `fromGql(node)`: commenter Map optional; `message.fragments[]` maps → text (`''` default) and `emote.emoteID`; `message.userBadges[]` with String `setID` and `version`; `contentOffsetSeconds` num→int else 0 — no finiteness check, so a non-finite number (JSON `1e400` → `Infinity`) throws `UnsupportedError` from `toInt()` (confirmed with the Dart SDK); `getComments` does not catch it, so the whole page fails; `message.userColor` (e.g. `#FF0000`). Otherwise never throws. Getters `text` = fragments' text joined; `key` = `id ?? '$offsetSeconds|$commenterId|$text'` (dedupe across overlapping pages).
- `VodCommentPage { List<VodComment> comments; bool hasNextPage }`.

### lib/models/video_collection.dart

`VideoCollection { String id; String title; int itemCount; String firstVideoId; String? thumbnailUrl; DateTime? updatedAt; int lengthSeconds = 0 }`. `tryParse(node)`: requires `id` matching `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$` and `items.edges[0].node.id` matching `^[0-9]{1,20}$` (else null — an empty collection is dropped); `title` String or `''`; `itemCount` = `items.totalCount` when finite > 0 else 1; `thumbnailURL` https-only; `updatedAt` tryParse; `lengthSeconds` finite > 0 else 0. `listFrom(data)` reads `data.user.collections.edges[].node`. Shown as the "Collections" shelf on the channel profile's videos tab.

### lib/models/clip.dart

- `enum ClipPeriod { lastDay('LAST_DAY'), lastWeek('LAST_WEEK'), lastMonth('LAST_MONTH'), allTime('ALL_TIME') }` (`gqlName`; labels in lib/l10n/enum_labels.dart).
- `enum ClipSort { top('VIEWS_DESC'), trending('TRENDING'), newest('VIEWS_DESC'), oldest('VIEWS_DESC') }` — anonymous clients get "server error" for other GQL sorts (verified 2026-10-13), so newest/oldest are fetched as VIEWS_DESC and re-sorted client side. Getter `fetched` = trending stays trending, everything else → top. `order(clips)`: newest → copy sorted by `createdAt` desc; oldest → asc; top/trending → unchanged.
- `typedef ClipDateRange = ({DateTime start, DateTime end})`.
- `isValidClipSlug(slug)` = `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,99}$` (old CamelCase and new `Words-AbC123_x` slugs; slugs go into URLs/GQL so anything else is refused).
- `class Clip`: `String id`, `String slug`, `String title`, `int viewCount`, `DateTime createdAt`, `int durationSeconds`, `String broadcasterId`, `String broadcasterLogin`, `String broadcasterDisplayName`, `String? thumbnailUrl`, `String? curatorLogin` (null when the clipper's account is gone), `String? curatorDisplayName`, `String? gameId`, `String? gameName`, `String? vodId` (source VOD while it exists), `int? vodOffsetSeconds`, `bool isFeatured = false`. Getters `duration` (seconds) and `vodOffset` (Duration?).
- `Clip.fromGql(node, {broadcaster})`: requires String `id`, valid-slug `slug`, parseable `createdAt`, finite num `durationSeconds`, else `FormatException('Invalid clip node')`. `title` default `''`; `viewCount` = `_nonNegative` (finite → max(0, toInt)) else 0; owner = `node.broadcaster` Map else the passed map; `curator.login/displayName`; `game.id/name`; `video.id` → vodId; `vodOffsetSeconds` only when vodId non-null and `videoOffsetSeconds` ≥ 0; `isFeatured == true`.
- `Clip.tryHelix(node, {broadcasterLogin = ''})` (Helix `/clips` items): returns null unless `id` is a valid slug, `created_at` parses, `duration` finite num. slug = id; `title`; `view_count`; `broadcaster_id`; `broadcaster_name` → display name; `broadcasterLogin` from the argument; `thumbnail_url` https-only; `creator_name` → curatorDisplayName (curatorLogin null); `game_id` (gameName null); `video_id` non-empty → vodId; `vod_offset` ≥ 0 only with a VOD; `is_featured == true`.
- `ClipPage { List<Clip> items; bool hasMore; bool featuredKnown = true }` (featuredKnown false when the `isFeatured` field had to be dropped).
- `ClipFeedPage { List<Clip> items; String? cursor }`, `hasMore => cursor != null`.
- `ClipRendition { int height; double frameRate; String url (unsigned) }`, `label` = `'${height}p${frameRate.round()}'` when frameRate > 31 else `'${height}p'`.
- `ClipPlayback { List<ClipRendition> renditions; String signature; String token }`, `signedUrl(r)` = `signClipUrl(r.url, …)`.
- `signClipUrl(url, {signature, token})`: parses the URL and replaces its query with `{...existing, 'sig': signature, 'token': token}` (encoded by `Uri`).
- `ClipExtras { bool autoClip; bool hasPortrait }`, `tryParse(clip)`: `autoClip = isAutoCurated == true`; `hasPortrait` = some asset of `type == 'RECOMPOSED'` with numeric `aspectRatio` strictly between 0 and 1. Drives the "Auto clip" badge and the portrait toggle in the clip player.

### lib/models/clip_comment.dart

- `ClipComment { String id; String body; DateTime createdAt; String authorLogin; String authorDisplayName; String? authorImageUrl; int likeCount = 0; int replyCount = 0; bool heartedByCreator = false; bool likedByViewer = false; bool collapsed = false }`. `tryParse(node)`: requires non-empty String `id`, String `body`, parseable `createdAt`; author `login` (default `''`), `displayName` (default login; empty → login), `profileImageURL` https-only; counts via `_count` (finite > 0 → int else 0); `isHeartedByCreator`, `isLikedByViewer`, `isCollapsedByCommunity` booleans (`== true`). `withLike({liked})` returns a copy: unchanged count when `liked == likedByViewer`, else +1 or −1 (clamped ≥ 0), `likedByViewer = liked` (optimistic like toggle).
- `ClipCommentPage { List<ClipComment> items; String? cursor }`, `tryParse(data)`: `data.clip.comments.edges` must be a List (else null); nodes parsed (nulls skipped); cursor = `pageInfo.endCursor` only when `hasNextPage == true` and non-empty.
- `enum ClipCommentBlock { commentsDisabled, notFollowing, followTooRecent, notSubscribed, unverified, other }`, `of(codes)` priority: `COMMENTS_DISABLED` → commentsDisabled; `USER_NOT_FOLLOWING` → notFollowing; `FOLLOWING_LENGTH_REQUIREMENT_NOT_MET` → followTooRecent; `USER_NOT_SUBSCRIBED` → notSubscribed; `EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED` or `PHONE_NOT_VERIFIED` → unverified; else other.
- `ClipCommentEligibility { bool allowed; ClipCommentBlock? block; int? requiredFollowMinutes }`, `tryParse(data)`: `data.clip.self.commentCreationEligibility` with bool `isAllowed` (else null); true → allowed; false → `block = of(errorCodes stringified)`, `requiredFollowingLengthMinutes` num > 0 → int.

### lib/models/recap.dart

`Recap { String id; String title; String channelId; String channelLogin; String channelDisplayName; List<Clip> clips; DateTime? streamStartAt; DateTime? postedAt }`. `isValidId(id)` = `^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,64}$`. `tryParse(node)`: requires valid `id` and `state == 'PUBLISHED'`; owner map from `channel {id, login, displayName}`; each `clips[]` entry with a `clip` Map parsed by `Clip.fromGql(…, broadcaster: owner)` (FormatException skipped), position = `position` int else list index; no clips → null; sorted by position then original index. `title` trimmed or `''`; `channelId` String or `''`; `channelLogin`; display name = displayName when non-empty else login; dates via tryParse. Getters `duration` = sum of clip durations; `thumbnailUrl` = first clip's thumbnail. `RecapUnavailableException` (const, no message) when a recap is missing/unpublished. Recaps are the stream "highlight reels" shown on the channel profile and opened from links.

### lib/models/chat_identity.dart

- `ChatBadgeChoice { String setId; String version; String title; String? imageUrl }`, `key = '$setId/$version'`. `tryParse(raw)`: Map with `setID` and `version` matching `^[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{1,64}$`; `title` non-empty String else setId; image = `imageUrlDouble ?? imageUrlNormal`, kept only when https. `listFrom(raw)` parses a List skipping nulls.
- `enum ChatBadgeScope { channel, global }`.
- `ChatIdentity { List<ChatBadgeChoice> channelBadges; List<ChatBadgeChoice> globalBadges; ChatBadgeChoice? channelSelected; ChatBadgeChoice? globalSelected; bool? flairHidden }`; `badgesFor(scope)`, `selectedFor(scope)`. `fromBody(body)`: `data` Map with `user` Map (else null); `data.channel.self.availableBadges` / `selectedBadge` (channel scope), `user.availableBadges` / `user.selectedBadge` (global), `user.subscriptionSettings.isBadgeModifierHidden` bool → flairHidden. Feeds the "Chat identity" sheet (pick channel/global badge, hide sub flair) — query lives in TwitchSocialService/AccountGql.

### lib/models/chat_ui_settings.dart

`enum ChatUiSetting` with `field`, `operation`, `mutation`, `inputType`:
| value | field | operation | mutation | inputType |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| emoteAnimations | isEmoteAnimationsEnabled | SyncedSettingsSetEmoteAnimations | setEmoteAnimationsEnabled | SetEmoteAnimationsEnabledInput |
| gifs | isGifsEnabled | SyncedSettingsSetGifsEnabled | setGifsEnabled | SetGifsEnabledInput |
| gifAnimations | isGifAnimationsEnabled | SyncedSettingsSetGifAnimationsEnabled | setGifAnimationsEnabled | SetGifAnimationsEnabledInput |
| readableColors | isReadableChatColorsEnabled | SetReadableChatColorsPreference | setReadableChatColorsEnabled | SetReadableChatColorsEnabledInput |
`document` = `mutation <operation>($input: <inputType>!) { <mutation>(input: $input) { <field> } }`.
`ChatUiSettings(Map<ChatUiSetting,bool> values)` (unmodifiable), `operator [](setting)` (non-null assert). `fromSettings(settings)`: Map where ALL four fields are bools, else null. `differencesFrom(other)` lists settings whose values differ; `==` true when no differences; `hashCode` over values in enum order. These are the viewer's Twitch-account-synced chat preferences shown in chat settings ("Synced with Twitch").

### lib/models/follow_result.dart

`enum FollowResult { done, tooManyFollows, unverifiedEmail, unverifiedPhone, unverifiedPhoneAndEmail, forbidden, refused, noWebSession, signedOut, needsTvSession, tvSessionExpired, integrityRefused, gqlError, failed }`. Getters: `succeeded` (done), `needsWebSession` (noWebSession|signedOut), `needsTvConnect` (needsTvSession|tvSessionExpired), `canRetry` (refused|gqlError|failed). `fromBody(body, field, {trace})`: `trace.tvSessionRejected` → tvSessionExpired (first); body not Map → failed; non-empty errors → if NOT `isIntegrityRefusal(body)` → gqlError, else `integrityRefused` when the TV session was already used, otherwise `needsTvSession` (offer TV login); `data[field]` not Map → failed; `payload.error` not a Map → done; codes `TOO_MANY_FOLLOWS`, `UNVERIFIED_EMAIL`, `UNVERIFIED_PHONE`, `UNVERIFIED_PHONE_AND_EMAIL`, `FORBIDDEN` → matching values; other code → refused.
`GiftSettings { bool followedChannelsOnly; bool countHidden }`, `copyWith`, `fromSettings(map)` requires both bools (`giftsToFollowedChannelsOnly`, `isGiftCountHidden`), value equality.

### lib/models/gif_picker.dart

- `GifPickerConfig { bool enabled; bool allowlisted; String apiKey; String rating }`, `canSend = enabled && allowlisted && apiKey.isNotEmpty`. `fromConfig(map)`: `isEnabled`, `isAllowlisted` must be bools (else null); `apiKey` kept when matching `^[A-Za-z0-9]{8,64}$` else `''`; `rating = giphyRating(contentRating)` = `'pg-13'` when trimmed upper-case equals `PG_13`, else `'pg'`. The api key is Twitch's Giphy key handed to the viewer for the GIF picker (GiphyService uses it).
- `GiphyGif { String id; String title; String url; String previewUrl; int width; int height }`, `fromJson(json)`: `id` `^[A-Za-z0-9]{1,64}$`; `images.original.url` → url, `images.fixed_height.url` → previewUrl, `fixed_height.width/height` parsed ints > 0; URLs must be https on `giphy.com` or `*.giphy.com`; title String or `''`.
- `enum GifSendStatus { sent, cooldown, refused, integrityRefused, signedOut, failed }`; `GifSendResult(status, {secondsUntilCanSend = 0})`, `fromBody`: errors → integrityRefused/failed; `data.sendGifMessage` Map required; `secondsUntilCanSend` num > 0 → ceil; non-null `error` → cooldown (with seconds) if seconds > 0 else refused; `message.id` String → sent, else failed.

### lib/models/unban_request.dart

- `UnbanRequestState { bool enabled; bool? canRequest; String? status; DateTime? createdAt; String? moderatorNote }`; statuses allowed `PENDING, APPROVED, DENIED, ACKNOWLEDGED, CANCELED` (others → null). `hasRequest = status != null && createdAt != null`; `canSend = enabled && canRequest == true && status != 'PENDING'`. `fromBody(body)`: `data.channel` Map (else null); `unbanRequestsSettings.isEnabled == true`; `self.canRequestUnban` bool; `self.lastUnbanRequest.{status, createdAt, resolverMessage}` (note trimmed, blank → null).
- `enum UnbanRequestResult { sent, refused, signedOut, failed }`; `UnbanRequestOutcome(result, {String? code})`, `fromBody`: `data.createUnbanRequest` Map required; `error` Map → refused with `error.code`; `unbanRequest` Map → sent; else failed. Shown on the "You are banned" chat banner with a request form.

### lib/models/community_goal.dart

- `enum CommunityGoalStatus { unstarted, started, ended, fulfilled, archived, unknown }` (`UNSTARTED`, `STARTED`, `ENDED`, `FULFILLED`, `ARCHIVED`, other → unknown).
- `enum GoalContributionResult { contributed, notEnoughPoints, maxPerStream, notOpen, refused, integrityRefused, signedOut, failed }`, `fromBody`: errors → integrityRefused/failed; `data.contributeCommunityPointsCommunityGoal` Map required; no `error` Map → contributed; codes `NOT_ENOUGH_POINTS`, `MAX_PER_STREAM`, `NOT_CURRENTLY_REDEEMABLE`|`NOT_FOUND` → notOpen, other → refused.
- `CommunityGoal { String id; String title; String description = ''; CommunityGoalStatus status = unknown; int amountNeeded; int pointsContributed = 0; int smallContribution = 0; int perStreamMaximum = 0; bool isInStock = false; Uri? imageUrl; int? color (ARGB); int contributedThisStream = 0 }`. Getters: `remaining = max(0, needed − contributed)`; `percentRaised` = `contributed*100 ~/ needed` clamped 0–100 (0 when needed ≤ 0); `progress` = ratio clamped 0–1; `isOpen = status == started && isInStock && remaining > 0`; `isMaxedThisStream = perStreamMaximum > 0 && contributedThisStream >= perStreamMaximum`; `maxContribution({balance})` = max(0, min(balance, streamLeft, remaining)) where streamLeft = perStreamMaximum − contributedThisStream when a maximum exists, else remaining. `withContributedThisStream(points)` copy.
- `listFrom(body)`: errors → null; channel = `data.user.channel` when user is a Map else `data.channel`; `communityPointsSettings.goals` List (else null); parsed goals EXCLUDING unstarted and archived.
- `contributionsFrom(body)`: `data.user.channel.self.communityPoints.goalContributions[]` → `{goal.id: userPointsContributedThisStream.toInt()}`.
- `tryParse(raw)`: non-empty `id`, `amountNeeded` num > 0 required; `title`/`description` trimmed; `pointsContributed`, `smallContribution`, `perStreamUserMaximumContribution` via `_count` (num > 0 → int else 0); `isInStock == true`; image = `image.url2x ?? image.url` (https) else `defaultImage`; `backgroundColor` `#RRGGBB` → `0xFF000000 | rgb`.

### lib/models/drop_campaign.dart

- `enum DropState { unknown, locked, inProgress, claimable, claimed }`.
- `enum DropClaimResult { claimed, alreadyClaimed, needsAccountLink, expired, limitReached, refused, integrityRefused, signedOut, failed }`, `isClaimed` (claimed|alreadyClaimed). `fromBody`: errors → integrityRefused/failed; `data.claimDropRewards` Map; `isUserAccountConnected == false` → needsAccountLink; status `ELIGIBLE_FOR_ALL`→claimed, `DROP_INSTANCE_ALREADY_CLAIMED`→alreadyClaimed, `EXPIRED`→expired, `ENTITLEMENT_LIMIT_REACHED`→limitReached, else refused.
- `enum CommunityDropsOfferKind { watch, subs, turbo, several }`; `CommunityDropsOffer` const ctors `.watch(minutes)`, `.subs(count)`, `.turbo()` (amount 0), `.several()` (amount 0); equality on kind+amount; `toString` `'CommunityDropsOffer(<kind>, <amount>)'`.
- `DropProgress { int minutesWatched = 0; int subs = 0; bool preconditionsMet = true; bool isClaimed = false; String? dropInstanceId }`, `tryParse(self)`: `currentMinutesWatched`, `currentSubs` (count), `hasPreconditionsMet != false`, `isClaimed == true`, `dropInstanceID` non-empty.
- `TimedDrop { String id; String rewardName; Uri? rewardImageUrl; int requiredMinutes = 0; int requiredSubs = 0; int requiredTurboSubs = 0; DropProgress? progress }`. `state`: no progress → unknown; claimed → claimed; instance id → claimable; preconditions not met → locked; else inProgress. `fraction`: 0 without progress; 1 when claimed/claimable; minutes ratio if requiredMinutes > 0; else subs ratio; else 0 (clamped). `minutesLeft` = clamp(required − watched, 0, required). `tryParse`: non-empty `id`; `benefitEdges[0].benefit.name` (trimmed) and `imageAssetURL` (Uri.tryParse, any scheme); `requiredMinutesWatched`, `requiredSubs`, `requiredTurboSubs`; `self` → progress.
- `DropCampaign { String id; String name; String gameName = ''; Uri? imageUrl; Uri? accountLinkUrl; Uri? detailsUrl; DateTime? endAt; bool? isAccountConnected; List<TimedDrop> drops = [] }`. `offer`: not exactly one drop → several; turbo subs → turbo; minutes only → watch(minutes); subs only → subs(count); else several. `listFrom(body)`: `data.user.channel` Map (else null); `viewerDropCampaigns` null → null when errors present else `[]`; non-List → null. `tryParse`: `imageURL`, `accountLinkURL`, `detailsURL` https-only; `endAt` parsed; `self.isAccountConnected` bool; `timeBasedDrops[]`.

### lib/models/weekly_rewards.dart

- `WeeklyRewardTier { int tier; int channelPoints; String? badgeUrl }`, `tryParse`: num `tier` required; `channelPoints` num or 0; `badge.image4x` https.
- `enum WeeklyRewardsStatus { earned, complete, progress }`.
- `WeeklyRewards { int daysVisitedThisWeek; int daysRequiredPerWeek; int accumulatedWeeks = 0; bool hasEarnedThisWeek = false; bool hasVisitedToday = false; WeeklyRewardTier? currentTier; List<WeeklyRewardTier> tiers = []; DateTime? endsAt; List<DateTime> weekResets = [] }`. `status`: complete if `isComplete`, else earned if hasEarnedThisWeek, else progress. `daysShown = clamp(daysVisited, 0, required)`. `isComplete = tiers non-empty && currentTier != null && currentTier.tier >= tiers.last.tier && hasEarnedThisWeek`. `fromBody`: `data.channel.self.weeklyVisitRewards` Map with `eventConfig` Map; `daysRequiredPerWeek` > 0 required; `rewardTiers` parsed and sorted by tier; `weekResetDates` Strings parsed → UTC, sorted; `endDate` → UTC; `daysVisitedThisWeek`, `accumulatedWeeks`, `hasEarnedWeeklyRewardThisWeek`, `hasVisitedToday`, `currentReward`.
- `CommunityMoment { String id; String? clipSlug }`, `fromEvent(data)`: `moment_id` matching `^[A-Za-z0-9_:\-]{1,128}$`, `clip_slug` non-empty (from PubSub/Hermes community-moment events).
- `enum MomentClaimResult { claimed, alreadyClaimed, expired, invalid, integrityRefused, signedOut, failed }`, `isClaimed`; `fromBody`: errors → integrityRefused/failed; `data.claimCommunityMoment` Map; `error` null → claimed if `moment` Map else failed; `ALREADY_CLAIMED`→alreadyClaimed, `CLAIM_PERIOD_EXPIRED`→expired, `INVALID_MOMENT_ID`→invalid, else failed.

### lib/models/profile_edit.dart

- `enum AccountEditOutcome { done, refused, integrityRefused, signedOut, failed }`.
- `enum ProfileImageKind { avatar, banner }`.
- `enum ProfileImageFormat { png('PNG','image/png'), jpg('JPG','image/jpeg'), gif('GIF','image/gif') }` with `gqlName`, `mimeType`; `ofFileName(name)` by lower-cased extension after the last `.`: `png`, `jpg`|`jpeg`, `gif`, else null.
- `ProfileImagePick { ProfileImageFile? file }` (wrapper for a picker result; null file = cancelled/refused).
- `ProfileImageFile { Uint8List bytes; ProfileImageFormat format }`, `maxBytes = 10 * 1024 * 1024`, `isAcceptable = bytes.isNotEmpty && bytes.length <= maxBytes`; `fromNamed(name, bytes)` → null for unknown extension or unacceptable size.
- `AccountEditResult<T> { AccountEditOutcome outcome; String? code; T? value }`, const named ctors `.failed()` and `.signedOut()`, `succeeded = outcome == done`.
- `EditableProfile { String id; String login; String displayName; String description; String? profileImageUrl; String? bannerImageUrl }`, constants `maxDescriptionLength = 300`, `maxSocialLinks = 5`. `tryParse(user)`: non-empty `id` and `login` required; displayName non-empty else login; description String or `''`; `profileImageURL`/`bannerImageURL` https-only.
- `LoginRenameStatus { bool eligible; DateTime? eligibleAt }`, `tryParse(data)`: `data.currentUser.loginRenameStatus` with bool `isEligible` (else null); `eligibleAt` via `DateTime.tryParse('${…}')`.
- `enum UsernameAvailability { available, taken, unknown }`.
- `SocialLink { String id; String title; String url }`, `maxTitleLength = 25`. `listFrom(channel)`: `channel.socialMedias` must be a List (else null); items with String `id`, String valid `url`; title = non-empty `title` else the URL's host. `isValidUrl(url)`: trimmed parse, scheme `https` or `http`, host contains a `.`, no userinfo.
Rust: plain structs + `enum`s; `bytes::Bytes` for image data.

### lib/models/onsite_notification.dart

`OnsiteNotification { String id; String body; DateTime createdAt (local); bool isRead; String? category; String? creatorName; String? avatarUrl; String? thumbnailUrl; String? channelLogin; Uri? link }`. Constants: `maxBodyLength = 500`, login `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$`, markup `<[^>]*>`. `read()` / `unread()` return copies with `isRead` true/false. `fromJson(json)`: `id` non-empty ≤ 256 chars; `body` String with every `<…>` tag removed then trimmed, 1–500 chars; `createdAt` parseable (converted `.toLocal()`); `isRead == true`; `category` non-empty; `creatorName` = first `creators[].displayName` when non-empty ≤ 64; `avatarUrl` = that creator's `profileImageURL`, `thumbnailUrl` = `thumbnailURL` — both only when https AND host ends with `.jtvnw.net` or `.twitch.tv`; `channelLogin` = `extra.login` lower-cased only when `extra.__typename == 'User'` and login valid; `link` = first `actions[].url` that is https on `twitch.tv` or `*.twitch.tv` (opened in-app by the deep-link router).
`enum NotificationPlatform { push, email }`; `NotificationCategorySetting { String category; Map<NotificationPlatform,bool> platforms }`, `fromJson`: `category` `^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]{1,64}$`, `platforms` List; each with `platformName` `push`/`email` and bool `isEnabled`; neither platform present → null.

### lib/models/recommended_channel.dart

`RecommendedChannel { String userId; String login (lower-case); String displayName; String? avatarUrl; String? title; String? category; int? viewers }`, `isLive = viewers != null`. `fromItem(item)`: `__typename == 'PersonalSectionChannel'`; `user.id` `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, `user.login` `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$`; displayName non-empty ≤ 64 else login; `content` used only when `__typename == 'Stream'`: `viewersCount` int ≥ 0, `broadcaster.broadcastSettings.title` (trimmed, non-blank), `game.displayName`; `profileImageURL` https on `*.jtvnw.net` only.
`enum RecommendationSection { recommended('RECOMMENDED_SECTION'), similar('SIMILAR_SECTION') }` (`apiValue`). `enum NotInterestedReason { notInterested, offensive, other }`, `apiValue` = camelCase → SCREAMING_SNAKE (`NOT_INTERESTED`, `OFFENSIVE`, `OTHER`).

### lib/models/whisper_history.dart

- `WhisperPeer { String id; String login (lower-case); String displayName }`, `fromJson`: id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, login `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$`, displayName non-empty ≤ 64 else login.
- `WhisperHistoryMessage { String id; String fromId; String text; DateTime sentAt (local) }`, `maxLength = 2000`; `fromJson`: id non-empty ≤ 128; `from` a valid peer; `sentAt` parseable; `content.content` String, non-blank, ≤ 2000 chars (else null). Emote ranges in `content.emotes` are not parsed (text only).
- `WhisperHistoryThread { String id; WhisperPeer peer; WhisperHistoryMessage? last; int unread; bool archived; bool muted }`, `fromJson(json, {me})`: id `^[0-9]{1,20}_[0-9]{1,20}$`; `participants` List → first valid peer whose id ≠ `me` (else null); `messages.edges[0].node` → last; `unreadMessagesCount` int > 0 else 0; `isArchived`, `isMuted` (`== true`).
- `WhisperPrivacy { bool blockStrangers; bool banned = false; bool disabledByParent = false }`, `fromSettings`: bool `isBlockingWhispersFromStrangers` required; `isWhisperBanned`, `isWhispersDisabledByParent`.

### lib/models/channel_leaderboards.dart

`enum LeaderboardKind { cheers, gifts }`; `enum LeaderboardPeriod { day, week, month, allTime, unknown }`. `LeaderboardEntry { int rank; int score; String userId; String displayName }`, `tryParse(node)`: int `rank`, int `score`, `user` Map with String `id` and non-empty `displayName`. `ChannelLeaderboards { LeaderboardPeriod period = unknown; LeaderboardKind? preferred; Map<LeaderboardKind, List<LeaderboardEntry>> boards = {}; Map<LeaderboardKind, LeaderboardEntry> mine = {} }`; `kinds` = enum order filtered to present boards; `isEmpty` = every board list empty. `tryParse(body)`: body Map with `errors == null`; `data.user` Map; `user.channel.leaderboardSet` Map; config = `user.settings.leaderboard` (or `{}`); enabled: cheers unless `isCheerEnabled == false`, gifts unless `isSubGiftEnabled == false`; field names cheers→`bits`, gifts→`subGift` (the clip board is fetched but ignored); each enabled board Map → entries from `items.edges[].node`, `myPosition` → mine. `timePeriod` `DAY`/`WEEK`/`MONTH`/`ALLTIME` → period; `defaultLeaderboard` `BITS`→cheers, `SUBGIFT`→gifts.

### lib/models/emote_traits.dart

Constants: prefix `https://static-cdn.jtvnw.net/emoticons/v2/`; id pattern `^(emotesv2_[0-9a-f]{32}|[0-9]{1,20})(_[A-Z]{2})?$`; `emoteModifierCodes = {'BW','HF','SG','SQ','TK'}` (black&white, horizontal flip, sunglasses, squish, thinking). `twitchEmoteIdOf(url)`: id = path segment after the prefix up to the next `/`, returned when it matches the pattern, else null (non-Twitch URLs → null). `emoteModifierOf(url)`: the `_XX` suffix's code if it is one of the modifier codes, else null. `bitsThresholdFrom(body)`: `data.emote.bitsBadgeTierSummary.threshold` int > 0, else null.

### lib/models/legendary_collection.dart

`LegendaryEmote { String id; String token; String tier; bool isOwned }`, id `^[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,80}$`; `imageUrl = https://static-cdn.jtvnw.net/emoticons/v2/<id>/default/dark/2.0`; `tryParse(raw)`: `raw.emote.{id, token}` Strings with valid id; `tier` String or `''`; `isOwned == true`. `LegendaryCollection { String campaignName; String? imageUrl; List<LegendaryEmote> emotes = []; int unopenedChests = 0; int freeChests = 0 }`; `paidChests = unopened − free`; `ownedIds`; `newlyOwnedSince(before)` = owned now and not owned in `before` (reveal after opening chests); `tierOrder = ['LEGENDARY','EPIC','RARE','UNCOMMON','COMMON']`; `ownedCount`; `byTier` = groups sorted by tierOrder index (unknown tiers last). `fromBody(body)`: `data.legendaryEmoteSeason` Map; `campaign.name`, `campaign.imageURL` (https); `emotePool` parsed, empty → null; `userProgress.chestInventory[]`: for each `unopenedCount` num > 0 add to unopened, and to free when `chestType == 'FREE'`.

### lib/models/live_together.dart

`enum LiveTogetherKind { costream, guestStar }`. `LiveTogetherMember { String id; String login; String displayName; String? imageUrl; int? viewers }`, `isLive = viewers != null`; `tryParse`: id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, login `^[a-z0-9_]{1,25}$`, displayName non-empty else login, `profileImageURL` https; `viewers` = `stream.viewersCount` int ≥ 0, or 0 when a `stream` Map exists without a count, or null when no stream (offline). `LiveTogether { LiveTogetherKind kind; LiveTogetherMember? host; List<LiveTogetherMember> members; int? totalCount; int? totalViewers }`. `othersThan(channelId)` = host then members excluding the current channel, de-duplicated by id (first wins). `hiddenCount(channelId)` = `totalCount − (others + 1)` when positive, else 0 (0 when totalCount null). `fromCostream(body)`: `data.user.stream.costreamDetails` Map; members from `topCostreamers`, host from `organizer`; neither → null; `costreamersCount` int > 0, `totalViewersCount` int ≥ 0. `fromGuestStar(body)`: `data.channel.guestStarSessionCall.guests[].user` members (none → null), host = `channel.owner`. Shown as the "Live together" strip on the channel screen.

### lib/models/resub_notification.dart

`ResubNotification { int months; String tokenId; bool isGift = false }`; `tryParse(body)`: Map with `errors == null`; `data.user.self.resubNotification` Map; `token` matching `^[A-Za-z0-9_:\-]{1,128}$`; `months` int ≥ 1; `isGiftSubscription == true`. Drives the "Share your resub (N months)" prompt in chat.

### lib/models/shoutout_profile.dart

`ShoutoutCategory { String id; String name; String? boxArtUrl }`; `ShoutoutSchedule { String? title; DateTime startAt; List<String> categories = [] }`; `ShoutoutProfile { String id; String login; String displayName; String? profileImageUrl; List<ShoutoutCategory> categories = []; ShoutoutSchedule? nextStream; bool? following }`, `withFollowing(bool?)` copy. `tryParse(body)`: Map with no `errors`; `data.user` Map with String `id`, String `displayName`, `channel.name` String matching `^[a-z0-9_]{1,25}$`; displayName empty → login; `profileImageURL` https with host; categories from `channel.home.shelves.categoryShelf.edges[].node` with String `id` and non-empty `displayName` (+ https `boxArtURL`); nextStream from `channel.schedule.nextSegment` when not `isCancelled == true` and `startAt` parses (title trimmed non-blank, category display names). `followingIn(body)` → null if errors/no `data.user.self`; else true iff `self.follower.followedAt` is a String. Rendered as the shoutout card when a streamer runs `/shoutout`.

### lib/models/unlockable_emote.dart

`UnlockableEmote { String id; String token }`, `modifiers = ['BW','HF','SG','SQ','TK']`, id `^[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,80}$`; `imageUrl = https://static-cdn.jtvnw.net/emoticons/v2/<id>/default/dark/2.0`; `modifiedId(m) = '<id>_<m>'`; `modifiedImageUrl(m)` same URL with the modified id. `listFrom(body)`: `data.user` Map (else null); `subscriptionProducts` not a List → `[]`; only products with `tier == '1000'` (Tier 1) — their emotes with valid id and String token, de-duplicated by id. Shown in the emote picker as locked "subscribe to unlock" emotes (and their modifier variants).

### lib/models/community_points.dart

- `PointsReward { String id; String title; int cost; bool isEnabled = true; bool requiresInput = false; Color? backgroundColor; String? imageUrl; String? automaticType }`. Getters: `isAutomatic = automaticType != null`; `isRedeemable = !isAutomatic` (built-ins need a picker; listed but not tappable unless the emote-unlock flow handles them); `affordableWith(balance)` = balance non-null and ≥ cost.
  - `tryParse(json)` (custom reward): String `id` and `title` required; `cost` num→int else 0; `isEnabled` bool else true; `isUserInputRequired` → requiresInput (default false); `backgroundColor` via `_hexColor` (strip `#`, trim, exactly 6 hex digits → `Color(0xFF000000 | value)`, else null); `image.url`.
  - `tryParseAutomatic(json)`: String `id` and `type` required; `cost` null → returns null (power-ups bought with bits come with `cost: null`; reading them as 0 would list fake free rewards); title = `automaticRewardTitle(type)`; image = `defaultImage ?? image` `.url`; `automaticType = type`.
  - `automaticRewardTitle(type, [l])`: `SEND_HIGHLIGHTED_MESSAGE`→kind `highlight`, `RANDOM_SUB_EMOTE_UNLOCK`→`randomEmote`, `CHOSEN_SUB_EMOTE_UNLOCK`→`chosenEmote`, `CHOSEN_MODIFIED_SUB_EMOTE_UNLOCK`→`modifiedEmote`, `SINGLE_MESSAGE_BYPASS_SUB_MODE`→`subOnlyMessage` → localized `pointsAutomaticReward(type: kind)` (ICU select); unknown type → lower-cased with `_` replaced by spaces. `titleIn(l)` = automatic title in `l`'s language or the streamer's title.
- `PredictionOutcome { String id; String title; String color ('BLUE'|'PINK', default 'BLUE'); int totalPoints = 0; int totalUsers = 0 }`; `odds(potTotal)` = `potTotal / totalPoints` or null when either ≤ 0 (no "1x" before bets). `tryParse` requires String id/title.
- `Prediction { String id; String title; String status (ACTIVE|LOCKED|RESOLVED|CANCELED, default 'ACTIVE'); List<PredictionOutcome> outcomes = []; String? creatorId; String? creatorName }`; `isOpen` (ACTIVE), `isLocked` (LOCKED), `totalPoints`, `totalUsers` sums. `tryParse`: id/title required; `createdBy.id/displayName`; outcomes parsed.
- `CommunityPointsContext { int? balance; String? pointsName; String? pointsImageUrl; List<PointsReward> rewards = []; List<Prediction> predictions = []; String? availableClaimId }`, `static const empty`; `hasClaim`; `hasAnything = balance != null || rewards/predictions non-empty`; `activePrediction` = first open, else first locked, else null; `pointsImageUrlAt(scale)`: scale 1 → url; otherwise replaces a trailing `icon-1.png` with `icon-4.png` (scale 4) or `icon-2.png` (anything else); non-matching URLs unchanged (verified: icon-1/2/4 exist, icon-3 is 404).
- `emoteUnlockRewardTypes = {'RANDOM_SUB_EMOTE_UNLOCK','CHOSEN_SUB_EMOTE_UNLOCK','CHOSEN_MODIFIED_SUB_EMOTE_UNLOCK'}`; `unlocksEmotes(type)`.
- `enum PointsActionStatus { success, integrityRefused, notAuthenticated, network, unknown }`; `PointsActionResult(status, [rawCode])`, `ok`, `busyCode = 'busy'` (ours: another action running, nothing sent), `isSuccess`. `fromError(code)`: null → ok; lower-cased contains `integrity` → integrityRefused; contains `unauthenticated`/`unauthorized`/`login` → notAuthenticated; exactly `request_failed` → network; else unknown (raw code kept). `text(l)`: unknown + busy → `l.pointsBusy`; else `l.pointsResult(status: status.name, reason: rawCode ?? l.pointsNoReason)`; `message` = text in current locale.
- `enum PredictionRestriction { none, moderator, regionLocked, categoryLocked }`; `predictionRestrictionOf(raw)`: `CAN_MANAGE_PREDICTIONS`→moderator, `REGION_LOCKED`→regionLocked, `CATEGORY_REGION_LOCKED`→categoryLocked, else none.
- `PredictionAccess { PredictionRestriction restriction = none; bool? hasAcceptedTerms; bool temporaryBadgeEnabled = true }`, `unknown`; `isSpectator` (region or category locked), `needsTerms = hasAcceptedTerms == false`; `withTermsAccepted()`, `withRestriction(v)`. `fromBody`: `data` Map (else unknown); `currentUser.predictionsSettings.{hasAcceptedTOS, isTemporaryChatBadgeEnabled (default true)}`; `predictionEvent.self.restriction`.
- `predictionRefusalKind(code)`: `MUST_ACCEPT_TOS`→`mustAcceptTerms`, `REGION_LOCKED`→`regionLocked`, `CATEGORY_REGION_LOCKED`→`categoryLocked`, `EVENT_MANAGER`→`manager`, `SPECTATOR_MODE_DUPLICATE`→`spectatorDuplicate`, `SPECTATOR_MODE_INELIGIBLE`→`spectatorIneligible`, `NOT_ENOUGH_POINTS`→`notEnoughPoints`, `EVENT_NOT_ACTIVE`→`notActive`, `MAX_POINTS_PER_EVENT`→`maxPoints`, `MULTIPLE_OUTCOMES`→`multipleOutcomes`, else `other` (keys of an ICU select message for the bet sheet).
Rust: `Color` as `u32` ARGB; localization through the PC i18n layer with the same keys.

### lib/models/stream.dart

json_serializable (`createToJson: false`, `fieldRename: snake`; generated `stream.g.dart` — never hand-edited). `StreamTwitch` (Helix `/streams` item) positional ctor fields, JSON keys and types (all required/non-null unless noted; a missing required key throws a cast error):
| field | JSON key | type | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| userId | `user_id` | String | — |
| userLogin | `user_login` | String | — |
| userName | `user_name` | String (display name) | — |
| gameId | `game_id` | String | — |
| gameName | `game_name` | String | — |
| title | `title` | String | — |
| viewerCount | `viewer_count` | num → int | — |
| startedAt | `started_at` | String (ISO-8601, kept as text) | — |
| thumbnailUrl | `thumbnail_url` | String template with `{width}x{height}` | — |
| tags | `tags` | List<String> (≤10) | `[]` when absent/null |
| language | `language` | String (ISO 639-1, `other`, or '') | `''` |
| id | `id` | String broadcast id (the archive VOD's `broadcast { id }`) | `''` |
`StreamsTwitch { List<StreamTwitch> data; Map<String,String> pagination }` — `pagination` must be a map (Helix sends `{}` or `{"cursor": "…"}`); `data` items parsed with `StreamTwitch.fromJson`. Rust: `#[derive(Deserialize)] #[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]` with `#[serde(default)]` on tags/language/id and `pagination: HashMap<String,String>`.

### lib/models/community_highlights.dart

Helpers and models for the "community highlights" stack above chat (polls, predictions, pinned messages, outgoing raids), parsed from anonymous GQL (camelCase) and Hermes PubSub (snake_case). Never throws.
**Helpers (public, used across many parsers):**
- `chField(map, camel, [snake])` = `map[camel]` if non-null or no snake key given, else `map[snake]`.
- `chMap(v)` = v if Map else null. `chList(v, {max = 20})` = list truncated to `max` items, else `[]`.
- `chString(v, {max = 500})`: non-empty String; if longer than `max` UTF-16 units cut to `max` (one less if the last kept unit is a high surrogate, so a character is never split); then `chWellFormed`; empty → null. `chWellFormed(text)` replaces every unpaired surrogate by U+FFFD. (Rust strings are always valid UTF-8, so the PC port only needs "truncate to N UTF-16 units without splitting a char"; compute via `encode_utf16`.)
- `chInt(v)`: finite num → int; String → `int.tryParse(trim)`; else null.
- `chSeconds(v, {max})` = `Duration(seconds: clamp(chInt(v), 0, max))`.
- `chDate(v)`: number or numeric string → if `|n| < 1e12` treat as seconds (×1000) else milliseconds; must be within [2000-01-01 (946684800000 ms), 2200-01-01 (7258118400000 ms)) → UTC DateTime; other String → `DateTime.tryParse`, year in [2000, 2200) → `.toUtc()`; else null.
- Private: `_maxWindow = 1 day`, `_maxText = 500`, `_maxFragments = 200`, id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, login `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$`.

**Polls**: `enum PollStatus { active, completed, terminated, archived, unknown }` (`ACTIVE`/`COMPLETED`/`TERMINATED`/`ARCHIVED`). `PollChoice { String id; String title; int votes; int voters }`. `Poll { String id; String title; PollStatus status; List<PollChoice> choices; Duration duration; int totalVotes; int totalVoters; String? channelId; DateTime? startedAt; DateTime? endedAt; Duration? remaining; bool multiChoice = false }`, `isActive`, `endsAt = startedAt + duration`. `fromGql` (id key `id`) / `fromPubsub` (id key `poll_id`): id ≤64 required; choices: id (`id` for GQL / `choice_id`, falling back to `choice_id`) ≤64, title ≤100, `votes.total`, `totalVoters|total_voters`; `title` ≤200; `durationSeconds|duration_seconds` clamped to 1 day (else zero); totalVotes = `votes.total` or sum of choice votes; `totalVoters|total_voters`; `owned_by` → channelId (≤20); `startedAt|started_at`, `endedAt|ended_at`; `remainingDurationMilliseconds|remaining_duration_milliseconds` kept when 0…86 400 000; `settings.multichoice|multi_choice .isEnabled|is_enabled == true`.

**Predictions** (highlight version, distinct from community_points'): `enum PredictionStatus { active, locked, resolvePending, resolved, cancelPending, canceled, unknown }` (`ACTIVE`, `LOCKED`, `RESOLVE_PENDING`, `RESOLVED`, `CANCEL_PENDING`, `CANCELED`|`CANCELLED`). `enum PredictionColor { blue, pink, unknown }`. `TopPredictor { String name; int points; int? pointsWon }`. `PredictionOutcome { String id; String title; PredictionColor color; int points; int users; List<TopPredictor> topPredictors }`. `Prediction { String id; String title; PredictionStatus status; List<PredictionOutcome> outcomes; Duration window; String? channelId; DateTime? createdAt; DateTime? lockedAt; DateTime? endedAt; String? winningOutcomeId }`, `locksAt = createdAt + window`, `totalPoints`. `fromGql`/`fromPubsub` share `_parse`: id ≤64; outcomes: id, title ≤100, color, `totalPoints|total_points`, `totalUsers|total_users`, `topPredictors|top_predictors[]` → name = `user.displayName` ?? `user_display_name` ?? '' (≤64), `points`, `result.points_won`; window = `predictionWindowSeconds|prediction_window_seconds` clamped to 1 day; `channel_id`; `createdAt|created_at`, `lockedAt|locked_at`, `endedAt|ended_at`; winner = `winning_outcome_id` ?? `winningOutcome.id`.

**Pinned messages**: `PinnedBadge { String setId; String version }`; `PinnedFragment { String text; String? emoteId }`; `PinnedChatMessage { String id (pin id); String? messageId; String? senderId; String senderLogin; String senderDisplayName; String? color ('#RRGGBB' unchecked); List<PinnedBadge> badges; String text (≤500); List<PinnedFragment> fragments; String? pinnedBy; DateTime? startsAt; DateTime? updatedAt; DateTime? endsAt (null = until unpinned) }`.
- `fromGql(node)` (edge node of `pinnedChatMessages`): `id` and `pinnedMessage` Map required; fragments from `pinnedMessage.content.fragments` (≤200), each text cut so the running total ≤ 500; emoteId when `fragment.content.__typename == 'Emote'` (`emoteID` ?? `id`); sender `id` (≤20), `login` (≤25), `displayName` (≤64), `chatColor` (≤7); badges `sender.displayBadges[]` with `setID` (version default `'1'`); text = `content.text` ?? joined fragments ?? ''; `pinnedBy.displayName`; `startsAt`, `updatedAt`, `endsAt` on the edge node.
- `fromPubsub(node)` (Hermes `pin-message` data): `id` and `message` Map; fragments' emote from `emote` or `emoticon` (`id` ?? `emoticon_id` ?? `emote_id`); sender `display_name`, `chat_color`; badges `sender.badges[]` with `id` → setId; `pinned_by.display_name`; dates `message.starts_at`/`updated_at`/`ends_at`.
