**Outgoing raids**: `OutgoingRaid { String id; String sourceId; String targetId; String targetLogin; String targetDisplayName; int viewerCount; String? targetProfileImage; int? targetViewerCount; DateTime? createdAt; int forceRaidNowSeconds = 90 }`, `goAt = createdAt + forceRaidNowSeconds`. `fromPubsub`: `id`, numeric `source_id`/`target_id`, valid `target_login` required; `target_display_name` ?? login; `viewer_count`; `target_profile_image` https (≤300); `target_viewer_count`; `raid_created_at`; `force_raid_now_seconds` clamped to 10 min else 90. Drives the "Raiding <target> in N s — Join" banner (TwitchViewerActionsService.joinRaid).

### lib/models/channel_profile.dart

Profile page parsers (tolerant; null only when the channel is missing). Private helpers: `_imageHosts = {'static-cdn.jtvnw.net', 'panels.twitch.tv'}`; `_image(v)` = chString ≤400, https, host in that set, no userinfo, no explicit port → normalized URL; `_link(v)` = chString ≤1000 trimmed, http/https with host; `_exact(v, max)` = String of length ≤ max (longer refused, never cut) → chString; `_color(v)` = ≤7 chars, `#` stripped, `^[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$` → `0xFFRRGGBB`.
- `kPanelMarkdownMaxDepth = 4`. `_flattenMarkdown(text)`: per line, the leading run of block markers (`>`, `-`, `*`, `+`, `1.`/`1)` up to 9 digits, with spaces/tabs; regex `^(?:[ \t]*(?:>|[-*+]|\d{1,9}[.)])[ \t]*)+`) is cut after the 4th marker; the rest of the line has `> * + - . )` backslash-escaped (deep nesting froze Markdown layout).
- `enum BroadcasterRole { partner, affiliate, none }`.
- `SocialLink { String name (lower-case site key e.g. twitter, discord); String title; String url }`, `fromGql`: valid `_link(url)` required; name ≤40 lower-cased or ''; title ≤60 ?? name.
- `ChannelPanel { String id; String? title; String? imageUrl; String? linkUrl; String? description; String? altText }`, `fromGql`: id ≤64 and `type == 'DEFAULT'` (extension panels skipped); title ≤120; `_image(imageURL)`; `_link(linkURL)`; description ≤5000 flattened; altText ≤300; dropped if title, image and description are all null.
- `LastBroadcast { String? title; DateTime? startedAt; String? gameId; String? gameName }`, `fromGql`: title ≤300, `chDate(startedAt)`, game id exact ≤20 digits only, `game.displayName` ≤100; null when both title and startedAt null.
- `LiveStatus { bool isRerun; int viewers; DateTime? startedAt; String? gameName }`, `fromGql(stream)`: requires `id` (≤32); `type == 'rerun'`; `viewersCount` clamped 0…2^31; `createdAt` (uptime); `game.displayName`.
- `ChannelProfile { String id; String login; String displayName; String? description; DateTime? createdAt; String? profileImageUrl; String? bannerUrl; String? offlineImageUrl; int? accentColor; BroadcasterRole role = none; int? followers; List<SocialLink> socials = []; List<ChannelPanel> panels = []; LastBroadcast? lastBroadcast; LiveStatus? live (null offline) }`, `fromGql(user)`: `id` exact ≤20 all digits, `login` exact ≤25 `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$` (else null); displayName ≤64 ?? login; description ≤1000; images via `_image`; `primaryColorHex` → accent; role partner > affiliate > none; `followers.totalCount`; `channel.socialMedias` (≤20); `panels` (≤40).
- `ScheduleSegment { String id; DateTime startAt; DateTime? endAt; String? title; bool isCancelled = false; String? categoryName }`, `fromGql`: id ≤200 and startAt required; title ≤140; first `categories[].name` ≤100.
- `ScheduleExtras { DateTime? vacationStart; DateTime? vacationEnd; Set<DateTime> recurringStarts = {} }`, `none`; `isRecurring(segment)` = recurringStarts contains `segment.startAt.toUtc()`; `onVacationAfter(now)` = start non-null and end after now. `fromHelix(json)` (Helix `/schedule`): `data.vacation.start_time/end_time`; `data.segments[]` (≤100) with `is_recurring == true` → `start_time` UTC.
- `ChannelSchedule { List<ScheduleSegment> segments (sorted by start); DateTime? nextStartAt }`, `empty`; `fromGql(schedule)` (null → empty): segments (≤100) sorted; `nextSegment.startAt`. `upcoming(now)` = segments whose end (or start + 4 h when no end) is after now.

### lib/models/hype_train.dart

Hype-train data from GQL (camelCase) and realtime Hermes events (mostly snake_case, sometimes both). Tolerant parsing: wrong/missing fields get defaults, never exceptions.

**Field helpers (public)**: `htField(map, keys)` = first non-null value among `keys`; `htString` (String or null); `htInt` via `_int` (finite num with |v| ≤ `_maxInt = 2^40` → int, else null — guards sums from garbage); `htBool` (`== true`); `htMap` (Map or null); `htList` (List truncated to `_maxListLength = 200`, else `[]`); `htDate(map, keys)`: ISO String → `tryParse().toUtc()`, or epoch-milliseconds num within [2000-01-01, 2100-01-01]; result outside 2000–2100 → null (Go zero time `0001-01-01` = unset).

**Enums & mappers**: `HtVariant { regular, goldenKappa, treasure, community, mythic }` (piñata renders as regular). `HtAthState { none, approaching, reached, justReached }` via `htAthState`: `APPROACHING`, `REACHED`, `JUST_REACHED`. `HtSource { bits, subs, explicitPurchase, admin, raid, unknown }` via `htSource`: `BITS`, `SUBS`, `EXPLICIT_PURCHASE`, `ADMIN`, `RAID`. `HtEndReason { completed, expired, inProgress, unknown }` via `htEndReason`: `COMPLETED`, `EXPIRED`, `IN_PROGRESS`. `HtUnlockStatus { none, approaching, reached }` via `htUnlockStatus`: `APPROACHING`, `REACHED`. `HtRewardType { emote, badge, unknown }`.

**`HtAction(String raw)`** (value equality on raw, `toString` `'HtAction(<raw>)'`): `isCheer` for raw in {`CHEER`, `POWER_CHEER`, `BITS_ON_EXTENSION`, `BITS_EXTENSION`, `POLLS`, `POLL`}; `subTier` 1/2/3 for `TIER_n_SUB` or `TIER_n_GIFTED_SUB`; `isGift` = ends with `_GIFTED_SUB`.

**`HtReward { HtRewardType type; String id; String? token; String? imageUrl; String? setId }`**: `parse(raw)` accepts realtime flat `{type, id, token}` and GQL `{id, type, emote{id token}}` / `{badge{id setID imageURL}}`; type from `type` or `__typename` (`EMOTE`/`HypeTrainEmoteReward` → emote, `BADGE`/`HypeTrainBadgeReward` → badge, else inferred from which sub-map exists); id = `emote.id` ?? `badge.id` ?? `id`/`badge_id` (empty → null); token = `emote.token` ?? `token`; imageUrl = `badge.imageURL` ?? `image_url`/`imageURL`; setId = `badge.setID` ?? `set_id`/`setID`. `emoteUrl` = `https://static-cdn.jtvnw.net/emoticons/v2/<id>/default/dark/3.0` only for emote type and id `^[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,80}$`; `badgeUrl` = `htCdnUrl(imageUrl)`. `htCdnUrl(raw)` = https, no userinfo, no port, host exactly `static-cdn.jtvnw.net`, else null. `htRewards(iterable)` parses skipping nulls.

**`HtLevel { int value; int cumulativeGoal; List<HtReward> rewards }`**, `parse`: `value` default 1, `goal` → cumulativeGoal default 0 (train total at end of level), `rewards`.
**`HtProgress { HtLevel level; int goal (in-level); int value (in-level); int total (train running total); int? remainingSeconds; HtAthState athState = none }`**, `parse(raw)`: null unless `total` and `level.value` ≥ 1 exist (an empty progress must not reset the banner to level 1); goal/value/total clamped ≥ 0; value from `progression` or `value`; `remainingSeconds|remaining_seconds`; `allTimeHighState|all_time_high_state`. `copyWith({athState})`.
**`HtUser { String id; String login; String displayName; String? profileImageUrl }`**, `parse`: non-empty id; login default ''; `displayName|display_name` ?? login; `profileImageURL|profile_image_url` through htCdnUrl.
**`HtConductor { HtSource source; HtUser user; Map<String,int> participations }`**, `parse`: user required; realtime `participations` map `{"SOURCE.ACTION": qty}` (key's part after the last `.`), plus GQL `participation[{action, quantity}]`; quantities > 0 summed per action; `amount` = sum of all.
**`HtConfig { String? difficulty; int? maxLevel; Map<String,int> conversionRates; Map<HtSource,String> conductorBadges; HtReward? calloutEmote; int? kickoffMinPoints }`**, `parse` (non-Map → empty config): maxLevel from `difficultySettings` (a list) — the entry matching `difficulty` (or the first with a maxLevel when no difficulty); `participationConversionRates[]` action (≠ `UNKNOWN`) → value > 0 (e.g. TIER_1_SUB → 500, CHEER → 1); `conductorRewards[]` with `type == 'CURRENT'` → per source the first reward with a valid badge URL; callout emote from `calloutEmote{id token}` or flat `calloutEmoteId`/`calloutEmoteToken`; `kickoff.minPoints|min_points`.
**`HtTreasure { int discountPercentage; int threshold; HtUnlockStatus status }`**, `parse`: `discountLevelThreshold|discount_level_threshold` required; `discountPercentage|discount_percentage` default 0; status from `status|discount_status|discountStatus`.
**`HtCommunity { int current; int next; int previous; int threshold; HtUnlockStatus status; int? maxRewardLevel }`**, `parse(Map)`: `currentRewardAmount`, `nextRewardAmount`, `previousRewardAmount` (default 0), `rewardLevelThreshold` default 5, `status|rewardStatus`, `maxRewardLevel`.
**`HtMythic { HtReward? selectedEmote; List<HtReward> pool }`** from `selectedEmote{id token}` and `emotePool[]`.
**`HtAth { int level; int goal; int progression; int total }`** (record train), `parse`: `level.value` required.
**`HtSharedChannel { HtUser user; HtProgress? progress; HtAth? ath; List<HtConductor> conductors }`**; **`HtShared { String id; List<HtSharedChannel> channels }`**, `parse`: merges per user id from `sharedProgress[].{user, channelProgress}`, `sharedAllTimeHighRecords|channelAllTimeHighRecords[].{user, channelAllTimeHigh}`, `sharedConductors[].{user, conductors}`; none → null; id default ''.
**`htVariantOf(raw)`** precedence: Mythic (`variantTrainDetails.__typename == 'MythicTrainDetails'` or `hypeTrainType|hype_train_type == 'MYTHIC'`) > Community (`CommunityTrainDetails`/`COMMUNITY`) > Golden Kappa (`isGoldenKappaTrain|is_golden_kappa_train`, `GoldenKappaTrainDetails`, `GOLDEN_KAPPA`, `GOLDEN`) > Treasure (`isTreasureTrain|is_treasure_train`, `TreasureTrainDetails`, a `treasureTrainDetails` map, `TREASURE`) > regular.
**`HypeTrainExecution { String id; HtProgress progress; DateTime? startedAt; DateTime? expiresAt; DateTime? endedAt; HtEndReason endReason = inProgress; List<HtConductor> conductors; HtConfig config; HtAth? ath; HtVariant variant; HtTreasure? treasure; HtCommunity? community; HtMythic? mythic; HtShared? shared; bool isFastMode = false }`**, `level` getter, `copyWith(…)` (id/startedAt/isFastMode fixed). `parse(raw)` (GQL execution, level-up `hype_train`, realtime start payload): non-empty id and parseable progress required; reason from `endReason|endingReason|ending_reason`, absent → inProgress if no `endedAt|ended_at` else unknown; dates `startedAt|started_at`, `expiresAt|expires_at`; `allTimeHigh|all_time_high`; treasure from `treasureTrainDetails` or the variant details when typename is TreasureTrainDetails; community/mythic from variant details by typename; `sharedHypeTrainDetails`; `isFastMode|is_fast_mode`.
**`HypeTrainApproaching { String id; int goal; Map<int,int> deadlines; Set<String> participants; List<HtReward> levelOneRewards; HtVariant variant }`**, `parse`: id from `id|approaching_hype_train_id`; deadlines (events still needed → seconds left) from GQL `eventsRemaining[{events, secondsRemaining}]` and realtime `events_remaining_durations{"1": seconds}` (both > 0); `goal` default 3; `participants` strings; `levelOneRewards|level_one_rewards`.
**`HtCardStatus { int level; HtVariant variant = regular; bool isShared = false; bool isAllTimeHigh = false }`** (stream card pill).

### lib/models/hype_train_event.dart

Realtime messages of the Hermes topic `hype-train-events-v2.<channelId>`. `sealed class HypeTrainEvent`; `parse(String json)` never throws: decode → Map with String `type`; `data` Map (else `{}`); unusable → `HtUnknownEvent(type or '')`.
Type mapping:
- `hype-train-approaching` → `HtApproachingEvent(HypeTrainApproaching.parse(data))`.
- `hype-train-start` and `hype-train-shared-conversion` → `HtStartEvent(HypeTrainExecution.parse(data.hypeTrain|hype_train ?? data))`.
- `hype-train-progression` → `HtProgressionEvent` (requires `progress`): `trainId` = `data.id` (the train, verified), `sequenceId` = `sequence_id` ?? progress.total (orders contributions), `user_id`, `user_login`, `user_display_name`, `user_profile_image_url` (CDN-checked), `action` (`HtAction`, default `UNKNOWN`), `source`, `quantity` (default 0), `isLarge` = `is_large_event`, `expiresAt` = `expires_at`, `sharedProgress` = map userId → progress from `sharedProgress[]` (null if empty), `treasure` from `treasureTrainDetails` or `variantTrainDetails`.
- `hype-train-level-up` → `HtLevelUpEvent(timeToExpire: time_to_expire (epoch ms) required, progress required, train: HypeTrainExecution.parse(hype_train))`.
- `hype-train-conductor-update` → `HtConductorEvent(HtConductor.parse(data))`.
- `hype-train-end` → `HtEndEvent(trainId: id, endedAt: ended_at, reason: ending_reason, totals: participation_totals[] with quantity → HtTotal(source, action, quantity), rewards: rewards[])`; `synthesized = false` (the store synthesizes one itself at expiry + 10 s when Twitch sent none — then there are no totals).
- `hype-train-shared-merge-trains` → `HtMergeEvent(train: hypeTrain required, previousProgress: prevProgress, previousChannels: prevUsers, joiningChannels: joiningUsers)`.
- `hype-train-shared-channel-left` → `HtChannelLeftEvent(trainId: id, continuesIn: HtUser(user), shouldEnd: shouldTrainEnd)`.
- `hype-train-cooldown-expiration`, `hype-train-reward-eligibility`, `hype-train-rewards` → `HtIgnoredEvent(type)`.
- anything else / failed parse → `HtUnknownEvent(type)`.
`HtTotal { HtSource source; HtAction action; int quantity }`.

### lib/models/pinata.dart

`enum PinataEligibility { yes, no }`; `PinataState { int stage; bool? isEligibleForRewards; DateTime? expiresAt }`, `eligibility` = yes only when `isEligibleForRewards == true`. `fromBody(body)`: `data.user.channel.hypeTrain.execution` Map with `isActive == true`, `variantTrainDetails.__typename == 'PinataTrainDetails'` (else null); `deteriorationStage` num ≥ 0 → int else 0; `self.isEligibleForRewards` bool?; `expiresAt` parsed → UTC. The piñata banner shows the deterioration stage (0…) of the piñata during that train variant.

### lib/models/twitch_tv_session.dart

- `TwitchTvDeviceCode { String deviceCode; String userCode; Duration interval; DateTime expiresAt }`, `minimumInterval = 1 s`, user code shape `^[A-Za-z0-9-]{4,16}$`. `activationUri = https://www.twitch.tv/activate?device-code=<userCode>` (`Uri.https`, query-encoded). `isExpiredAt(t)` = `!t.isBefore(expiresAt)` (expired at exactly expiresAt). `fromJson(json, {now})`: `device_code` non-empty, `user_code` matching shape, `expires_in` num > 0 required; `interval` num > 0 else 5 s, floored at 1 s; `expiresAt = now + expires_in s`.
- `TwitchTvGrant { String accessToken; String? refreshToken }`, `fromJson`: non-empty `access_token`; `refresh_token` non-empty or null. `toString` = `'TwitchTvGrant(refreshable: <bool>)'` (never prints tokens).
- `sealed class TwitchTvPoll` with `TwitchTvPollPending`, `TwitchTvPollSlowDown`, `TwitchTvPollExpired`, `TwitchTvPollDenied`, `TwitchTvPollFailed`, `TwitchTvPollGranted(grant)`.
- `TwitchTvIdentity { String clientId; String userId }`, `fromJson`: String `client_id` and non-empty String `user_id`.
- `TwitchTvSession { String accessToken; String? refreshToken; String userId; String deviceId }`. `withGrant(grant)` keeps userId/deviceId, replaces access, keeps old refresh when the grant has none. `encode()` = JSON `{"access": …, "refresh"?: … (omitted when null), "user": …, "device": …}` (persisted in secure storage). `decode(raw)`: null/empty/invalid JSON/non-Map → null; `access`, `user`, `device` must be non-empty Strings; refresh optional non-empty. `toString` `'TwitchTvSession(user: <id>)'`. `deviceId` is sent as `X-Device-Id` with rerouted mutations. Rust: `serde` struct with `#[serde(rename = "access")]` etc., `skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none"` on refresh; store with the `keyring` crate.

### lib/models/chat_history.dart

Private limits: numeric id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`; message id `^[0-9A-Za-z-]{1,64}$` (UUID-like; ids reach outgoing IRC reply tags so nothing else kept); `_maxTextLength = 1000`; `_maxFragments = 300`; `_maxShortLength = 64`. `_string(v, {max = 64})` = non-empty String cut to max code units; `_id` = message-id pattern; `_numeric` = numeric pattern; `_badge(json)` = `(setId: setID, version: version)` both non-empty ≤64.
- `ChatHistoryUser { String? id (numeric); String? login; String? displayName; String? color ('chatColor') }`, `tryParse` (any Map).
- `ChatHistoryFragment { String text; String? emoteId; int bits = 0; ({String id, String url})? gif }`, `tryParse`: String `text` required; `content.__typename` `Emote` → emoteId = `content.id`; `CheermoteToken` → bits = `bitsAmount` int in 1…1 000 000 else 0; `GifContent` → gif `(id ≤64, url ≤2048)` when both present; other/absent content → plain.
- `ChatHistoryParent { String id; String? text; ChatHistoryUser? sender }`, `tryParse`: valid message id; `content.text` ≤1000; `sender`.
- `ChatHistoryMessage { String id; DateTime sentAt (UTC); bool deleted = false; ChatHistoryUser? sender (null → line dropped later); String? color; List<({String setId, String version})> badges; List<ChatHistoryFragment> fragments; String? sourceChannelId (Shared Chat origin); ChatHistoryParent? parent; ChatHistoryParent? threadParent }`. `tryParse`: valid `id`, parseable `sentAt` required; fragments from `content.fragments` (first 300), stopping once the running text length exceeds 1000; no fragments but `content.text` → one plain fragment (≤1000); `deleted = deletedAt != null`; color = `senderChatColor` ?? `sender.chatColor`; badges from `senderBadges` (first 300); `sourceChannel.id` numeric; `parentMessage`, `threadParentMessage`.

### lib/models/chat_rules.dart

`ChatRules { String channelId; String channelName; String? iconUrl; List<String> rules }`, `maxRules = 50`, `maxRuleLength = 500`, `hasRules = rules.isNotEmpty`. `fromGql(user)`: `id` (≤32) required; `displayName` ≤64 or ''; icon = `profileImageURL` (≤400) only https on `static-cdn.jtvnw.net`, no userinfo/port; rules = `chatSettings.rules` (first 200 inspected) each `chString` (≤500) trimmed, blanks dropped, then first 50.

### lib/models/followed_category.dart

`FollowedCategory { String id; String name; String displayName = ''; String? slug; String boxArtUrl = ''; int viewers = 0 }`. Patterns: id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, slug `^[a-z0-9-]{1,100}$`, image host prefix `https://static-cdn.jtvnw.net/`. Getters: `title` = displayName if non-empty else name; `twitchUrl` = `twitchUrlFor(slug, name)`. `twitchUrlFor({slug, name})` = `https://www.twitch.tv/directory/category/<slug>` when the slug is valid, else `https://www.twitch.tv/directory/game/<name>` (path segments percent-encoded by `Uri`; twitch.tv redirects). `validSlug(v)` returns the slug when it matches. `tryParse(node)`: numeric `id` and non-empty `name` required; `displayName` String or ''; `slug` validated; `boxArtURL` kept only if it starts with the Twitch image host prefix else ''; `viewersCount` int > 0 else 0.

### lib/models/watch_streak.dart

- `enum WatchStreakState { active, suspended, none }` (suspended = a stream was missed, can still be saved before expiry).
- `enum WatchStreakChange { recovered, expired }`; `watchStreakChange(before, after)`: only when before is suspended and both non-null: after state none OR after.value < before.value → expired; after active → recovered; else null.
- `watchStreakChangeText(change, {streak, l})`: recovered → `streakRecovered(count: streak.value ?? 0)`; expired → `streakExpired`.
- `typedef WatchStreakCopy = ({String title, String? subtitle})`.
- `WatchStreak { int value; int? threshold; int? pointsBonus; WatchStreakState state = active; DateTime? expiresAt; bool canShare = false; String? milestoneId }`; `shared()` = copy with canShare false. `tryParse(body)`: Map with no `errors`; `data` Map; `channelViewerMilestoneSettings.isWatchStreakOptOut == true` → null (opted out); `data.channel.self.watchStreakMilestone` Map with inner `watchStreakMilestone` Map; `value` (int or numeric String) ≥ 0; `watchStreakThreshold`, `watchStreakCopoBonus` positive ints; `state` `SUSPENDED`→suspended, `NONE`→none, else active; `expiresAt` parsed; `canShare = shareStatus == 'CAN_SHARE'`; milestone id non-empty ≤128. `parseBatch(body)`: no errors; `data.batchGetWatchStreaks` List → `{channel.id: value}` for entries not suspended with value > 0.
- `copy({live, channelName, now, l})` (panel wording): suspended & live → (`streakSaveTitle`, `streakSaveLive(count: value)`); suspended & offline → (`streakExpiringTitle`, `streakExpiring(name, time: _timeLeft(expiresAt − now))` or null when unknown/past); otherwise title `streakYour(value)`, subtitle null unless threshold & bonus known: `next − value == 1` → `streakOneMore(count: bonus, points)`, else `canShare` → `streakReached(count: bonus, next, points)`, else `streakReach(count: bonus, next, points)`; `points` = bonus formatted with `NumberFormat.decimalPattern(localeName)`.
- `_timeLeft(d, l)`: hours > 0 → `chatShortHoursMinutes(hours, minutes)`; minutes > 0 → `chatShortMinutesSeconds(minutes, seconds)`; else `chatShortSeconds(seconds)` ("5h 12m", "4m 30s", "12s").

### lib/models/report.dart

- `enum ReportKind { user, channel, emote, message, clip }`.
- `enum ReportReason` with wire `id`: harassment `harassment`, hatefulConduct `hateful_conduct`, spam `spam`, sexualContent `sexual_content`, gore `gore`, harm `harm`, selfHarm `selfharm`, terrorism `terrorism_mass_violence`, impersonation `impersonation`, misinformation `misinformation`, ipViolation `ip_violation`, underaged `underaged`, banEvasion `tos_ban_evasion`, chatBanEvasion `chat_ban_evasion`, cheating `cheating`, bitsViolation `bits_violation`. Shared list (in this order): harassment, hatefulConduct, spam, sexualContent, gore, harm, selfHarm, terrorism, impersonation, misinformation, ipViolation, underaged. `forKind`: user → shared + banEvasion + bitsViolation; channel → shared + banEvasion + cheating; emote → shared; message → shared + banEvasion + chatBanEvasion + cheating; clip → shared + banEvasion + cheating.
- `ReportTarget { ReportKind kind; String targetUserId; String contentId; String name; String extra = '' }` with const ctors: `.user({userId, name})` (content = user id), `.channel({channelId, name})`, `.emote({emoteId, ownerId, name})` (target = owner), `.message({messageId, senderId, channelId, name})` (extra = channel id), `.clip({slug, broadcasterId, name})`. `contentType`: user/channel → `USER_REPORT`, emote → `EMOTE_REPORT`, message → `CHAT_REPORT`, clip → `CLIP_REPORT`. `reasons` = `forKind(kind)`. `twitchEmoteId(url)` = capture of `^https://static-cdn\.jtvnw\.net/emoticons/v[12]/([A-Za-z0-9_]+)/` (only Twitch emotes are reportable).

### lib/models/emotes.dart

json_serializable models (generated `emotes.g.dart`; regenerate, never edit). Exact JSON mapping:
- **`EmoteTwitch`** (`fieldRename: snake`, Helix emote objects): `id` (`id`, String, required), `name` (`name`, String, required), `emoteType` (`emote_type`, String?), `ownerId` (`owner_id`, String?), `tier` (`tier`, String? — "1000"/"2000"/"3000" for sub emotes, '' otherwise), `format` (`format`, List<String>? e.g. `["static","animated"]`).
- **`EmoteBTTV`**: `id` (`id`), `code` (`code`), `animated` (`animated`, bool, default false).
- **`EmoteBTTVChannel`**: `channelEmotes` (`channelEmotes`, List<EmoteBTTV>, required), `sharedEmotes` (`sharedEmotes`, required).
- **`RoomFFZ`** (snake): `set` (`set`, num→int, required), `vipBadge` (`vip_badge`, ImagesFFZ?), `modUrls` (`mod_urls`, ImagesFFZ?), `userBadgeIds` (`user_badge_ids`, Map<String, List<dynamic>>?). Getter `badgeIdsByUser` inverts it to `Map<userId (toString), List<badgeId>>`.
- **`ImagesFFZ`**: `url1x` (`"1"`, String required), `url2x` (`"2"`, String?), `url4x` (`"4"`, String?).
- **`OwnerFFZ`** (snake): `displayName` (`display_name`), `name` (`name`), both required.
- **`EmoteFFZ`**: `name`, `height` (num→int), `width` (num→int), `owner` (OwnerFFZ), `urls` (ImagesFFZ), `animated` (ImagesFFZ?), `modifier` (bool default false), `modifierFlags` (`modifier_flags`, int default 0).
- **`Emote7TV`** (snake): `id`, `name` (the alias in the set), `data` (Emote7TVData).
- **`Owner7TV`** (snake): `username`, `displayName` (`display_name`).
- **`Emote7TVData`** (snake): `id`, `name` (original name), `flags` (num→int), `owner` (Owner7TV?), `host` (Emote7TVHost), `animated` (bool default false).
- **`Emote7TVHost`**: `url` (protocol-relative `//cdn.7tv.app/emote/<id>`), `files` (List<Emote7TVFile>).
- **`Emote7TVFile`** (snake): `name` (`1x.webp`…), `width`, `height`, `format` (`WEBP`, `AVIF`, `GIF`, `PNG`), `staticName` (`static_name`, String?, e.g. `1x_static.webp`).
- **`Emote`** (the common emote; `@JsonSerializable()` with toJson; camelCase keys used for local caches): `name` (required), `realName?` (original name when aliased), `width?`, `height?`, `zeroWidth` (bool, required), `url` (required), `staticUrl?`, `subTier?` (1–3), `type` (EmoteType, required, enum encoded by name), `ownerDisplayName?`, `ownerUsername?`, `ownerId?`, `animated` (default false), `limitedTime` (default false), `providerId?`, `modifierFlags` (default 0). toJson key order: name, realName, width, height, zeroWidth, url, subTier, staticUrl, type, ownerDisplayName, ownerUsername, ownerId, animated, limitedTime, providerId, modifierFlags.
  - `hidesItself` = `modifierFlags & FfzModifier.hidden != 0`.
  - `renamed(newName)`: copy with `name = newName`, `realName = null` if newName equals the original (`realName ?? name`) else the original name.
  - `Emote.fromTwitch(e, type)`: `url = https://static-cdn.jtvnw.net/emoticons/v2/<id>/default/dark/3.0`, `staticUrl = …/<id>/static/dark/3.0`, subTier from tier `1000`→1, `2000`→2, `3000`→3; `ownerId`; `animated` = format contains `animated`; `limitedTime = emote_type == 'limitedtime'`; zeroWidth false.
  - `Emote.fromBTTV(e, type)`: `url = https://cdn.betterttv.net/emote/<id>/3x`; `staticUrl = https://cdn.betterttv.net/emote/<id>/static/3x.webp` only if animated; `zeroWidth` = code in the constant list `zeroWidthEmotes` (`SoSnowy, IceCold, SantaHat, TopHat, ReinDeer, CandyCane, cvMask, cvHazmat`); `providerId = id`.
  - `Emote.fromFFZ(e, type)`: `zeroWidth = modifier`; `modifierFlags = modifier ? modifier_flags : 0`; width/height; url = animated 4x ?? 2x ?? 1x ?? urls 4x ?? 2x ?? 1x; staticUrl = urls best only when animated exists; owner display name/username; animated = animated != null.
  - `Emote.from7TV(e, type)`: file = LAST file whose format ≠ `AVIF` (largest non-AVIF); `zeroWidth = flags & 256 == 256`; `realName` = data.name when it differs from the alias; width/height of the FIRST file; `url = 'https:' + host.url + '/' + file.name` or `''` when no file; `staticUrl = 'https:' + host.url + '/' + file.staticName` when present; owner; animated; providerId = data.id.
  - `unlockHint(l)`: twitchGlobal → `emoteUnlockHint(how: 'global')`; twitchSub/twitchUnlocked → `owned` (null when ownerId null — tag-parsed emotes); twitchFollower → `follow`; twitchBits → `bits`; twitchChannel → `subscribe` when subTier null, else `emoteUnlockTier(tier)`; third-party → null.
  - `urlFor({animate})` = animate ? url : staticUrl ?? url.
- **`FfzModifier`** bit flags: hidden 1, flipX 2, flipY 4, growX 8, slide 16, appear 32, disappear 64, rotate 128, rotate90 256, greyscale 512, sepia 1024, rainbow 2048, hyperRed 4096, hyperShake 8192, cursed 16384, jam 32768, bounce 65536; `moving` = slide|appear|disappear|rotate|rainbow|hyperShake|jam|bounce; `tinted` = greyscale|sepia|hyperRed|cursed|rainbow.
- **`enum EmoteType`** (order): twitchBits, twitchFollower, twitchSub, twitchGlobal, twitchUnlocked, twitchChannel, ffzGlobal, ffzChannel, bttvGlobal, bttvChannel, bttvShared, sevenTVGlobal, sevenTVChannel, sevenTVPersonal, bttvPersonal. `toString()` English labels: 'Twitch bits emote', 'Twitch follower emote', 'Twitch sub emote', 'Twitch global emote', 'Twitch unlocked emote', 'Twitch channel emote', 'FrankerFaceZ global emote', 'FrankerFaceZ channel emote', 'BetterTTV global emote', 'BetterTTV channel emote', 'BetterTTV shared emote', '7TV global emote', '7TV channel emote', '7TV personal emote', 'BetterTTV personal emote'.

### lib/models/badges.dart

json_serializable (generated `badges.g.dart`):
- **`BadgeInfoTwitch`** (Helix badge version): `imageUrl1x` (`image_url_1x`), `imageUrl2x` (`image_url_2x`), `imageUrl4x` (`image_url_4x`), `id`, `title`, `description` — all required Strings.
- **`BadgeInfoFFZ`** (snake): `id` (num→int), `title`, `color` (CSS colour string), `urls` (BadgeUrlsFFZ).
- **`BadgeUrlsFFZ`**: `url1x` (`"1"`), `url2x` (`"2"`), `url4x` (`"4"`), all required.
- **`BadgeInfo7TV`**: `tooltip`, `urls` (List<List<String>>, pairs like `["1","url"]`), `users` (List<String>).
- **`BadgeInfoBTTV`**: `providerId` (`providerId`), `badge` (BadgeDetailsBTTV).
- **`BadgeDetailsBTTV`**: `description`, `svg` (URL).
- **`ChatBadge { String name; String url; BadgeType type; String? color }`**: `fromTwitch` (title, 4x url), `fromBTTV` (description, svg), `fromFFZ` (title, 4x url, color), `from7TV` (tooltip, `urls[2][1]`).
- `twitchBadgeExplainerKinds` map (badge set id → explainer kind): broadcaster→broadcaster, moderator→moderator, lead_moderator→leadModerator, vip→vip, subscriber→subscriber, founder→founder, bits→bits, bits-leader→bitsLeader, sub-gifter→subGifter, sub-gift-leader→subGiftLeader, partner→partner, staff→staff, premium→premium, turbo→turbo, artist-badge→artist, no_audio→noAudio, no_video→noVideo, predictions→predictions, hype-train→hypeTrain.
- `channelBoundBadgeKinds` = {broadcaster, moderator, leadModerator, vip, subscriber, founder, bits, bitsLeader, subGifter, subGiftLeader, artist, predictions, hypeTrain}.
- `typedef BadgeOrigin = ({bool elsewhere, String? channel})` (badge earned in another channel of a Shared Chat).
- `badgeExplainerText(l, kind, origin)`: null kind → null; not elsewhere or kind not channel-bound → `chatBadgeExplainer(kind)`; elsewhere & channel-bound → `chatBadgeExplainerElsewhere(kind, channel)` or null when the channel is unknown.
- `twitchBadgeExplainerKind(badgeKey)` = map lookup of the part before `/` in `set/version`.
- `enum BadgeType { twitch, bttv, ffz, sevenTV }`, `toString`: 'Twitch badge', 'BetterTTV badge', 'FrankerFaceZ badge', '7TV badge'.

### lib/models/category.dart

json_serializable (snake, `category.g.dart`). `CategoryTwitch(boxArtUrl, id, name)`: `box_art_url` (String template with `{width}x{height}`), `id`, `name` — all required. `CategoriesTwitch(data, pagination)`: `data` (List<CategoryTwitch>, required), `pagination` (`Map<String,String>?`, nullable — Helix `/games` has none, `/games/top` and `/search/categories` give `{"cursor": …}` or `{}`). Used by Helix `getTopCategories`, `getCategory`, `searchCategories`. Rust: `#[serde(rename_all="snake_case")]`, `pagination: Option<HashMap<String,String>>`.

### lib/models/channel.dart

json_serializable (snake, `channel.g.dart`).
- `Channel` (Helix `GET /channels` item; named ctor fields): `broadcasterId` (`broadcaster_id`), `broadcasterLogin` (`broadcaster_login`), `broadcasterName` (`broadcaster_name`), `broadcasterLanguage` (`broadcaster_language`), `title`, `gameId` (`game_id`), `gameName` (`game_name`) — required Strings; `contentClassificationLabels` (`content_classification_labels`, List<String>, default `[]`, e.g. `SexualThemes` → content warning); `tags` (List<String>, default `[]`).
- `ChannelQuery` (Helix `/search/channels` item): `broadcasterLogin` (`broadcaster_login`), `displayName` (`display_name`), `id`, `isLive` (`is_live`, bool), `startedAt` (`started_at`, String, '' when offline) — required; `gameName` (`game_name`, default ''), `title` (default '').

### lib/models/user.dart

json_serializable (snake, `user.g.dart`). Private `_dateOrNull(v)` = `DateTime.tryParse` when String else null.
- `UserTwitch(id, login, displayName, profileImageUrl, {createdAt})`: `id`, `login`, `display_name`, `profile_image_url` required Strings; `created_at` → `DateTime?` via `_dateOrNull` (account age shown on user cards).
- `UserBlockedTwitch(userId, userLogin, displayName)`: `user_id`, `user_login`, `display_name` (Helix `/users/blocks`).

### lib/models/followed_channel.dart

json_serializable (snake, `followed_channel.g.dart`). `FollowedChannel`: `broadcasterId` (`broadcaster_id`), `broadcasterLogin` (`broadcaster_login`), `broadcasterName` (`broadcaster_name`), `followedAt` (`followed_at`, `DateTime.parse` — throws on malformed). `FollowedChannels`: `data` (List), `pagination` (`Map<String,String>`, required), `total` (num→int). From Helix `/channels/followed` (20 per page) for the Following › channels list (offline included).

### lib/models/shared_chat_session.dart

json_serializable (snake, `shared_chat_session.g.dart`). `SharedChatSession { String sessionId (session_id); String hostBroadcasterId (host_broadcaster_id); List<Participant> participants; String createdAt (created_at); String updatedAt (updated_at) }`; `Participant { String broadcasterId (broadcaster_id) }`. From Helix `/shared_chat/session`; drives Shared Chat source-channel badges/avatars on chat lines.

### lib/models/team.dart

Private helpers: numeric id `^[0-9]{1,20}$`, login `^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,25}$`, team name `^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,64}$`; `_string(v, {max = 300})` = trimmed non-empty String cut to max; `_https(v)` = `_string` ≤500 parsed with scheme https.
- `TeamMember { String id; String login (lower-case); String displayName }`, `fromJson`: `user_id` numeric, `user_login` valid; `user_name` (≤64) ?? login.
- `Team { String id; String name (lower-case); String displayName; String info = ''; String? thumbnailUrl; String? bannerUrl; List<TeamMember> members = [] }`, `maxMembers = 500`. `fromJson` (Helix `/teams` and `/teams/channel` item): numeric `id` and valid `team_name` required; `team_display_name` (≤100) ?? name; `info` (≤2000): HTML tags `<[^>]*>` → space, then entities `&nbsp;`→space, `&lt;`→`<`, `&gt;`→`>`, `&quot;`→`"`, `&#39;`→`'`, `&amp;`→`&` (in that order), whitespace collapsed, trimmed; `thumbnail_url`, `banner` https only; `users` first 500 → members. Shown on the channel profile ("Teams") and a Team screen listing members.

### lib/models/cheermote.dart

- `CheermoteTier { int minBits; int color (ARGB); Map<String,String> _urls }` keyed `dark/animated`, `dark/static`, `light/animated`, `light/static` (scale 2 URLs). `url({dark, animated})` = exact theme/kind, else same theme other kind, else the first URL available.
- `typedef Cheer = ({String prefix, int amount, CheermoteTier tier})`.
- `Cheermotes` (private ctor; `empty`; `isEmpty`), map lower-cased prefix → tiers sorted by `minBits` descending. `fromHelix(json)` (Helix `/bits/cheermotes`): `data` List; entries with `prefix` matching `^[a-zA-Z0-9]{0,39}[a-zA-Z]$` and a `tiers` List; tier requires int `min_bits` ≥ 1, `color` `#RRGGBB`, `images` Map; per theme `dark`/`light` and kind `animated`/`static` take `scales['2'] ?? scales['1']` when it is a Twitch image (https, no userinfo, host `d3aqoihi2n8ty8.cloudfront.net` or `*.jtvnw.net` or `*.twitch.tv`); tiers without any URL dropped.
- `match(word)`: `^([a-zA-Z0-9]*[a-zA-Z])(\d{1,7})$` (prefix ending in a letter, then 1–7 digits, e.g. `Cheer100`, `4Head50`); prefix looked up case-insensitively; returns the highest tier whose minBits ≤ amount (amount ≥ 1), else null. Chat renders the matched cheer as the tier image + amount in the tier colour.

### lib/models/events.dart

7TV EventAPI websocket payloads (json_serializable, `events.g.dart`).
- `SevenTVEvent { int op; int? t; SevenTVEventData d }` with toJson (`t` omitted when null — `includeIfNull: false`); used to SEND subscribe/unsubscribe frames (op 35/36) and parse dispatches (op 0).
- `SevenTVEventData { String? type; Map<String,String>? condition; SevenTVEventEmoteSetBody? body }`; toJson writes only `type` and `condition` (body is `includeToJson: false`).
- `SevenTVEventUpdatedEmote` (snake): `value` (Emote7TV?), `oldValue` (`old_value`, Emote7TV?).
- `SevenTVEventEmoteSetBody { Owner7TV actor; List<SevenTVEventUpdatedEmote>? pushed; List<SevenTVEventUpdatedEmote>? pulled }` (`emote_set.update` dispatch: emotes added/removed live, with the actor shown in chat as "<actor> added <emote>").
Example subscribe frame: `{"op":35,"d":{"type":"emote_set.update","condition":{"object_id":"<setId>"}}}`.

### lib/models/moderation.dart

Helix moderation answer models (json_serializable, snake, `createToJson: false`, generated `moderation.g.dart`). Almost every field has a default, so missing keys never throw (the exception: `RewardRedemption.reward`, which is required and throws a cast error when absent).
- `ChannelRoleUser { String userId ('user_id', ''), String userLogin ('user_login', ''), String userName ('user_name', '') }`, `displayName` = userName or userLogin when empty. (Mods/VIPs lists.)
- `ShieldModeStatus { bool isActive ('is_active', false); String moderatorName ('moderator_name', ''); String lastActivatedAt ('last_activated_at', '') }`.
- `BlockedTerm { String id ('id', ''); String text ('text', ''); String? expiresAt ('expires_at') }`.
- `UnbanRequest { id, userId ('user_id'), userLogin ('user_login'), userName ('user_name'), text, status, createdAt ('created_at') }` all Strings default '', `displayName` getter (the moderator's pending unban list — distinct from lib/models/unban_request.dart which is the banned viewer's own request).
- `BannedUser { String userId ('user_id', ''); String expiresAt ('expires_at', '' = permanent ban); String reason ('reason', '') }`, `timeoutEndsAt` = null when empty else `DateTime.tryParse`.
- `PollChoice { String id; String title; int votes (num→int, 0) }`; `ChannelPoll { String id; String title; List<PollChoice> choices (default []); String status }`, `isActive = status == 'ACTIVE'`, `totalVotes` = sum. (Helix poll statuses: ACTIVE, COMPLETED, TERMINATED, ARCHIVED, MODERATED, INVALID.)
- `PredictionOutcome { String id; String title; int users (0); int channelPoints ('channel_points', 0) }`; `ChannelPrediction { String id; String title; List<PredictionOutcome> outcomes ([]); String status }`, `isActive` (ACTIVE), `isLocked` (LOCKED), `isOpen` = active or locked.
- `RedemptionReward { String id; String title; int cost (0) }`; `RewardRedemption { String id; String userLogin ('user_login'); String userName ('user_name'); RedemptionReward reward (required); String userInput ('user_input', ''); String redeemedAt ('redeemed_at', ISO string, '') }`, `displayName`. `TwitchModApi.getPendingRedemptions` sorts by `redeemedAt` with plain String comparison (ISO-8601 sorts chronologically).
- `CommercialStart { int length (0); int retryAfter ('retry_after', 0) }`.
These feed the moderator tools sheet (Mods/VIPs, Shield mode, blocked terms, unban requests, user ban status on the user card, polls, predictions, reward queue, ads). Rust: `#[derive(Deserialize, Default)] #[serde(default, rename_all = "snake_case")]` except `reward`.

### lib/models/celebration.dart

Celebrations delivered on the Hermes topic `celebration-events-v1` (alert-set / community-gift effects drawn by ONSET's own particle system, lib/screens/channel/celebrations/particles/). Note: this model drives an animation subsystem specified elsewhere; here only its data.
- `enum CelebrationEffect { fireworks, rain, float, flamethrower, extinctionEvent, unknown }`, `parse`: `FIREWORKS`, `RAIN`, `FLOAT`, `FLAMETHROWER`, `EXTINCTION_EVENT`, else unknown. `preset` (ONSET's own pairing, not Twitch's): fireworks→confetti, extinctionEvent→explosion, flamethrower→flame, rain→sparkles, float→starBonk, unknown→sparkles.
- `enum CelebrationPreset { confetti, explosion, flame, loud, onboardingSparkles, questionMarks, sparkles, starBonk }` (confetti = tumbling bits falling; explosion = radial burst with shock ring; flame = embers rising and flickering from the bottom; loud = rings pulsing out like sound waves; onboardingSparkles = gentle twinkles; questionMarks = question marks floating up; sparkles = a burst of stars; starBonk = stars circling and bouncing).
- `enum CelebrationIntensity { cool, cute, noice, pogChamp, omegaPog }`, `parse`: `COOL`, `CUTE`, `NOICE`, `POG_CHAMP`, `OMEGA_POG`, default cool. `scale`: 0.55, 0.65, 0.8, 0.95, 1.1. `opacity`: 0.55, 0.65, 0.78, 0.9, 1.
- `enum CelebrationArea { chat, video, everywhere }`, `parse`: `CHAT`, `VIDEO`, `EVERYWHERE`, default everywhere.
- `Celebration { CelebrationEffect effect; CelebrationArea area; CelebrationIntensity intensity; Duration duration; String? eventId; String? source; Color? primaryColor }`, `isAutomatic = source == 'system_gifting'`. `tryParse(data)` (payload of `alert_sets_celebration`): effect from `type` (unknown → null: a future effect degrades to nothing); `area`, `intensity`; `duration` = `duration` seconds (num, default 5.0) ×1000 clamped to 500…15 000 ms; `alert_event_id` (de-dup id, e.g. `COMMUNITY_GIFT_SUBSCRIPTION:<uuid>`); `celebration_source`; `primary_color_hex` (`#`-stripped 6 hex → opaque Color). Strings read with a type check (never `as String?`).

### lib/models/chat_gif.dart

GIFs sent with Twitch's GIF keyboard, from an IRC message's `gifs` tag (`<start>-<end>|<gifID>|<gifURL>` entries joined by commas; positions zero-based after a `/me` prefix, end inclusive).
- `ChatGif { int start; int end; String id; String url (kept exactly as sent — Twitch's doc forbids changing it) }`, value equality, `toString` `'ChatGif(<start>-<end>, <id>)'`. `parseTag(value)`: null/empty → `[]`; split on `,`; each must have exactly three `|` fields, non-empty id and url, range of exactly two integers split on `-`; sorted by start.
- `allowedHosts = {'giphy.com', 'jtvnw.net'}`; `isLoadable`: https, no userinfo, port absent or 443, lower-cased host equal to or a subdomain of an allowed host (the tag can come from the third-party recent-messages history, so arbitrary URLs would leak viewers' IPs; a refused GIF shows its title text instead).
- `GifMessagePart { String text; ChatGif? gif }`, `maxGifs = 5`. `split(message, gifs)` (gifs sorted): character start offsets computed in UTF-16 units skipping the low half of surrogate pairs (positions count code points like the `emotes` tag); a GIF range is mapped via code-point positions when `end < number of code points`, else read as UTF-16 units provided it does not cut a surrogate pair; requires `0 ≤ start ≤ end`; overlapping (start before the previous kept end) dropped; at most 5 kept; plain text parts inserted between/after; nothing kept → `[]`. Rust: build a `Vec<usize>` of char starts in UTF-16 units from `encode_utf16`, same rules.

### lib/models/community_highlight_event.dart

Realtime community-highlight events (Hermes). `enum HighlightTopic { polls('polls'), predictions('predictions-channel-v1'), pinned('pinned-chat-updates-v1'), raid('raid') }` with `prefix` and `topicFor(channelId)` = `'<prefix>.<channelId>'` (e.g. `polls.123`).
`sealed class ChEvent`; `ChEvent.parse(topic, text)` never throws (JSON decode failure, non-Map, non-String `type`, or any exception → `ChUnknown`):
- polls: `Poll.fromPubsub(data.poll)` + type `POLL_CREATE`→create, `POLL_UPDATE`→update, `POLL_COMPLETE`→complete, `POLL_TERMINATE`→terminate, `POLL_ARCHIVE`→archive → `PollEvent(kind, poll)`; unknown type or poll → ChUnknown.
- predictions: `Prediction.fromPubsub(data.event)` → `PredictionEvent(prediction, timestamp: chDate(data.timestamp))` (any type; timestamp orders out-of-order updates).
- pinned: `pin-message` → `PinEvent(PinnedChatMessage.fromPubsub(data))`; `update-message` → `PinUpdateEvent(data.id ≤64)` (details not carried — refetch snapshot); `unpin-message` → `UnpinEvent(id)`.
- raid: `OutgoingRaid.fromPubsub(map.raid)` (note: top-level `raid`, not `data`) + `raid_update_v2`|`raid_update`→update, `raid_go_v2`|`raid_go`→go, `raid_cancel_v2`|`raid_cancel`→cancel → `RaidEvent(kind, raid)`.
Enums `PollEventKind { create, update, complete, terminate, archive }`, `RaidEventKind { update, go, cancel }`; classes `PollEvent`, `PredictionEvent`, `PinEvent`, `PinUpdateEvent`, `UnpinEvent`, `RaidEvent`, `ChUnknown`.

### lib/models/creator_goal.dart

Creator goals (sub/follower goal bar above chat) from Hermes topic `creator-goals-events-v1.<channelId>`.
- `enum CreatorGoalKind { subs, subPoints, newSubs, newSubPoints, followers, other }` from `contributionType` `SUBS`, `SUB_POINTS`, `NEW_SUBS`, `NEW_SUB_POINTS`, `FOLLOWERS`, else other.
- `enum CreatorGoalState { active, reached, ended }`: `eventType == 'goal_ended'` or `state == 'ENDED'` → ended; `goal_achieved` or `ACHIEVED` → reached; else active.
- `CreatorGoal { String id; CreatorGoalKind kind; CreatorGoalState state; String description; int current; int target }`, `progress` = current/target clamped 0–1 (0 if target ≤ 0), `isReached = state == reached || current >= target`. `tryParse(raw, {eventType})`: non-empty `id`, num `currentContributions`, num `targetContributions` > 0; description trimmed; current clamped 0…2^31.
- `mergeCreatorGoal(goals, goal)`: removes the goal with the same id; ended → returns the others; new → appended; existing → re-inserted at its old index (clamped).

### lib/models/hermes.dart

Hermes (`wss://hermes.twitch.tv/v1?clientId=…`, Twitch's WebSocket gateway that replaced pubsub-edge; client in lib/services) message envelope — shapes confirmed live 2026-07-30.
`HermesMessage { String type; String? id; String? parentId (ties a response to its request); Map<String,dynamic> raw }`. Type constants: `welcome`, `keepalive`, `notification`, `subscribeResponse`, `unsubscribeResponse`, `authenticateResponse`, `reconnect`, `revocation`, `parseError`.
- `tryParse(payload)`: JSON object with String `type` (else null — a malformed frame must not drop a healthy connection); `id`/`parentId` read with type checks.
- `keepaliveInterval` = `welcome.keepaliveSec` (measured 15 s) clamped to `minKeepaliveSec = 5` … `maxKeepaliveSec = 120`, null when absent; `recoveryUrl` = `welcome.recoveryUrl`; `sessionId` = `welcome.sessionId`.
- `isSubscribeOk` = `subscribeResponse.result == 'ok'`. `responseError` = first non-null `error` in subscribe/unsubscribe/authenticate responses (stringified). `errorCode` = first String `errorCode` in `parseError`/responses (safe to log; never echoes what was sent).
- `subscriptionId` = `<notification|subscribeResponse|unsubscribeResponse|revocation>.subscription.id` — notifications do NOT repeat the topic; the client must keep id → topic itself.
- `notificationPayload` = `notification.pubsub` as a Map, or decoded from its JSON string (double-encoded like old PubSub); malformed → null. `payloadType` = its `type`; `payloadData` = its `data` Map.
`HermesTopics` builders (a topic without its channel suffix silently subscribes to nothing): `predictionsChannel` → `predictions-channel-v1.<id>`, `communityPointsChannel` → `community-points-channel-v1.<id>`, `creatorGoals` → `creator-goals-events-v1.<id>`, `celebrations` → `celebration-events-v1.<id>`, `channelSubGifts` → `channel-sub-gifts-v1.<id>`, `sharedChat` → `shared-chat-channel-v1.<id>`, `shoutout` → `shoutout.<id>`, `communityMoments` → `community-moments-channel-v1.<id>`, `contentClassificationLabels` → `content-classification-labels-v1.<id>`, `videoPlayback` → `video-playback-by-id.<id>`, `broadcastSettings` → `broadcast-settings-update.<id>`; user topics (the only ones needing authentication; channel topics work anonymously): `predictionsUser` → `predictions-user-v1.<userId>`, `communityPointsUser` → `community-points-user-v1.<userId>`, `userSubscribeEvents` → `user-subscribe-events-v1.<userId>`, `userBitsUpdates` → `user-bits-updates-v1.<userId>`, `privateCallout(userId, channelId)` → `private-callout.<userId>.<channelId>`. `sendsUntypedPayloads(topic)` = starts with `content-classification-labels-v1.`; `sendsFlatPayloads(topic)` = starts with `video-playback-by-id.`, `broadcast-settings-update.` or `channel-sub-gifts-v1.` (payload has no `type`/`data` nesting). Lists: `forViewerInChannel(userId, channelId)` = [communityMoments, privateCallout]; `forChannel(channelId)` = [predictionsChannel, communityPointsChannel, creatorGoals, celebrations, shoutout, contentClassificationLabels, videoPlayback, broadcastSettings, channelSubGifts] (hype train excluded — followed by its own store); `forViewer(userId)` = [communityPointsUser, predictionsUser, userSubscribeEvents, userBitsUpdates].
Rust: `tokio-tungstenite` client; `HermesMessage` as a `serde_json::Value` wrapper with these accessors.

### lib/models/ivs_stats.dart

`IvsStats` — one-per-second snapshot of the native IVS player statistics, sent by `IvsPlayerCore` (Android platform channel) while the stats overlay is open; it is how latency/bitrate is made visible for measuring on a device. All fields optional; wrong types read as unknown, never throw (older/newer native builds cannot crash the overlay). `fromEvent(Map event)`: `bitrate` (`bitrate`, num→round, bits/s), `fps` (`fps`), `droppedFrames` (`dropped`), `decodedFrames` (`decoded`), `renderedFrames` (`rendered`), `bufferedMs` (`bufferedMs`, buffered position ahead of playhead), `lowLatency` (`lowLatency`, bool — whether the LL segment path is used), `playbackRate` (`playbackRate`, double), `bandwidth` (`bandwidth`, estimated bits/s), and from the nested `quality` Map: `qualityName` (`name`), `width`, `height`, `codecs` (String e.g. `avc1.64002A,mp4a.40.2`), `framerate` (double). Getter `resolution` = `'<width>×<height>'` (U+00D7) when both known. PC: the native player is replaced (e.g. libmpv/gstreamer); the PC player must surface the same stat set (bitrate, fps, dropped/decoded/rendered frames, buffer ahead in ms, low-latency flag, playback rate, bandwidth estimate, quality name, resolution, codecs, framerate) for the stats overlay.

### lib/models/private_callout.dart

- `PrivateCallout { String id; String body; String? actionLabel; Uri? actionUrl }`, `maxBodyLength = 300`, id `^[A-Za-z0-9_:\-]{1,128}$`. `fromEvent(data)` (Hermes `private-callout.<userId>.<channelId>`): `data.private_callout` Map; valid `id`; `body` trimmed 1–300 chars; first `actions[]` whose `url` is https with a host → label = trimmed non-blank `body` or null, url. Shown as a dismissible callout card in chat (e.g. "You earned a drop — claim").
- `PredictionWin { String eventId; int points }`, `fromEvent(type, data)`: only `type == 'prediction-result'`; `data.prediction.result.type == 'WIN'`, int `points_won` > 0, String `prediction.event_id` (from `predictions-user-v1`); shown as "You won N points" toast.

### lib/models/raid_arrival.dart

`RaidArrival { String fromName; String? fromLogin; String targetName; String? targetAvatarUrl; int viewers; DateTime at }` — the viewer followed a raid. `RaidArrival.leaving(raid, {fromName, at, fromLogin})`: targetName = `raid.targetDisplayName`, avatar = `twitchImageOrNull(raid.targetProfileImage)` (https, host exactly the Twitch image host `static-cdn.jtvnw.net`, no userinfo — lib/utils/https_url.dart), viewers = max(0, raid.viewerCount). Shown on the target channel as "Raided from <fromName> with N viewers" banner.

### lib/models/stream_push.dart

Live updates of a stream's metadata from Hermes (`video-playback-by-id.<id>`, `broadcast-settings-update.<id>`, flat payloads).
- `sealed class StreamPush`: `ViewerCountPush(int viewers)`, `StreamUpPush()`, `StreamDownPush()`, `BroadcastSettingsPush({String? title, String? gameName, String? gameId})`. `abstract interface class StreamPushTarget { void applyStreamPush(StreamPush push); }`.
- `parseStreamPush(type, data)`: `viewcount` → `viewers` num ≥ 0 → ViewerCountPush(toInt); `stream-up`; `stream-down`; `broadcast_settings_update` → title = `status`, gameName = `game`, gameId = `game_id` via `_text` (non-empty String, or a num stringified), all null → null; other types → null.
- `patchStream(stream, push)`: null stream → null; viewer count / broadcast settings → copy of the `StreamTwitch` with those fields replaced (others kept, including tags/language/id); up/down → unchanged.
- `StreamPushState({downRetryDelay = 20 s})`: `generation` counter incremented by `noteValuePush()`; `reconcile(fetched, current, fetchStartedAt)`: if any is null or no push arrived since the fetch began (`fetchStartedAt == generation`) → fetched; else the fetched stream with the pushed `viewerCount`, `title`, `gameName`, `gameId` from `current` (a Helix refresh older than a push must not undo it). `scheduleDownRetry(retry)`: cancels any pending timer and re-checks after 20 s (a `stream-down` is confirmed by refetching before showing offline). `dispose()` cancels the timer.
Rust: enums + a small struct with a `tokio::time::sleep` task handle.

### lib/models/third_party_cosmetics.dart

7TV cosmetics: name paints, 7TV badges and personal emotes, from both the 7TV EventAPI (v3 websocket, snake_case, RGBA ints) and the 7TV v4 GQL (`SevenTVApi.getUsersCosmetics`, hex strings).
- `enum NamePaintKind { linearGradient, radialGradient, image, solid }`; `NamePaintStop(double at, Color color)`.
- `NamePaint { String id; String name = ''; NamePaintKind kind; double angle = 90; bool repeating = false; List<NamePaintStop> stops = []; String? imageUrl; Color? color; List<Shadow> shadows = [] }`.
  - `isDrawable`: gradients need ≥ 2 stops; image needs imageUrl; solid needs color.
  - `fallbackColor` = color ?? first stop colour (used when painting is off).
  - `gradientShader(bounds)`: colours/stops (positions clamped 0–1), tile mode repeated if `repeating` else clamp. Linear: angle in degrees → radians; direction `Alignment(sin r, −cos r)`; gradient from `−direction` to `direction` (CSS angle convention: 0° = bottom→top, 90° = left→right). Radial: centred default radius. Solid: two-stop linear of the same colour. Image/insufficient → null (image paints are drawn with the image as a text mask by the chat renderer).
  - `fromEventApi(data)`: String `id` required; if `data.function` is null and `data.gradients[0]` is a Map, the gradient map is the source (newer payloads), else data itself; `function` `LINEAR_GRADIENT` → linear, `RADIAL_GRADIENT` → radial, `URL` → image, else solid when `data.color` is an int, else null. `angle` (default 90), `repeat == true`, `stops[]` with int `color` (RGBA) and `at` (default 0), `image_url` via `_https`, `color` RGBA int, `shadows[]` with int `color`, `x_offset`, `y_offset`, `radius` → `Shadow(color, Offset(x, y), blurRadius)`.
  - `fromGql(paint)`: String `id` and `data` Map required; first `data.layers[]` with a `ty` Map; stops from `ty.stops[].{at, color.hex}`; kind from `ty.__typename` (`PaintLayerTypeLinearGradient`, `PaintLayerTypeRadialGradient`, `PaintLayerTypeImage`, `PaintLayerTypeSingleColor`, else null); `angle` default 90; `repeating == true`; image = `pickStillImage(ty.images)` for image paints; color = `ty.color.hex`; shadows from `data.shadows[].{color.hex, offsetX, offsetY, blur}`.
- `colorFromRgbaInt(v)`: v masked to 32 bits = 0xRRGGBBAA → `Color.fromARGB(AA, RR, GG, BB)`.
- `colorFromHex(hex)`: strip `#`; 6 digits → append `FF`; 8 digits as is (RRGGBBAA); else null; parsed through colorFromRgbaInt.
- `_https(url)`: null/empty → null; protocol-relative `//…` prefixed with `https:`; must parse with scheme https.
- `pickStillImage(images)`: candidates = maps with mime ≠ `image/avif` and an https URL; prefer `frameCount == 1`; among the pool, the largest `scale` ≤ 3 (default scale 1), else the first of the pool; returns its https URL.
- `pickAnimatedImage(images)`: candidates (non-AVIF, https, scale ≤ 3) sorted by scale; the largest-scale one with `frameCount > 1` (absent frameCount counts as 1), else the largest candidate.
- `sevenTvBadgeFromEventApi(data)`: `host.url` (https-normalized) required; files with format ≠ `AVIF`, String names without `/`; name = first starting with `3x`, else last, else `3x.webp`; `ChatBadge(name: tooltip ?? name ?? '', url: '<base>/<file>', type: sevenTV)`.
- `sevenTvBadgeFromGql(badge)`: url = `pickAnimatedImage(images)` (null → null); name = `description ?? name ?? ''`.
- `sevenTvEmoteFromGql(item, type)`: `item.emote` Map; url = pickAnimatedImage(emote.images); `item.alias` non-empty required; `animated` = any image frameCount > 1; staticUrl = pickStillImage when animated and a still exists; width/height of the first image; zeroWidth = `item.flags.zeroWidth == true` or `emote.flags.defaultZeroWidth == true`; realName = defaultName when it differs from the alias; providerId = emote.id.
- `SevenTvUserCosmetics { NamePaint? paint; ChatBadge? badge; String? personalSetId; List<Emote> personalEmotes = [] }`, `none`; `fromGql(user)`: null → none; `style.activePaint` → NamePaint.fromGql; `style.activeBadge` → badge; `personalEmoteSet.id` and `.emotes.items[]` → emotes of type `sevenTVPersonal`.
Rust: colours as `[u8;4]`/`u32`; shader construction moves to the PC renderer (egui/iced gradient mesh) using the same angle convention. (Renderer superseded by D3: Slint with Skia.)

### lib/models/third_party_events.dart

Live third-party emote/cosmetic events: 7TV EventAPI dispatches (`wss://events.7tv.io/v3`) and the BTTV socket (`wss://sockets.betterttv.net/ws`). Parsers never throw (any exception → null).
- `enum SevenTvEntitlementKind { badge, paint, emoteSet }`.
- `SevenTvActor(username, displayName)`, `parse(actor)`: Map; `username` default ''; `display_name` default username; both empty → null.
- `sealed class SevenTvDispatch`, `parse(decoded)`: requires `op == 0`, `d` Map with `body` Map; by `d.type`: `emote_set.update` → `SevenTvEmoteSetChange`; `user.update` → `SevenTvActiveSetSwitch`; `cosmetic.create` → `SevenTvCosmeticCreated`; `entitlement.create` → `SevenTvEntitlement(granted: true)`; `entitlement.delete` → granted false; else null.
- `SevenTvEmoteRename(String oldName, Emote7TV emote)`.
- `SevenTvEmoteSetChange { String setId; SevenTvActor? actor; List<Emote7TV> added; List<String> removed; List<SevenTvEmoteRename> renamed }`, `isEmpty`. `_parse(body)`: String `body.id`; only changes with `key == 'emotes'` in `pushed`/`pulled`/`updated`; added = `pushed[].value` parsed as Emote7TV when it has a `data` Map; removed = `pulled[].old_value.name`; renamed = `updated[]` where `old_value.name` differs from the new value's name. Chat shows "<actor> added/removed/renamed <emote>" system lines and updates the emote map live.
- `SevenTvActiveSetSwitch { String userId; SevenTvActor? actor; String setId; String setName }`, `_parse`: String `body.id`; in `updated[]` the change with `key == 'connections'` whose `value` List contains a field `key == 'emote_set'` with a `value` Map holding String `id` (and `name`, default '') — the channel switched its active emote set (re-fetch set via `getEmoteSet`).
- `SevenTvCosmeticCreated { String id; NamePaint? paint; ChatBadge? badge }`, `_parse`: `body.object` Map with `data` Map; id = `data.id ?? object.id`; `object.kind` `PAINT` → `NamePaint.fromEventApi({...data, id})`; `BADGE` → `sevenTvBadgeFromEventApi(data)`; else null.
- `SevenTvEntitlement { SevenTvEntitlementKind kind; String refId; String twitchUserId; bool granted }`, `_parse`: `object.kind` `BADGE`/`PAINT`/`EMOTE_SET`, String `object.ref_id`, `object.user` Map whose `connections[]` has one with `platform == 'TWITCH'` and String `id` (the Twitch user id) — assigns/removes a cosmetic to a chatter.
- `sealed class BttvSocketEvent`, `parse(decoded, {channelId})`: Map with `data` Map; by `name`: `emote_create` (only when `data.channel == 'twitch:<channelId>'`) → `BttvEmoteCreated(EmoteBTTV.fromJson(data.emote))`; `emote_update` (same check) → `BttvEmoteUpdated(id, code)` (non-empty code); `emote_delete` (same check) → `BttvEmoteDeleted(data.emoteId)`; `lookup_user` → `BttvProUser(providerId, pro: data.pro == true, emotes: data.emotes[] with String id and code → EmoteBTTV)` (BTTV Pro personal emotes); else null.
Rust: serde_json value navigation; the same filters.

### lib/models/user_notice_presentation.dart

How a USERNOTICE chat row is drawn (data only; the drawing lives in the chat widgets).
- `enum NoticeLayout { special, generic, subGoal, raid }` (special = tinted row with accent bar for subs/gifts/paid events; generic = no tint, accent in text colour for announcements/milestones; subGoal = special + sub-goal progress; raid = led by the raider's avatar).
- `enum NoticeGlyph { anonymousGift, sharedChat, megaphone, raider, charity, gift, milestone }`.
- `NoticePresentation(layout, {icon, iconBias = defaultIconBias})`, `defaultIconBias = 0.3`, `centredIconBias = 0.5` (anonymous gift icon centred, others slightly above middle); value equality; `toString` `'NoticePresentation(<layout>, <icon>, <bias>)'`.
- `noticePresentation(notice)` by `UserNoticeKind`: announcement → generic + megaphone; raid → raid + raider; unraid → generic; viewerMilestone → generic + milestone; charityDonation → special + charity; giftSubBaseMatch/rewardGift → special + gift; subGift/communityGift/anonSubGift/anonCommunityGift → subGoal if `hasGoal`, else anonymous → special + anonymousGift (centred), else special; sub/resub → subGoal if hasGoal, else when `sourceRoomId` non-null and ≠ `roomId` (shared chat) → special + sharedChat, else special; extendSub/giftPaidUpgrade/anonGiftPaidUpgrade/primePaidUpgrade/bitsBadgeTier/payItForward/unknown → special without icon.
- `enum ChatBubbleEvent { subs, gifts, raids, announcements, other }` and `chatBubbleEventOf(notice)`: sub, resub, extendSub, giftPaidUpgrade, anonGiftPaidUpgrade, primePaidUpgrade → subs; subGift, communityGift, anonSubGift, anonCommunityGift, giftSubBaseMatch, rewardGift, payItForward → gifts; raid, unraid → raids; announcement → announcements; viewerMilestone, charityDonation, bitsBadgeTier, unknown → other (used by the per-event-type chat filter settings).

### lib/models/user_notice.dart

A typed IRC `USERNOTICE` (subs, gifts, raids, announcements…). Typed instead of substring matching (the old code asked whether msg-id *contained* "sub").
- `enum UserNoticeKind { sub, resub, subGift, communityGift, anonSubGift, anonCommunityGift, giftPaidUpgrade, anonGiftPaidUpgrade, primePaidUpgrade, extendSub, rewardGift, giftSubBaseMatch, raid, unraid, announcement, bitsBadgeTier, payItForward, viewerMilestone, charityDonation, unknown }`. msg-id mapping (`_kindFrom`): `sub`, `resub`, `subgift`→subGift, `submysterygift`→communityGift (mass-gift header; followed by one subGift per recipient sharing `msg-param-community-gift-id`), `anonsubgift`, `anonsubmysterygift`→anonCommunityGift, `giftpaidupgrade`, `anongiftpaidupgrade`, `primepaidupgrade`, `extendsub`, `rewardgift`, `giftsubbasematch` (sponsor-paid bonus sub with advertiser name/colour), `raid`, `unraid`, `announcement`, `bitsbadgetier`, `standardpayforward`|`communitypayforward`→payItForward, `viewermilestone`, `charitydonation`, else unknown (still renders its system-msg).
- Constants: `anonymousGifterUserId = '274598607'`, `anonymousGifterLogin = 'ananonymousgifter'` (modern IRC sends anonymous gifts as normal subgift from this account).
- `enum AnnouncementColor { primary, blue, green, orange, purple }` (from `msg-param-color`, case-insensitive; other → null).
- **Fields** (all from tags; `nullIfEmpty` treats `key=` as absent): `kind`; `systemMessage` (`system-msg` unescaped; empty for announcements whose text is the IRC body); `tier` (`msg-param-sub-plan`: `Prime`→'Prime', `1000`→'Tier 1', `2000`→'Tier 2', `3000`→'Tier 3', other passed through, absent → null); `isPrime` (sub-plan == `Prime`); `cumulativeMonths` (`msg-param-cumulative-months`); `streakMonths` (`msg-param-streak-months`, meaningful only when shared); `shouldShareStreak` (`msg-param-should-share-streak == '1'`); `recipientDisplayName` (`msg-param-recipient-display-name`, unescaped); `recipientLogin` (`msg-param-recipient-user-name`); `giftMonths` (`msg-param-gift-months`); `benefitEndMonth` (`msg-param-sub-benefit-end-month`); `massGiftCount` (`msg-param-mass-gift-count`, negative → 0); `senderCount` (`msg-param-sender-count`); `communityGiftId` (`msg-param-community-gift-id`); `originId` (`msg-param-origin-id`, unescaped); `displayName` (`display-name`, unescaped); `login` (`login`); `userId` (`user-id`); `isHistorical` (`historical == '1'`, replayed from recent-messages); `roomId` (`room-id`); `sourceRoomId` (`source-room-id`, shared chat); goal: `goalCurrentContributions` (`msg-param-goal-current-contributions`), `goalTargetContributions` (`msg-param-goal-target-contributions`), `goalContributionType` (`msg-param-goal-contribution-type`), `goalDescription` (`msg-param-goal-description`, unescaped); `senderLogin` (`msg-param-sender-login`), `senderName` (`msg-param-sender-name`, unescaped) for gift upgrades; `raidViewerCount` (`msg-param-viewerCount`); `raiderAvatarUrl` (`msg-param-profileImageURL` with `%s` replaced by `70x70`); `raiderDisplayName` (`msg-param-displayName`); `announcementColor`; `sponsorName` (`msg-param-advertiser-name`); `sponsorColor` (`msg-param-advertiser-hex-color` `#RRGGBB` → opaque Color); `multiMonthDuration` (`msg-param-multimonth-duration`); `wasGifted` (`msg-param-was-gifted == 'true'`); `giftMonthBeingRedeemed` (`msg-param-gift-month-being-redeemed`); `gifterName` (`msg-param-gifter-display-name` ?? `msg-param-gifter-name` ?? `msg-param-gifter-login`); `isAnonymousGift` (`msg-param-anon-gift == 'true'`); `priorGifterName` (`msg-param-prior-gifter-display-name` ?? `msg-param-prior-gifter-user-name`); `isPriorGifterAnonymous` (`msg-param-prior-gifter-anonymous == 'true'`); `giftMatchQuantity` (`msg-param-gift-sub-match-quantity` ?? `msg-param-gift-match-bonus-count`); `rewardRecipientCount` (`msg-param-selected-count`); `milestoneCategory` (`msg-param-category`); `milestoneValue` (`msg-param-value`); `milestonePoints` (`msg-param-copoReward`); `charityName` (`msg-param-charity-name`); `donationAmount` (`msg-param-donation-amount`); `donationExponent` (`msg-param-exponent`); `donationCurrency` (`msg-param-donation-currency`). Integer fields via `int.tryParse`; those marked through `_count` reject negatives (multiMonthDuration, giftMonthBeingRedeemed, giftMatchQuantity, rewardRecipientCount, milestoneValue, milestonePoints, donationAmount, donationExponent).
- Getters: `isGiftNotice` (subGift, anonSubGift, communityGift, anonCommunityGift, giftSubBaseMatch); `isMassGiftHeader` (communityGift, anonCommunityGift); `isAnonymous` (anon kinds incl. anonGiftPaidUpgrade, or userId/login is the anonymous gifter); `giftBombKey = communityGiftId ?? originId`; `hasGoal` (both goal counts present).
- `unescapeTag(value)`: IRCv3 tag unescape: `\s`→space, `\n`→LF, `\r`→CR, `\:`→`;`, `\\`→`\`, `\x`→`x` for any other char; a trailing lone `\` is kept.
- `msgIdOf(tags)`: `msg-id`, except `sharedchatnotice` → `source-msg-id`.
- `UserNotice.fromTags(tags)` never throws.

Note: the IRC parsing that produces the tags map is in lib/models/irc.dart (owned by the chat spec leaf); the wording is in user_notice_text.dart.

### lib/models/user_notice_text.dart

Wording of sub/gift/raid/etc. notices in the UI language from the typed tags (names in bold) instead of Twitch's English `system-msg`; falls back to system-msg when a kind has no sentence or a needed value is missing.
- `NoticeSegment { String text; bool bold = false }`, value equality, `toString` = `**text**` when bold.
- `userNoticeText(notice, {required channelName, l, showGiftCount = true})` dispatch: sub/resub → `_sub`; subGift → `_subGift`; communityGift → `_communityGift`; extendSub → `_extendSub`; raid → `_raid`; giftPaidUpgrade/anonGiftPaidUpgrade → `_giftPaidUpgrade`; payItForward → `_payItForward`; giftSubBaseMatch → `_sponsorBonus`; rewardGift → `_rewardGift`; viewerMilestone → `_watchStreak`; charityDonation → `_charity`; others/null → `_systemMessage`. Note: anonSubGift and anonCommunityGift kinds (legacy) go straight to system-msg.
- `readableNoticeName(displayName, login)`: no login → displayName; same case-insensitively → displayName; all runes ≤ 127 → displayName; else `'<displayName> (<login>)'`.
- `_sentences(message, bold, [more])`: the localized message contains private-use marker characters `l10nMark(i)` (lib/l10n) where names go; each marker rune is replaced by a bold segment `bold[i]`; other runes accumulate as plain text; each non-null sentence of `more` is appended as `' ' + sentence` in plain text. (PC: same technique — format with placeholder chars, then split.)
- `_giftCountSentence(senderCount)`: null/≤0 → null; 1 → `noticeGiftFirst`; n → `noticeGiftCount(count: n)`.
- `_sub`: name = displayName ?? login (readable form) else null; plan = `prime` if isPrime, `tier` if Tier 1–3, else `none`; resub → `noticeResubscribed(plan, name: mark0, tier: n or 0)`, sub → `noticeSubscribed(...)`; tail: months known & shouldShareStreak & streak > 0 → `noticeSubStreak(months, streak)`; else months > 1 → `noticeSubMonths(months)`; extra sentences `_multiMonthSentence` (multiMonthDuration ≥ 2 → `noticeSubMultiMonth(count)`) and `_redeemedGiftSentence` (wasGifted, month and total known, total ≥ 2, 1 ≤ month ≤ total; anonymous → `noticeGiftRedeemedAnonymous(month, total)`, else needs gifterName → `noticeGiftRedeemed(month, total, gifter)`).
- `_giftPaidUpgrade`: anon kind → `noticeGiftPaidUpgradeAnonymous(name)`; else gifter = senderName ?? senderLogin (null → fallback) → `noticeGiftPaidUpgrade(name, gifter)` with readable gifter.
- `_payItForward`: needs name and priorGifterName and not `isPriorGifterAnonymous`; no recipient → `noticePayForwardCommunity(name, gifter)`; else `noticePayForward(name, gifter, recipient)`.
- `_sponsorBonus`: sponsorName and giftMatchQuantity ≥ 1 → `noticeSponsorBonus(sponsor, count)`.
- `_rewardGift`: name and rewardRecipientCount ≥ 1 → `noticeRewardGift(name, count)`.
- `_watchStreak`: only `milestoneCategory == 'watch-streak'` and milestoneValue ≥ 1 → `noticeWatchStreak(name, count)` + `noticeWatchStreakPoints(points)` when points > 0.
- `_charity`: needs name, charityName, donationAmount, donationCurrency; exponent default 2, > 6 → fallback; value = amount / 10^exponent formatted with `NumberFormat.currency(locale: dateLocaleOf(l), name: currency, symbol: simpleCurrency symbol, decimalDigits: exponent)` → `noticeCharity(name, amount, charity)` (name and charity bold).
- `_subGift`: gifter = `chatAnonymousGifter` when anonymous else displayName ?? login (gifter is NOT given the login suffix); recipient readable; tier; months = giftMonths ?? 1; count sentence only if showGiftCount. months > 1 with tier → `noticeGiftedMonthsTier(gifter, months: noticeGiftMonths(count), tier: noticeGiftTier(tier), recipient)` (four bold parts); months > 1 without tier → `noticeGiftedMonths(gifter, months: noticeGiftMonthsShort(count), recipient)`; else `noticeGiftedSub(tiered: 'yes'|'no', gifter, tier, recipient)`.
- `_communityGift`: count = massGiftCount and gifter (anonymous → `chatAnonymousGifter`, else readable sender) required; senderCount (0 when hidden) → more = 1 → `noticeCommunityGiftFirst`, > 1 → `noticeCommunityGiftTotal(count)`; message `noticeCommunityGift(tiered, count, gifter: mark, tier, channel: channelName (plain text), end: 'more'|'last')`.
- `_extendSub`: benefitEndMonth 1–12 → `noticeExtendedSub(tiered, name, tier, month: DateFormat.MMMM(dateLocaleOf(l)) of that month)`.
- `_raid`: on a non-English UI with raiderDisplayName and raidViewerCount ≥ 0 → `noticeRaid(count, name: readable(raider, notice.login))`; else `_twitchRaid` = Twitch's sentence with the raider name (whole-word, case-insensitive, regex-escaped, `(?<!\w)…(?!\w)`) in bold, null when not found → system-msg fallback.
- `_systemMessage`: empty → `[]`; if the message starts (case-insensitively) with displayName or login followed by a non-word character, that prefix is bold; else one plain segment.
ARB keys used: noticeSubscribed, noticeResubscribed, noticeSubStreak, noticeSubMonths, noticeSubMultiMonth, noticeGiftRedeemed, noticeGiftRedeemedAnonymous, noticeGiftPaidUpgrade, noticeGiftPaidUpgradeAnonymous, noticePayForward, noticePayForwardCommunity, noticeSponsorBonus, noticeRewardGift, noticeWatchStreak, noticeWatchStreakPoints, noticeCharity, noticeGiftFirst, noticeGiftCount, noticeGiftedMonthsTier, noticeGiftMonths, noticeGiftTier, noticeGiftedMonths, noticeGiftMonthsShort, noticeGiftedSub, noticeCommunityGift, noticeCommunityGiftFirst, noticeCommunityGiftTotal, noticeExtendedSub, noticeRaid, chatAnonymousGifter (values in the Strings section).

### lib/models/irc.dart

The Twitch IRC message model AND its chat-line renderer (Flutter `InlineSpan` builder + emote/badge detail sheets). The IRC transport is in the chat layer (wss://irc-ws.chat.twitch.tv); this file parses lines and draws them. The PC port must split it: a pure `irc::Message` parser (this section's parsing rules, exact) and a renderer in the UI crate (same visual rules).

**Constants**: action prefix `\x01` (+ `ACTION ` → prefix length 8), suffix `\x01`; invalid char `U+E0000` (stripped — some clients add it to bypass duplicate-message limits; shows as a box on iOS); Twitch emote base `https://static-cdn.jtvnw.net/emoticons/v2`; server name `tmi.twitch.tv`; animated suffix `/default/dark/3.0`, static `/static/dark/3.0`; emoji size offset 5.0 (emoji font = emoteSize − 5); `kDeletedUserLogin = 'deleted_user'`, `kDeletedUserDisplayName = 'Deleted user'` (data value; shown localized via `chatDeletedUser`; no real display name contains a space); `gigantifiedEmoteFactor = 3.0`.

**`IRCMessage` fields**: `raw`; mutable `command` (`Command`); `tags` (Map<String,String>, mutable — ban-duration is written into it); `user` (login); `localEmotes` (Map word→Emote from the `emotes` tag); mutable `message`, `split` (words), `action`, `mention`; `actionCallback`/`actionLabel` (ONSET notices with a button); `raidArrival` (ONSET raid welcome card); `gifParts`; `textAsSent` (text after `/me` prefix, before normalisation; null for ONSET-made lines); lazy `autoModFlags = parseAutoModFlags(tags['flags'])` (chat_filters.dart).

**`IRCMessage.fromString(whole, {userLogin})`** (exact algorithm):
1. tags = substring between index 1 and the first space; a COPY with every `\s` replaced by space is split on `;`; each tag split at the FIRST `=`; tags with no `=` or empty value (`key=`) are SKIPPED (not stored). Other escapes are NOT unescaped here (UserNotice does its own).
2. rest = substring after the first space + 2 chars (skips ` :`); split on spaces: [0] prefix, [1] command, [2] channel, [3…] message.
3. user = `tags['login']` when prefix == `tmi.twitch.tv`, else `prefix.substring(0, prefix.indexOf('!'))` — a prefix without `!` (e.g. a bare server name other than `tmi.twitch.tv`) makes `indexOf` return −1 and throws a `RangeError`: the whole line is rejected (irc.dart line ~1811). Note also that step 1 assumes the line starts with `@tags`: an untagged line (no leading `@`) is mis-parsed — its first word minus one char is taken as the tag string and the remainder offset by 2 chars is read as prefix/command.
4. message = words[3…] joined by spaces, leading `:` removed (null when fewer than 4 parts).
5. `/me`: message longer than 8 and starting/ending with `\x01` → action = true, message = substring(8, len−1).
6. Twitch emotes from `emotes` tag (`id:start-end,start-end/id2:…`): for each id only the FIRST range is used; word = `message.substring(start, end+1)` (UTF-16 indices as in Dart; Twitch counts code points — ONSET behaviour kept as-is); `localEmotes[word] = Emote(name: word, zeroWidth: false, url: <base>/<id>/default/dark/3.0, staticUrl: <base>/<id>/static/dark/3.0, type: twitchSub)`.
7. `mention = mentionsUser(message, userLogin)` when a login is given.
8. `gifParts = GifMessagePart.split(message, ChatGif.parseTag(UserNotice.unescapeTag(rawTagValue('gifs'))))` using the RAW (escaped) tag value from the original tag string, because the global `\s` replacement would corrupt `\\s`.
9. `message = _normalizeText(message)` (split on spaces, remove U+E0000 from each word, trim, drop empty words, re-join with single spaces); `split = _splitWords(message)` (iterate grapheme clusters; runs of emoji characters (regexEmoji) form their own word even without spaces. When an emoji run starts, the pending text buffer is split on `' '` WITHOUT dropping empties (irc.dart line ~1730), so `a 😀 b` yields `["a", "", "😀", "b"]` — the empty word before an emoji run is kept and affects word indices used by filters/replies and an extra space in rendering; empties are dropped only for the final text buffer).
10. command: `PRIVMSG`→privateMessage, `CLEARCHAT`→clearChat, `CLEARMSG`→clearMessage, `NOTICE`→notice, `USERNOTICE`→userNotice, `ROOMSTATE`→roomState, `USERSTATE`→userState, `GLOBALUSERSTATE`→globalUserState, else none (debug log).

**Factories**: `IRCMessage.raidWelcome(arrival)` → notice with `tmi-sent-ts` = now ms and message `chatRaidWelcomeFrom(name: arrival.fromName)`, `raidArrival` set. `IRCMessage.createNotice({message, actionCallback, actionLabel})` → notice stamped now.

**Getters/derived**: `isFromDeletedUser` (user == `deleted_user` && display-name == `Deleted user` && no user-id); `shownDisplayName(l)`; `isUserNotice` = command userNotice OR tags contain `system-msg` OR raw command word is `USERNOTICE` (survives deletion/ban changing `command`; `_rawCommand` skips the `@tags ` part and an optional `:prefix`); lazy `notice = UserNotice.fromTags(tags)` when a user notice; `noticeText({channelName, l, showGiftCount})` cached per (channelName, locale, showGiftCount); `isGigantified` = `msg-id == 'gigantified-emote-message'` (first emote drawn ×3).

**Moderation application (static)**:
- `clearChat({messages, bufferedMessages, ircMessage})`: no target (message null) → whole chat cleared: both lists emptied and a notice `chatClearedByModerator` added; returns false. Else every line (in both lists) whose `user` equals the target login gets `command = clearChat` and, if `ban-duration` present, that tag copied (timeout seconds); returns true when a visible line changed state (command or duration differ).
- `clearMessage(...)`: first line whose `tags['id'] == target-msg-id` → `command = clearMessage` in each list; returns whether it changed. (Deleted lines stay; the deleted-message reveal feature shows `chatMessageDeletedInline`, tappable to reveal.)

**Filtering (AutoMod-style chat filters)**: `_filteredWords(settings)` — only when textAsSent exists, flags non-empty and settings enabled; cached per settings object; `maskFilteredText(text, flags, settings)` returns the text with flagged ranges replaced by a mark character; both `split` and each text gif part are re-tokenised and aligned: a masked word becomes `*`s and its index is hidden unless it is one of the message's Twitch emotes (kept, as in the official app). `isFiltered(settings)`; `shownText(filters)` = message without hidden words (used for link previews).

**Rendering `generateSpan(context, {style, assetsStore, badgeScale, emoteScale, launchExternal, onTapName, onTapPingedUser, onTapDeletedMessage, showMessage = true, channelIdToUserTwitch, timestamp = disabled, currentChannelId, animateEmotes = true, showGifs = true, animateGifs = true, chatFilters, onTapFiltered, nameContrast = readableNameColorContrast})`** — order of spans:
1. Timestamp (if enabled): from `tmi-sent-ts` (ms; now if absent); 24 h → `DateFormat.Hm()`, 12 h → `'h:mm'` (no am/pm); cached per (locale, 24h); text + space, monospace font (`OnsetTokens.monoFamily`) with tabular figures in the metadata colour.
2. Historical badge: `historical == '1'` → history icon (size = badge size) with tap tooltip `chatHistoricalMessage`, then a space.
