3. Shared-chat source avatar: channel = `source-room-id ?? room-id` looked up in `channelIdToUserTwitch`; circular avatar (badge size); the current channel gets a 1.5 px ring in the `live` colour; tooltip `chatSentFromCurrent(name)` or `chatSentFrom(name)`.
4. Badges (`_addUserBadges`): Twitch badges from `badges` tag (`set/version` comma list) mapped through `assetsStore.twitchBadgesToObject`. Moderator badge: if the user has an FFZ `Bot` badge → that image and name `chatBadgeModeratorBot(moderator)` and the separate Bot badge is skipped; else if the FFZ room defines `mod_urls` → custom mod image (4x ?? 2x ?? 1x); drawn on the `subOnly` colour background. VIP badge: FFZ room `vip_badge` image when defined. FFZ badges (user badges merged with room badges, de-duplicated by URL) on their badge colour background; a `Bot` badge is inserted at the START of the line (index 2 when historical, else 0). BTTV badge (SVG). 7TV badges. Each badge is tappable → asset sheet with `BadgeDetails` (type label + explainer `badgeExplainerText` using `_badgeOrigin` — elsewhere when `source-room-id` differs from the current channel).
5. Name: colour from `color` tag (default `#868686`), adjusted for contrast (`adjustChatNameColor` to `nameContrast`); shown name = localized deleted-user name, or `getReadableName(displayName, login)` (adds login when the display name is non-Latin); 7TV paint (`assetsStore.paintFor(userId)`) drawn by `PaintedName` only when every paint colour already passes the contrast target (`_readablePaint`), else the plain colour; bold; tap → user card. A `:` follows unless action.
6. Content: italic when action. Deleted (`showMessage == false`) → `' ' + chatMessageDeletedInline` in primary colour, tap → reveal. Replies (`reply-parent-display-name` present) skip the first word (the @parent shown in the reply header). Without GIFs → words; with GIF parts → text parts as words, GIF parts as a new line + `ChatGifImage` (tap → asset sheet with "GIF" subtitle) when `showGifs` and `isLoadable`, else the title as plain text.
   Word rules (processed right-to-left so zero-width stacks attach to the previous emote): hidden filtered word → the masked text with semantics `chatFilteredWord`, tap → `onTapFiltered`; cheer (only when the message has a `bits` tag and the channel has cheermotes) → tier image (dark/light, animated per setting) + amount in tier colour, bold, semantics `chatCheerSemantics(prefix, count)`; emote lookup order channel/global map → sender's 7TV personal emotes → tag `localEmotes`; zero-width emote (not first word) → stack: consecutive zero-width emotes over the next non-zero-width emote (or an emoji word as base); FFZ modifier flags of the stack applied to the base (`growX` doubles the width with fill; `hidden` emotes not drawn; other modifiers via `FfzModifiedEmote`); tap → sheet titled with the base emote (or emoji) and subtitle `chatEmoteStackWith(emotes)`; normal emote → image sized `emote.height × emoteScale` (or default 28 × scale) and width likewise, ×3 for the first emote of a gigantified message, tap → emote sheet; emoji word → text at emoteSize − 5; `@word` → bold, tap → `onTapPingedUser(word without @, before first comma)`; link (`regexLink`) → underlined primary colour, tap → `LinkRouter.open(text, launchExternal, confirm: true)`; else plain text. Image decode height = size × devicePixelRatio (ceil).
**Emote sheet `showEmoteDetailsBottomSheet(context, {emote, launchExternal, onRemoveRecent, onSubscribe})`**: optional rows "Subscribe" (`channelSubscribe`, detail `chatDetailsOpensTwitch`, external), "Report emote" for Twitch emotes with an owner login (`chatReportEmote`, opens the ONSET report sheet with `ReportTarget.emote` or the twitch.tv fallback), "Remove from recent" (`emoteMenuRemoveRecent`); title `name (realName)`; subtitle lines: type label, `chatEmoteBy(name)`, `emoteLimitedTime`, unlock hint, `emoteModifierLine(code)`, and for bits emotes `EmoteBitsThresholdLine` which asks `TwitchCommunityService.getEmoteBitsThreshold` and shows `emoteBitsThreshold(count)`.
**Asset sheet**: 64 dp rounded (16) tile with the image (≤48) — tap opens a full-size photo dialog; title + subtitle; hairline; actions; "Copy name" (`chatCopyName`, copies the name before ` (`), "Copy image URL" (`chatCopyImageUrl`), "Open in browser" (`chatOpenInBrowser`).

**`ROOMSTATE`** (defaults `emoteOnly '0'`, `followersOnly '-1'` (off; `0` = any follower, N = minutes), `r9k '0'`, `slowMode '0'` seconds, `subMode '0'`); `fromIRCMessage` copies `emote-only`, `followers-only`, `r9k`, `slow`, `subs-only` when present (partial ROOMSTATE updates keep previous values).
**`USERSTATE`** `{raw, color '', displayName '', mod false, subscriber false, vip false}`; `fromIRCMessage`: color/display-name when present; `mod`/`subscriber` true unless the tag is `'0'` (absent → true!); `vip` = some badge starts with `vip/` (empty badges → false).
**`mentionsUser(message, login)`**: empty login → false; regex `(?:^|[^a-z0-9_])@?<escaped lower login>(?![a-z0-9_])` on the lower-cased message (whole word, optional @).
**`enum Command { privateMessage, clearChat, clearMessage, notice, userNotice, roomState, userState, globalUserState, none }`**.
**`regexEmoji`** (from dart_emoji): one or more of U+1F300–1F5FF, 1F900–1F9FF, 1F600–1F64F, 1F680–1F6FF, 2600–26FF, 2700–27BF, 1F1E6–1F1FF, 1F191–1F251, 1F004, 1F0CF, 1F170–1F171, 1F17E–1F17F, 1F18E, 3030, 2B50, 2B55, 2934–2935, 2B05–2B07, 2B1B–2B1C, 3297, 3299, 303D, 00A9, 00AE, 2122, 23F3, 24C2, 23E9–23EF, 25B6, 23F8–23FA, 200D.
**`BadgeDetails`** widget (type label + explainer); **`EmoteBitsThresholdLine`** stateful widget (described above).

### lib/models/onset_release.dart

An ONSET build published as a GitHub release by the update workflow. Tag `build-<N>` where N is the Android build number (versionCode) of the APK; two assets: the arm64 APK and its SHA-256.
- Constants: `apkAssetName = 'onset-arm64.apk'`, `shaAssetName = 'onset-arm64.apk.sha256'`, tag pattern `^build-([1-9]\d{0,8})$` (1–9 digits, no leading zero, always fits a versionCode).
- Fields: `int build`, `String tag`, `String notes` (release body, Markdown), `int apkAssetId`, `int shaAssetId`, `int apkSize` (bytes).
- `tryParse(json)` (GitHub `GET /repos/{repo}/releases/latest` body): Map with String `tag_name` matching the pattern; `assets` List; find assets by exact `name` for both files (either missing → null: an update that cannot be verified is never offered); both `id` must be ints > 0; APK `size` int ≥ 0; `body` String or `''`; `build = int.parse(group 1)`.
PC: the same tag scheme, with desktop asset names per OS (e.g. `onset-windows-x64.zip` + `.sha256`) decided by the PC release workflow; the parser must still refuse releases lacking the hash asset.
